# backend/add_gender_column.py
from app.database import engine
from sqlalchemy import text
with engine.connect() as conn:
    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE teams ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS gender VARCHAR DEFAULT 'male'"))
    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE teams ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS team_type VARCHAR DEFAULT 'club'"))
    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE teams ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS current_league_level VARCHAR"))
    conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE teams ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS league_tier INTEGER DEFAULT 3"))
    conn.commit()
print("Columns added.")

db = SessionLocal()

# Check if there are any standings
count = db.query(LeagueStanding).count()
print(f"Total standings records: {count}")

# Check leagues
leagues = db.query(League).all()
print(f"Leagues in database: {[l.name for l in leagues]}")

if count == 0:
    print("\n⚠️ No standings data found! You need to add league standings.")
    print("You can insert sample standings via admin panel or SQL.")

    # Show sample
    sample = db.query(LeagueStanding).first()

====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\convert_to_flask.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Enhanced FastAPI → Flask Converter with Model/Schema Splitting
--------------------------------------------------------------
This script:
- Converts FastAPI project (app/) to Flask (flask_backend/)
- Splits models.py into individual files under app/models/
- Splits schemas.py into individual files under app/schemas/
- Transforms all routes, removes async, adapts dependencies, etc.

Run it from the project root (where the 'app' folder is).
"""

import os
import re
import shutil
import ast
import inspect
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Set

# =============================================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# =============================================================================
SOURCE_DIR = Path("app")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("flask_backend")
PYTHON_EXT = ".py"
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {"__pycache__", ".git"}

# =============================================================================
# UTILITY FUNCTIONS FOR SPLITTING MODELS AND SCHEMAS
# =============================================================================

def parse_classes_from_file(filepath: Path, class_type: str = "model") -> Dict[str, str]:
    """
    Extract class definitions from a Python file.
    class_type: 'model' or 'schema'
    Returns dict {class_name: class_source_code}
    """
    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        content = f.read()

    # Parse the file into an AST
    tree = ast.parse(content)

    classes = {}
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
            # Get the source code of the class
            # We need to locate the class block in the original text
            # Use ast.get_source_segment (Python 3.8+)
            try:
                source = ast.get_source_segment(content, node)
                if source:
                    classes[node.name] = source
            except Exception:
                # fallback: manually get lines
                lines = content.splitlines()
                start_line = node.lineno - 1
                end_line = node.end_lineno if hasattr(node, 'end_lineno') else len(lines)
                class_source = "\n".join(lines[start_line:end_line])
                classes[node.name] = class_source
    return classes


def split_models(input_file: Path, output_dir: Path) -> List[str]:
    """
    Split models.py into separate files per model class.
    Also extract enums and base classes (Base, etc.) into a shared file.
    Returns list of created model names.
    """
    models_dir = output_dir / "models"
    models_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # First, read the whole models.py content to capture imports and other top-level stuff
    with open(input_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        full_content = f.read()

    # Extract imports and other top-level lines (before first class)
    # We'll split at first class definition
    lines = full_content.splitlines()
    top_lines = []
    class_start_index = 0
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if re.match(r'^class\s+', line):
            class_start_index = i
            break
        top_lines.append(line)
    # Also include lines after class definitions? Usually not needed.
    top_code = "\n".join(top_lines)

    # Parse classes
    classes = parse_classes_from_file(input_file, "model")

    # Also find association tables (e.g., news_tags = Table(...))
    # We'll extract them and put in a separate file "associations.py"
    association_table_pattern = re.compile(r'^\s*(\w+)\s*=\s*Table\s*\(', re.MULTILINE)
    associations = {}
    for match in association_table_pattern.finditer(full_content):
        table_name = match.group(1)
        # Get the whole assignment statement until the next line that is not part of it
        start = match.start()
        # Find the end of the Table(...) by counting parentheses
        paren_count = 0
        end = start
        for i in range(start, len(full_content)):
            if full_content[i] == '(':
                paren_count += 1
            elif full_content[i] == ')':
                paren_count -= 1
                if paren_count == 0:
                    end = i + 1
                    break
        assignment = full_content[start:end]
        associations[table_name] = assignment

    # Write top_code to a base file (imports, enums, Base)
    base_file = models_dir / "_base.py"
    with open(base_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        # Write imports, enums, Base, and any top-level definitions
        # We also need to add all imports needed for models
        f.write(top_code)
        # Add any missing imports? We'll assume they are already there.
        # Also ensure we have from . import relationship etc.

    # Write associations.py
    if associations:
        assoc_file = models_dir / "associations.py"
        with open(assoc_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write("# Association tables\n")
            for name, code in associations.items():
                f.write(code + "\n\n")

    # Write each model class to its own file
    model_names = []
    for class_name, class_code in classes.items():
        # Skip if it's a base class or enum? We'll filter.
        if class_name in ("Base", "UserRole", "MatchEventType", "Gender", "LeagueLevel", "MatchType", "TournamentCategory"):
            # These are likely defined in top_code, so we skip
            continue
        # Also skip if it's an association table class (if any) – we already extracted
        # For models, we want all SQLAlchemy model classes
        filename = f"{class_name.lower()}.py"
        filepath = models_dir / filename
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            # Write the class code
            f.write(class_code)
        model_names.append(class_name)

    # Now we need to fix imports in each model file: they should import Base from ._base, and other models as needed.
    # We'll add a fix later, but we can generate a __init__.py that imports all models.
    init_content = "# Auto-generated __init__.py\n"
    for name in model_names:
        init_content += f"from .{name.lower()} import {name}\n"
    # Also import Base, enums, associations
    init_content += "from ._base import *\n"
    if associations:
        init_content += "from .associations import *\n"

    with open(models_dir / "__init__.py", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(init_content)

    # Also generate a __all__ list for convenience
    all_models = model_names + ["Base"] + list(associations.keys())
    all_list = f"__all__ = {all_models}"
    with open(models_dir / "__init__.py", 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write("\n" + all_list)

    return model_names


def split_schemas(input_file: Path, output_dir: Path) -> List[str]:
    """
    Split schemas.py into separate files per schema class.
    """
    schemas_dir = output_dir / "schemas"
    schemas_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    with open(input_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        full_content = f.read()

    # Extract top-level imports and enums
    lines = full_content.splitlines()
    top_lines = []
    class_start_index = 0
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if re.match(r'^class\s+', line):
            class_start_index = i
            break
        top_lines.append(line)
    top_code = "\n".join(top_lines)

    # Parse classes
    classes = parse_classes_from_file(input_file, "schema")

    # Write top_code to _base.py in schemas
    base_file = schemas_dir / "_base.py"
    with open(base_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(top_code)

    # Write each schema class
    schema_names = []
    for class_name, class_code in classes.items():
        # Skip enums (they are in top_code)
        if class_name in ("UserRoleEnum", "MatchEventType", "Gender", "LeagueLevel", "MatchType", "TournamentCategory"):
            continue
        filename = f"{class_name.lower()}.py"
        filepath = schemas_dir / filename
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(class_code)
        schema_names.append(class_name)

    # __init__.py
    init_content = "# Auto-generated __init__.py\n"
    for name in schema_names:
        init_content += f"from .{name.lower()} import {name}\n"
    init_content += "from ._base import *\n"
    with open(schemas_dir / "__init__.py", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(init_content)

    all_schemas = schema_names + ["UserRoleEnum", "MatchEventType", "Gender", "LeagueLevel", "MatchType", "TournamentCategory"]
    all_list = f"__all__ = {all_schemas}"
    with open(schemas_dir / "__init__.py", 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write("\n" + all_list)

    return schema_names


# =============================================================================
# FILE TRANSFORMATION FUNCTIONS (same as before, with adjustments for imports)
# =============================================================================

def transform_imports(content: str) -> str:
    """Replace FastAPI imports with Flask equivalents and update model/schema imports."""
    # General FastAPI -> Flask
    replacements = [
        (r"from fastapi import FastAPI, APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status, Query, Body, Path, Header",
         "from flask import Flask, Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort, make_response"),
        (r"from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer, OAuth2PasswordRequestForm",
         "from flask_jwt_extended import JWTManager, create_access_token, get_jwt_identity, jwt_required, verify_jwt_in_request"),
        (r"from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware",
         "from flask_cors import CORS"),
        (r"from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles",
         ""),
        (r"from fastapi.responses import Response",
         "from flask import Response"),
        (r"from pydantic import BaseModel",
         "from pydantic import BaseModel"),
    ]
    for pattern, repl in replacements:
        content = re.sub(pattern, repl, content)

    # Update model imports: from .. import models -> from ..models import (class)
    # We'll leave as is; the __init__.py will allow importing.
    return content


def transform_decorators(content: str) -> str:
    """Convert route decorators."""
    methods_map = {
        "get": "GET",
        "post": "POST",
        "put": "PUT",
        "delete": "DELETE",
        "patch": "PATCH",
        "options": "OPTIONS",
        "head": "HEAD",
    }
    for fastapi_method, http_method in methods_map.items():
        pattern = rf'@(\w+)\.{fastapi_method}\(([^)]*)\)'
        repl = rf'@\1.route(\2, methods=["{http_method}"])'
        content = re.sub(pattern, repl, content)
    return content


def transform_path_params(content: str) -> str:
    """Replace {param} with <param> in route strings."""
    def replace_in_route_line(match):
        line = match.group(0)
        line = re.sub(r'\{([^}]+)\}', r'<\1>', line)
        return line
    content = re.sub(r'^.*@\w+\.route\(.*$', replace_in_route_line, content, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    return content


def transform_dependencies(content: str) -> str:
    """Remove Depends, keep parameters."""
    content = re.sub(r',?\s*\bDepends\([^)]*\)', '', content)
    content = re.sub(r'\s*=\s*Depends\([^)]*\)', '', content)
    return content


def transform_async(content: str) -> str:
    """Remove async/await."""
    content = re.sub(r'^(\s*)async\s+def\s+', r'\1def ', content, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    content = re.sub(r'\bawait\s+', '', content)
    return content


def transform_exceptions(content: str) -> str:
    """Convert HTTPException to abort."""
    content = re.sub(r'raise HTTPException\(status_code=(\d+),\s*detail=([^)]*)\)', r'abort(\1, \2)', content)
    return content


def transform_response_model(content: str) -> str:
    """Remove response_model from decorators."""
    content = re.sub(r',?\s*response_model\s*=\s*[^,)]+', '', content)
    return content


def transform_router_to_blueprint(content: str, filename: Path) -> str:
    """Convert APIRouter to Blueprint."""
    if re.search(r'router\s*=\s*APIRouter\(', content):
        bp_name = filename.stem
        content = re.sub(
            r'router\s*=\s*APIRouter\([^)]*\)',
            f"bp = Blueprint('{bp_name}', __name__)",
            content
        )
        content = re.sub(r'@router\.', '@bp.', content)
    content = re.sub(r'from fastapi import.*APIRouter', 'from flask import Blueprint', content)
    return content


def transform_main_py(content: str) -> str:
    """Special handling for main.py."""
    content = re.sub(r'app\s*=\s*FastAPI\([^)]*\)', "app = Flask(__name__)", content)

    # Add CORS and JWT setup after app creation
    cors_setup = """
# CORS
CORS(app)

# JWT
app.config['JWT_SECRET_KEY'] = os.getenv('SECRET_KEY', 'your-secret-key')
jwt = JWTManager(app)
"""
    content = re.sub(r'(app\s*=\s*Flask\(__name__\))', r'\1\n' + cors_setup, content)

    # Replace include_router with register_blueprint
    def replace_include(match):
        line = match.group(0)
        parts = re.search(r'app\.include_router\((\w+)\.router', line)
        if parts:
            module = parts.group(1)
            prefix_match = re.search(r'prefix\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"', line)
            prefix = prefix_match.group(1) if prefix_match else ""
            new_line = f"app.register_blueprint({module}.bp, url_prefix=\"{prefix}\")"
            return new_line
        return line

    content = re.sub(r'app\.include_router\([^)]+\)', replace_include, content)
    return content


def process_file(input_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> None:
    """Transform a single Python file."""
    with open(input_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        content = f.read()

    content = transform_imports(content)
    content = transform_router_to_blueprint(content, input_path)
    content = transform_decorators(content)
    content = transform_path_params(content)
    content = transform_dependencies(content)
    content = transform_async(content)
    content = transform_exceptions(content)
    content = transform_response_model(content)

    # Special for main.py
    if input_path.name == "main.py":
        content = transform_main_py(content)

    # Also update model imports: from .. import models -> from ..models import ModelName
    # We'll do a simple replacement: replace "from .. import models" with "from ..models import *"
    # But better: we'll handle in each file. We'll leave as is; the __init__.py will allow importing.
    # However, we can replace "models." with "models." (no change) but ensure import.
    # We'll replace "from .. import models" with "from ..models import *"
    content = re.sub(r'from \.\. import models', 'from ..models import *', content)
    content = re.sub(r'from \.\. import schemas', 'from ..schemas import *', content)
    # Also handle direct references: models.User -> User (if imported)
    # Not doing that now.

    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(content)


def copy_non_python_files(src_dir: Path, dst_dir: Path) -> None:
    """Copy non-Python files."""
    for item in src_dir.rglob('*'):
        if item.is_file() and item.suffix != PYTHON_EXT:
            rel_path = item.relative_to(src_dir)
            dst_file = dst_dir / rel_path
            dst_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(item, dst_file)
            print(f"Copied: {rel_path}")


# =============================================================================
# MAIN
# =============================================================================

def main():
    print("=" * 70)
    print("ENHANCED FASTAPI → FLASK CONVERTER WITH SPLIT MODELS/SCHEMAS")
    print("=" * 70)

    if not SOURCE_DIR.exists():
        print(f"ERROR: Source directory '{SOURCE_DIR}' not found.")
        return

    # Clean output
    if OUTPUT_DIR.exists():
        print(f"Removing existing output directory: {OUTPUT_DIR}")
        shutil.rmtree(OUTPUT_DIR)
    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True)

    # Step 1: Split models and schemas
    models_path = SOURCE_DIR / "models.py"
    schemas_path = SOURCE_DIR / "schemas.py"
    if not models_path.exists():
        print("ERROR: models.py not found in source.")
        return
    if not schemas_path.exists():
        print("ERROR: schemas.py not found in source.")
        return

    print("\n[1] Splitting models.py into individual files...")
    model_names = split_models(models_path, OUTPUT_DIR)
    print(f"    Created {len(model_names)} model files.")

    print("\n[2] Splitting schemas.py into individual files...")
    schema_names = split_schemas(schemas_path, OUTPUT_DIR)
    print(f"    Created {len(schema_names)} schema files.")

    # Step 2: Convert all other Python files (routes, main, auth, database, etc.)
    print("\n[3] Converting route files and other modules...")
    for py_file in SOURCE_DIR.rglob(f"*{PYTHON_EXT}"):
        # Skip models.py and schemas.py since we already processed them (and we don't want them in the output)
        if py_file.name in ("models.py", "schemas.py"):
            continue
        # Also skip if in excluded dirs
        if any(part in EXCLUDED_DIRS for part in py_file.parts):
            continue
        rel_path = py_file.relative_to(SOURCE_DIR)
        output_file = OUTPUT_DIR / rel_path
        process_file(py_file, output_file)

    # Copy non-Python files (assets, etc.)
    print("\n[4] Copying static assets and other non-Python files...")
    copy_non_python_files(SOURCE_DIR, OUTPUT_DIR)

    # Step 5: Create a new requirements.txt
    req_file = OUTPUT_DIR / "requirements.txt"
    with open(req_file, 'w') as f:
        f.write("""# Flask requirements
Flask==2.3.3
Flask-CORS==4.0.1
Flask-JWT-Extended==4.5.3
Flask-SQLAlchemy==3.1.1
psycopg2-binary==2.9.9
python-dotenv==1.0.0
pydantic==2.5.0
email-validator==2.1.0
python-jose[cryptography]==3.3.0
passlib[bcrypt]==1.7.4
python-multipart==0.0.6
""")
    print(f"    Created {req_file}")

    # Create a README
    readme = OUTPUT_DIR / "README.md"
    with open(readme, 'w') as f:
        f.write("# Flask Backend Converted from FastAPI\n")
        f.write("This backend was automatically converted from a FastAPI project to Flask.\n")
        f.write("Please review the code and adjust as necessary.\n")


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\init_db.py
====================================================================================================

from app.database import SessionLocal, engine
from app import models
from app.auth import get_password_hash

print("Creating database tables...")
models.Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)

db = SessionLocal()
try:
    # Check if admin already exists
    existing_admin = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.username == "admin").first()
    if existing_admin:
        print("Admin user already exists. Skipping creation.")
    else:
        # Create admin user with role ADMIN
        hashed_pw = get_password_hash("admin123")
        admin = models.User(
            username="admin",
            email="admin@faz.zm",
            full_name="System Administrator",
            hashed_password=hashed_pw,
            role=models.UserRole.ADMIN,   # Use role instead of is_admin
            is_active=True
        )
        db.add(admin)
        db.commit()
        print("Admin user created successfully! (username: admin, password: admin123)")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"Error: {e}")
    db.rollback()
finally:
    db.close()


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\insert_league_data.py
====================================================================================================

from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import (
    Team, League, LeagueStanding, PromotionRelegationRule, Gender, LeagueLevel,
    CupCompetition, CupSeason, CupMatch, ContinentalQualification, NationalTeam
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

db = SessionLocal()

# ============ HELPER FUNCTION ============
def update_or_create_team(name, gender, league_level, tier):
    """Update existing team or create new one with proper fields"""
    existing_team = db.query(Team).filter(Team.name == name).first()
    short = name[:3].upper()
    if existing_team:
        # Update existing team (in case fields are missing)
        existing_team.short_name = short
        existing_team.gender = gender
        existing_team.team_type = "club"
        existing_team.current_league_level = league_level
        existing_team.league_tier = tier
        if not existing_team.founded:
            existing_team.founded = 1900
        if not existing_team.stadium:
            existing_team.stadium = "TBA"
        if not existing_team.coach:
            existing_team.coach = "TBA"
        print(f"  Updated: {name}")
    else:
        # Create new team
        db.add(Team(
            name=name,
            short_name=short,
            gender=gender,
            team_type="club",
            current_league_level=league_level,
            league_tier=tier,
            founded=1900,
            stadium="TBA",
            coach="TBA"
        ))
        print(f"  Added: {name}")
    db.flush()

# ============ TEAMS ============
# Men's Super League Teams
men_super_league_teams = [
    "Power Dynamos FC", "ZESCO United FC", "Nkana FC", "Red Arrows FC",

# ============ LEAGUES ============

print("Creating Leagues...")
print("=" * 50)

leagues_data = [
    {"name": "MTN Super League", "gender": Gender.MALE, "level": LeagueLevel.SUPER_LEAGUE, "tier": 1, "season": "2025-2026"},
    {"name": "National Division 1", "gender": Gender.MALE, "level": LeagueLevel.DIVISION_ONE, "tier": 2, "season": "2025-2026"},
    {"name": "Women's Super League", "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "level": LeagueLevel.SUPER_LEAGUE, "tier": 1, "season": "2025-2026"},
    {"name": "National Women's League - Southern", "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "level": LeagueLevel.NATIONAL_LEAGUE_SOUTH, "tier": 2, "season": "2025-2026"},
    {"name": "National Women's League - Northern", "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "level": LeagueLevel.NATIONAL_LEAGUE_NORTH, "tier": 2, "season": "2025-2026"},
]

for league_data in leagues_data:
    exists = db.query(League).filter(League.name == league_data["name"]).first()
    if not exists:
        db.add(League(**league_data))
        print(f"  Created: {league_data['name']}")
    else:
        print(f"  Already exists: {league_data['name']}")

db.commit()
print("\n✅ Leagues processed successfully!")

# ============ PROMOTION/RELEGATION RULES ============
print("\n" + "=" * 50)
print("Setting up Promotion/Relegation Rules...")
print("=" * 50)

men_super = db.query(League).filter(League.name == "MTN Super League").first()
men_div1 = db.query(League).filter(League.name == "National Division 1").first()
women_super = db.query(League).filter(League.name == "Women's Super League").first()
women_south = db.query(League).filter(League.name == "National Women's League - Southern").first()
women_north = db.query(League).filter(League.name == "National Women's League - Northern").first()

if men_super and men_div1:
    # Check if rules already exist
    existing1 = db.query(PromotionRelegationRule).filter(
        PromotionRelegationRule.from_league_id == men_div1.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.to_league_id == men_super.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.season == "2025-2026"
    ).first()
    if not existing1:
        db.add(PromotionRelegationRule(from_league_id=men_div1.id, to_league_id=men_super.id, positions_promoted=4, positions_relegated=0, season="2025-2026"))
        print("  Added: Men's promotion rule (Div1 → Super)")
    
    existing2 = db.query(PromotionRelegationRule).filter(
        PromotionRelegationRule.from_league_id == men_super.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.to_league_id == men_div1.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.season == "2025-2026"
    ).first()
    if not existing2:
        db.add(PromotionRelegationRule(from_league_id=men_super.id, to_league_id=men_div1.id, positions_promoted=0, positions_relegated=4, season="2025-2026"))
        print("  Added: Men's relegation rule (Super → Div1)")

if women_super and women_south and women_north:
    # Promotion from South to Super
    existing3 = db.query(PromotionRelegationRule).filter(
        PromotionRelegationRule.from_league_id == women_south.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.to_league_id == women_super.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.season == "2025-2026"
    ).first()
    if not existing3:
        db.add(PromotionRelegationRule(from_league_id=women_south.id, to_league_id=women_super.id, positions_promoted=2, positions_relegated=0, season="2025-2026"))
        print("  Added: Women's promotion rule (South → Super)")
    
    # Promotion from North to Super
    existing4 = db.query(PromotionRelegationRule).filter(
        PromotionRelegationRule.from_league_id == women_north.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.to_league_id == women_super.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.season == "2025-2026"
    ).first()
    if not existing4:
        db.add(PromotionRelegationRule(from_league_id=women_north.id, to_league_id=women_super.id, positions_promoted=2, positions_relegated=0, season="2025-2026"))
        print("  Added: Women's promotion rule (North → Super)")
    
    # Relegation from Super to South
    existing5 = db.query(PromotionRelegationRule).filter(
        PromotionRelegationRule.from_league_id == women_super.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.to_league_id == women_south.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.season == "2025-2026"
    ).first()
    if not existing5:
        db.add(PromotionRelegationRule(from_league_id=women_super.id, to_league_id=women_south.id, positions_promoted=0, positions_relegated=2, season="2025-2026"))
        print("  Added: Women's relegation rule (Super → South)")
    
    # Relegation from Super to North
    existing6 = db.query(PromotionRelegationRule).filter(
        PromotionRelegationRule.from_league_id == women_super.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.to_league_id == women_north.id,
        PromotionRelegationRule.season == "2025-2026"
    ).first()
    if not existing6:
        db.add(PromotionRelegationRule(from_league_id=women_super.id, to_league_id=women_north.id, positions_promoted=0, positions_relegated=2, season="2025-2026"))
        print("  Added: Women's relegation rule (Super → North)")

db.commit()
print("\n✅ Promotion/Relegation rules processed successfully!")

    {"name": "ABSA Cup", "gender": Gender.MALE, "competition_type": "cup", "season": "2025-2026"},
    {"name": "Charity Shield", "gender": Gender.MALE, "competition_type": "super_cup", "season": "2025-2026"},

# ============ SAMPLE CUP MATCHES (optional) ============
print("\n" + "=" * 50)
print("Adding Sample Cup Seasons and Matches...")
print("=" * 50)

for cup in cup_competitions:
    cup_obj = db.query(CupCompetition).filter(
        CupCompetition.name == cup["name"], 
        CupCompetition.season == cup["season"]
    ).first()
    if cup_obj:
        # Check if season already exists
        season_exists = db.query(CupSeason).filter(
            CupSeason.cup_competition_id == cup_obj.id, 
            CupSeason.season == cup["season"]
        ).first()
        if not season_exists:
            cup_season = CupSeason(
                cup_competition_id=cup_obj.id,
                season=cup["season"],
                started_at=datetime.now().date()
            )
            db.add(cup_season)
            db.flush()
            print(f"  Created season for {cup['name']}")
            
            # For ABSA Cup, add sample matches (optional – can be added later)
            if cup["name"] == "ABSA Cup":
                teams = db.query(Team).filter(Team.gender == "male", Team.league_tier == 1).limit(16).all()
                if len(teams) >= 2:
                    for i in range(0, len(teams), 2):
                        if i+1 < len(teams):
                            match = CupMatch(
                                cup_season_id=cup_season.id,
                                round_name="Round of 16",
                                home_team_id=teams[i].id,
                                away_team_id=teams[i+1].id,
                                match_date=datetime.now() + timedelta(days=30 + i),
                                venue="National Stadium",
                                is_played=False
                            )
                            db.add(match)
                            print(f"    Added match: {teams[i].name} vs {teams[i+1].name}")
        else:
            print(f"  Season already exists for {cup['name']}")

db.commit()
print("\n✅ Sample cup matches processed (you can add more via admin panel).")

print("\n" + "=" * 50)
print("🎉 ALL DATA INSERTED/UPDATED SUCCESSFULLY! 🎉")
print("=" * 50)
db.close()

for item in news_items:
    slug = item["title"].lower().replace(" ", "-").replace("'", "").replace("?", "")

    if not existing:

# backend/insert_sample_matches.py
from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import Match, Team
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import random

db = SessionLocal()

print("=" * 60)
print("Inserting Sample Match Data")
print("=" * 60)

# Get all teams
teams = db.query(Team).all()
team_names = [team.name for team in teams]

if len(team_names) < 2:
    print("Not enough teams found! Please run insert_league_data.py first.")
    exit()

# Sample matches data
matches_data = [
    # Upcoming matches (future dates)
    {"home_team": "ZESCO United FC", "away_team": "Nkana FC", "match_date": datetime.now() + timedelta(days=3), "venue": "Levy Mwanawasa Stadium", "competition": "Super League", "status": "upcoming"},

    {"home_team": "ZESCO United FC", "away_team": "Power Dynamos FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7), "venue": "Levy Mwanawasa Stadium", "competition": "Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 2, "away_score": 1},
    {"home_team": "Nkana FC", "away_team": "Red Arrows FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=5), "venue": "Nkana Stadium", "competition": "Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 1, "away_score": 1},
    {"home_team": "Kabwe Warriors FC", "away_team": "Green Buffaloes FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=3), "venue": "Godfrey Chitalu Stadium", "competition": "Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 0, "away_score": 2},
    {"home_team": "NAPSA Stars FC", "away_team": "Mufulira Wanderers FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=2), "venue": "NAPSA Stadium", "competition": "Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 3, "away_score": 0},
    {"home_team": "FC MUZA", "away_team": "ZANACO FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=1), "venue": "MUZA Stadium", "competition": "Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 2, "away_score": 2},
    
    # Women's finished matches
    {"home_team": "ZESCO Ndola Girls FC", "away_team": "Indeni Roses FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=4), "venue": "Levy Mwanawasa Stadium", "competition": "Women's Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 3, "away_score": 1},
    {"home_team": "Green Buffaloes Women FC", "away_team": "Red Arrows Women FC", "match_date": datetime.now() - timedelta(days=2), "venue": "Independence Stadium", "competition": "Women's Super League", "status": "finished", "home_score": 2, "away_score": 0},
]

print("\n📊 Inserting matches...")
count = 0
skipped = 0

for match_data in matches_data:
    # Check if teams exist
    home_team = db.query(Team).filter(Team.name == match_data["home_team"]).first()
    away_team = db.query(Team).filter(Team.name == match_data["away_team"]).first()
    
    if not home_team:
        print(f"  ⚠️ Home team not found: {match_data['home_team']}")
        skipped += 1
        continue
    if not away_team:
        print(f"  ⚠️ Away team not found: {match_data['away_team']}")
        skipped += 1
        continue
    
    # Check if match already exists (simple check by teams and date)
    existing = db.query(Match).filter(
        Match.home_team == match_data["home_team"],
        Match.away_team == match_data["away_team"],
        Match.match_date == match_data["match_date"]
    ).first()
    
    if existing:
        print(f"  Skipped (exists): {match_data['home_team']} vs {match_data['away_team']}")
        skipped += 1
        continue
    
    match = Match(
        home_team=match_data["home_team"],
        away_team=match_data["away_team"],
        home_score=match_data.get("home_score", 0),
        away_score=match_data.get("away_score", 0),
        match_date=match_data["match_date"],
        venue=match_data["venue"],
        competition=match_data["competition"],
        status=match_data["status"],
        is_home_match=True
    )
    db.add(match)
    count += 1
    print(f"  Added: {match_data['home_team']} vs {match_data['away_team']} ({match_data['status']})")

db.commit()

print("\n" + "=" * 60)
print("🎉 MATCHES INSERTION COMPLETE! 🎉")
print("=" * 60)
print(f"\nSummary:")
print(f"  - Matches added: {count}")
print(f"  - Skipped: {skipped}")
print(f"\nNow you can view matches at:")
print(f"  - /matches (all matches)")
print(f"  - /matches?status=upcoming (upcoming matches)")
print(f"  - /matches?status=live (live matches)")
print(f"  - /matches?status=finished (finished matches)")

db.close()

# backend/insert_sample_standings.py
from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import LeagueStanding, League, Team
from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload

db = SessionLocal()

print("=" * 60)
print("Inserting Sample League Standings")
print("=" * 60)

# Helper function to create standings
def create_standings(league_name, team_filter, season, standings_data):
    league = db.query(League).filter(League.name == league_name).first()
    if not league:
        print(f"  League '{league_name}' not found!")
        return 0
    
    # Get teams for this league
    teams = db.query(Team).filter(team_filter).all()
    if not teams:
        print(f"  No teams found for league '{league_name}'!")
        return 0
    
    count = 0
    for idx, team in enumerate(teams[:len(standings_data)]):
        if idx >= len(standings_data):
            break
        data = standings_data[idx]
        
        existing = db.query(LeagueStanding).filter(
            LeagueStanding.league_id == league.id,
            LeagueStanding.team_id == team.id,
            LeagueStanding.season == season
        ).first()
        
        if not existing:
            standing = LeagueStanding(
                league_id=league.id,
                team_id=team.id,
                season=season,
                played=data['played'],
                won=data['won'],
                drawn=data['drawn'],
                lost=data['lost'],
                goals_for=data['goals_for'],
                goals_against=data['goals_against'],
                points=data['points']
            )
            db.add(standing)
            count += 1
            print(f"  Added: {team.name} - P{data['points']}")
        else:
            print(f"  Already exists: {team.name}")
    
    return count

# ============ MEN'S SUPER LEAGUE STANDINGS ============
print("\n📊 Men's Super League Standings:")

print(f"  - Women's Super League: {women_super_count} teams")
print(f"  - Men's Division 1: {men_div1_count} teams")
print(f"  - Women's Southern: {women_south_count} teams")
print(f"  - Women's Northern: {women_north_count} teams")
print(f"\nTotal: {men_super_count + women_super_count + men_div1_count + women_south_count + women_north_count} standings added")
print("\nRun 'python check_standings.py' again to verify.")
db.close()

# backend/insert_tournament_data.py
from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import Tournament, TournamentCategory, Gender, NationalTeam, TournamentParticipation
import sys

db = SessionLocal()

# ============ MEN'S TOURNAMENTS ============
men_tournaments = [
    {"name": "International Friendly", "category": TournamentCategory.FRIENDLY, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": False, "is_major_tournament": False},
    {"name": "COSAFA Cup", "category": TournamentCategory.COSAFA, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": False, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "CAF"},
    {"name": "AFCON", "category": TournamentCategory.AFCON, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "CAF"},
    {"name": "Four Nations Tournament", "category": TournamentCategory.FOUR_NATIONS, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": False, "is_major_tournament": False},
    {"name": "CHAN", "category": TournamentCategory.CHAN, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "u23", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "CAF"},
    {"name": "FIFA World Cup", "category": TournamentCategory.FIFA_WORLD_CUP, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "FIFA"},
    {"name": "Olympics", "category": TournamentCategory.OLYMPICS, "gender": Gender.MALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "Olympic"},
]

# ============ WOMEN'S TOURNAMENTS ============
women_tournaments = [
    {"name": "International Friendly", "category": TournamentCategory.FRIENDLY, "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": False, "is_major_tournament": False},
    {"name": "COSAFA Women's Cup", "category": TournamentCategory.COSAFA, "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": False, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "CAF"},
    {"name": "WAFCON", "category": TournamentCategory.WAFCON, "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "CAF"},
    {"name": "Four Nations Tournament", "category": TournamentCategory.FOUR_NATIONS, "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": False, "is_major_tournament": False},
    {"name": "FIFA Women's World Cup", "category": TournamentCategory.FIFA_WORLD_CUP, "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "FIFA"},
    {"name": "Olympics", "category": TournamentCategory.OLYMPICS, "gender": Gender.FEMALE, "age_group": "senior", "has_qualifiers": True, "is_major_tournament": True, "continent": "Olympic"},
]

print("=" * 50)
print("Inserting Tournaments (skipping existing)...")
print("=" * 50)

# Insert Men's Tournaments (commit after each)
for tour in men_tournaments:
    exists = db.query(Tournament).filter(Tournament.name == tour["name"]).first()
    if not exists:
        db.add(Tournament(**tour))
        db.commit()  # Commit immediately to avoid duplicate issues
        print(f"  Added: {tour['name']} (Men)")
    else:
        print(f"  Skipped (already exists): {tour['name']} (Men)")

# Insert Women's Tournaments (commit after each)
for tour in women_tournaments:
    exists = db.query(Tournament).filter(Tournament.name == tour["name"]).first()
    if not exists:
        db.add(Tournament(**tour))
        db.commit()  # Commit immediately
        print(f"  Added: {tour['name']} (Women)")
    else:
        print(f"  Skipped (already exists): {tour['name']} (Women)")

print("\n✅ Tournaments processed successfully!")

# ============ ADD SAMPLE TOURNAMENT PARTICIPATIONS (skip if exist) ============
print("\n" + "=" * 50)
print("Adding Sample Tournament Participations...")
print("=" * 50)

sample_participations = [
    # Chipolopolo (Men's Senior)
    {"national_team_name": "Chipolopolo", "tournament_name": "AFCON", "year": "2023", "round_reached": "Group Stage"},
    {"national_team_name": "Chipolopolo", "tournament_name": "COSAFA Cup", "year": "2024", "round_reached": "Winner"},
    {"national_team_name": "Chipolopolo", "tournament_name": "FIFA World Cup", "year": "2022", "round_reached": "Did Not Qualify"},
    {"national_team_name": "Chipolopolo", "tournament_name": "Four Nations Tournament", "year": "2024", "round_reached": "Runner-up"},
    {"national_team_name": "Chipolopolo", "tournament_name": "CHAN", "year": "2024", "round_reached": "Group Stage"},
    
    # Copper Queens (Women's Senior)
    {"national_team_name": "Copper Queens", "tournament_name": "WAFCON", "year": "2023", "round_reached": "Quarterfinal"},
    {"national_team_name": "Copper Queens", "tournament_name": "COSAFA Women's Cup", "year": "2024", "round_reached": "Winner"},
    {"national_team_name": "Copper Queens", "tournament_name": "FIFA Women's World Cup", "year": "2023", "round_reached": "Group Stage"},
    {"national_team_name": "Copper Queens", "tournament_name": "Olympics", "year": "2024", "round_reached": "Quarterfinal"},
    {"national_team_name": "Copper Queens", "tournament_name": "Four Nations Tournament", "year": "2024", "round_reached": "Winner"},
]

participation_count = 0
for part in sample_participations:
    team = db.query(NationalTeam).filter(NationalTeam.name == part["national_team_name"]).first()
    tournament = db.query(Tournament).filter(Tournament.name == part["tournament_name"]).first()
    if team and tournament:
        exists = db.query(TournamentParticipation).filter(
            TournamentParticipation.national_team_id == team.id,
            TournamentParticipation.tournament_name == part["tournament_name"],
            TournamentParticipation.year == part["year"]
        ).first()
        if not exists:
            db.add(TournamentParticipation(
                national_team_id=team.id,
                tournament_name=part["tournament_name"],
                year=part["year"],
                round_reached=part["round_reached"]
            ))
            db.commit()  # Commit after each participation
            participation_count += 1
            print(f"  Added: {team.name} - {part['tournament_name']} ({part['year']})")
        else:
            print(f"  Skipped (exists): {team.name} - {part['tournament_name']} ({part['year']})")
    else:
        if not team:
            print(f"  Warning: Team '{part['national_team_name']}' not found")
        if not tournament:
            print(f"  Warning: Tournament '{part['tournament_name']}' not found")

print(f"\n✅ Added {participation_count} new tournament participations (skipped existing)")

print("\n" + "=" * 50)
print("🎉 ALL TOURNAMENT DATA INSERTED SUCCESSFULLY! 🎉")
print("=" * 50)
db.close()


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\migrate_to_uuid.sql
====================================================================================================

-- =====================================================
-- COMPLETE DATABASE MIGRATION TO UUID
-- Converts all integer IDs to UUID while preserving data
-- Populates all empty tables with sample data
-- =====================================================

-- Enable UUID extension
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- =====================================================
-- STEP 1: Backup existing integer IDs in temp tables
-- =====================================================

-- Create mapping tables for old integer IDs to new UUIDs
CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_team_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM teams WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_user_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM users WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_league_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM leagues WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_league_standing_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM league_standings WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_news_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM news WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_match_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM matches WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_player_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM players WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_cup_competition_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM cup_competitions WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_cup_season_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM cup_seasons WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_cup_match_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM cup_matches WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_national_team_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM national_teams WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_tournament_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM tournaments WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_tournament_participation_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM tournament_participations WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_promotion_relegation_rule_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM promotion_relegation_rules WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_tag_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM tags WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_bulk_import_log_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM bulk_import_logs WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_continental_qualification_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM continental_qualifications WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_match_event_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM match_events WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_match_report_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM match_reports WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_national_team_fixture_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM national_team_fixtures WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_season_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM seasons WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE TEMP TABLE temp_promotion_relegation_id_map AS
SELECT id AS old_id, uuid_generate_v4() AS new_id 
FROM promotion_relegations WHERE id IS NOT NULL;

-- =====================================================
-- STEP 2: Create new UUID-based tables
-- =====================================================

-- Drop existing foreign key constraints (to be recreated later)
-- Teams table has no outgoing FKs to worry about first

-- Create new teams table
CREATE TABLE teams_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    short_name VARCHAR,
    logo VARCHAR,
    founded INTEGER,
    stadium VARCHAR,
    coach VARCHAR,
    description TEXT,
    gender VARCHAR,
    team_type VARCHAR,
    current_league_level VARCHAR,
    league_tier INTEGER,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new users table
CREATE TABLE users_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    email VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    username VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    hashed_password VARCHAR,
    full_name VARCHAR,
    role USERROLE,
    club_id UUID,
    is_active BOOLEAN,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new leagues table
CREATE TABLE leagues_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    gender GENDER,
    level LEAGUELEVEL,
    tier INTEGER,
    season VARCHAR,
    current_champion_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new league_standings table
CREATE TABLE league_standings_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    league_id UUID,
    team_id UUID,
    season VARCHAR,
    played INTEGER,
    won INTEGER,
    drawn INTEGER,
    lost INTEGER,
    goals_for INTEGER,
    goals_against INTEGER,
    points INTEGER
);

-- Create new news table
CREATE TABLE news_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    title VARCHAR,
    slug VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    content TEXT,
    excerpt VARCHAR(300),
    featured_image VARCHAR,
    author_id UUID,
    views INTEGER,
    is_published BOOLEAN,
    published_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
);

-- Create new matches table
CREATE TABLE matches_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    home_team VARCHAR,
    away_team VARCHAR,
    home_score INTEGER,
    away_score INTEGER,
    match_date TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
    venue VARCHAR,
    competition VARCHAR,
    status VARCHAR,
    is_home_match BOOLEAN,
    referee_id UUID
);

-- Create new players table
CREATE TABLE players_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR,
    position VARCHAR,
    jersey_number INTEGER,
    date_of_birth DATE,
    nationality VARCHAR,
    photo VARCHAR,
    team_id UUID,
    goals INTEGER,
    assists INTEGER,
    appearances INTEGER
);

-- Create new cup_competitions table
CREATE TABLE cup_competitions_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR,
    gender GENDER,
    competition_type VARCHAR,
    season VARCHAR,
    current_champion_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new cup_seasons table
CREATE TABLE cup_seasons_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    cup_competition_id UUID,
    season VARCHAR,
    winner_id UUID,
    runner_up_id UUID,
    started_at DATE,
    ended_at DATE
);

-- Create new cup_matches table
CREATE TABLE cup_matches_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    cup_season_id UUID,
    round_name VARCHAR,
    home_team_id UUID,
    away_team_id UUID,
    home_score INTEGER,
    away_score INTEGER,
    match_date TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
    venue VARCHAR,
    is_played BOOLEAN,
    winner_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new national_teams table
CREATE TABLE national_teams_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    gender GENDER,
    age_group VARCHAR,
    name VARCHAR,
    coach VARCHAR,
    captain_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new tournaments table
CREATE TABLE tournaments_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    category TOURNAMENTCATEGORY,
    gender GENDER,
    age_group VARCHAR,
    has_qualifiers BOOLEAN,
    is_major_tournament BOOLEAN,
    continent VARCHAR,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new tournament_participations table
CREATE TABLE tournament_participations_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    national_team_id UUID,
    tournament_name VARCHAR,
    year VARCHAR,
    round_reached VARCHAR,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new promotion_relegation_rules table
CREATE TABLE promotion_relegation_rules_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    from_league_id UUID,
    to_league_id UUID,
    positions_promoted INTEGER,
    positions_relegated INTEGER,
    season VARCHAR
);

-- Create new promotion_relegations table
CREATE TABLE promotion_relegations_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    team_id UUID,
    from_league_id UUID,
    to_league_id UUID,
    season VARCHAR,
    action VARCHAR,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new tags table
CREATE TABLE tags_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    slug VARCHAR UNIQUE
);

-- Create new news_tags table (junction)
CREATE TABLE news_tags_new (
    news_id UUID,
    tag_id UUID
);

-- Create new bulk_import_logs table
CREATE TABLE bulk_import_logs_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    table_name VARCHAR,
    file_name VARCHAR,
    rows_imported INTEGER,
    errors TEXT,
    imported_by UUID,
    imported_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new continental_qualifications table
CREATE TABLE continental_qualifications_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    season VARCHAR,
    competition_name VARCHAR,
    gender GENDER,
    qualifying_team_id UUID,
    qualification_reason VARCHAR,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new match_events table
CREATE TABLE match_events_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    match_id UUID,
    event_type MATCHEVENTTYPE,
    player_id UUID,
    related_player_id UUID,
    minute INTEGER,
    minute_added INTEGER,
    description VARCHAR(255),
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new match_reports table
CREATE TABLE match_reports_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    match_id UUID UNIQUE,
    referee_id UUID,
    content TEXT,
    yellow_cards JSON,
    red_cards JSON,
    incidents JSON,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new national_team_fixtures table
CREATE TABLE national_team_fixtures_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    national_team_id UUID,
    opponent VARCHAR,
    match_date TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
    venue VARCHAR,
    competition VARCHAR,
    match_type MATCHTYPE,
    home_away VARCHAR,
    goals_for INTEGER,
    goals_against INTEGER,
    result VARCHAR,
    is_played BOOLEAN,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Create new seasons table
CREATE TABLE seasons_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR UNIQUE,
    start_date DATE,
    end_date DATE,
    is_current BOOLEAN
);

-- Create new league_tables (legacy) table
CREATE TABLE league_tables_new (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    team_id UUID,
    played INTEGER,
    won INTEGER,
    drawn INTEGER,
    lost INTEGER,
    goals_for INTEGER,
    goals_against INTEGER,
    points INTEGER,
    season VARCHAR
);

-- =====================================================
-- STEP 3: Migrate data to new tables (convert IDs to UUID)
-- =====================================================

-- Migrate teams
INSERT INTO teams_new (id, name, short_name, logo, founded, stadium, coach, description, gender, team_type, current_league_level, league_tier, created_at)
SELECT tm.new_id, t.name, t.short_name, t.logo, t.founded, t.stadium, t.coach, t.description, t.gender, t.team_type, t.current_league_level, t.league_tier, t.created_at
FROM teams t
JOIN temp_team_id_map tm ON tm.old_id = t.id;

-- Migrate users (club_id will be updated after teams migration)
INSERT INTO users_new (id, email, username, hashed_password, full_name, role, club_id, is_active, created_at)
SELECT um.new_id, u.email, u.username, u.hashed_password, u.full_name, u.role, 
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = u.club_id),
       u.is_active, u.created_at
FROM users u
JOIN temp_user_id_map um ON um.old_id = u.id;

-- Migrate leagues
INSERT INTO leagues_new (id, name, gender, level, tier, season, current_champion_id, created_at)
SELECT lm.new_id, l.name, l.gender, l.level, l.tier, l.season,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = l.current_champion_id),
       l.created_at
FROM leagues l
JOIN temp_league_id_map lm ON lm.old_id = l.id;

-- Migrate league_standings
INSERT INTO league_standings_new (id, league_id, team_id, season, played, won, drawn, lost, goals_for, goals_against, points)
SELECT lsm.new_id,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_league_id_map WHERE old_id = ls.league_id),
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = ls.team_id),
       ls.season, ls.played, ls.won, ls.drawn, ls.lost, ls.goals_for, ls.goals_against, ls.points
FROM league_standings ls
JOIN temp_league_standing_id_map lsm ON lsm.old_id = ls.id;

-- Migrate news
INSERT INTO news_new (id, title, slug, content, excerpt, featured_image, author_id, views, is_published, published_at, created_at, updated_at)
SELECT nm.new_id, n.title, n.slug, n.content, n.excerpt, n.featured_image,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_user_id_map WHERE old_id = n.author_id),
       n.views, n.is_published, n.published_at, n.created_at, n.updated_at
FROM news n
JOIN temp_news_id_map nm ON nm.old_id = n.id;

-- Migrate matches
INSERT INTO matches_new (id, home_team, away_team, home_score, away_score, match_date, venue, competition, status, is_home_match, referee_id)
SELECT mm.new_id, m.home_team, m.away_team, m.home_score, m.away_score, m.match_date, m.venue, m.competition, m.status, m.is_home_match,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_user_id_map WHERE old_id = m.referee_id)
FROM matches m
JOIN temp_match_id_map mm ON mm.old_id = m.id;

-- Migrate players
INSERT INTO players_new (id, name, position, jersey_number, date_of_birth, nationality, photo, team_id, goals, assists, appearances)
SELECT pm.new_id, p.name, p.position, p.jersey_number, p.date_of_birth, p.nationality, p.photo,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = p.team_id),
       p.goals, p.assists, p.appearances
FROM players p
JOIN temp_player_id_map pm ON pm.old_id = p.id;

-- Migrate cup_competitions
INSERT INTO cup_competitions_new (id, name, gender, competition_type, season, current_champion_id, created_at)
SELECT cm.new_id, c.name, c.gender, c.competition_type, c.season,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = c.current_champion_id),
       c.created_at
FROM cup_competitions c
JOIN temp_cup_competition_id_map cm ON cm.old_id = c.id;

-- Migrate cup_seasons
INSERT INTO cup_seasons_new (id, cup_competition_id, season, winner_id, runner_up_id, started_at, ended_at)
SELECT csm.new_id,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_cup_competition_id_map WHERE old_id = cs.cup_competition_id),
       cs.season,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = cs.winner_id),
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = cs.runner_up_id),
       cs.started_at, cs.ended_at
FROM cup_seasons cs
JOIN temp_cup_season_id_map csm ON csm.old_id = cs.id;

-- Migrate cup_matches
INSERT INTO cup_matches_new (id, cup_season_id, round_name, home_team_id, away_team_id, home_score, away_score, match_date, venue, is_played, winner_id, created_at)
SELECT cmm.new_id,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_cup_season_id_map WHERE old_id = cm.cup_season_id),
       cm.round_name,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = cm.home_team_id),
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = cm.away_team_id),
       cm.home_score, cm.away_score, cm.match_date, cm.venue, cm.is_played,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_team_id_map WHERE old_id = cm.winner_id),
       cm.created_at
FROM cup_matches cm
JOIN temp_cup_match_id_map cmm ON cmm.old_id = cm.id;

-- Migrate national_teams
INSERT INTO national_teams_new (id, gender, age_group, name, coach, captain_id, created_at)
SELECT nm.new_id, nt.gender, nt.age_group, nt.name, nt.coach,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_player_id_map WHERE old_id = nt.captain_id),
       nt.created_at
FROM national_teams nt
JOIN temp_national_team_id_map nm ON nm.old_id = nt.id;

-- Migrate tournaments
INSERT INTO tournaments_new (id, name, category, gender, age_group, has_qualifiers, is_major_tournament, continent, created_at)
SELECT tm.new_id, t.name, t.category, t.gender, t.age_group, t.has_qualifiers, t.is_major_tournament, t.continent, t.created_at
FROM tournaments t
JOIN temp_tournament_id_map tm ON tm.old_id = t.id;

-- Migrate tournament_participations
INSERT INTO tournament_participations_new (id, national_team_id, tournament_name, year, round_reached, created_at)
SELECT tpm.new_id,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_national_team_id_map WHERE old_id = tp.national_team_id),
       tp.tournament_name, tp.year, tp.round_reached, tp.created_at
FROM tournament_participations tp
JOIN temp_tournament_participation_id_map tpm ON tpm.old_id = tp.id;

-- Migrate promotion_relegation_rules
INSERT INTO promotion_relegation_rules_new (id, from_league_id, to_league_id, positions_promoted, positions_relegated, season)
SELECT prm.new_id,
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_league_id_map WHERE old_id = pr.from_league_id),
       (SELECT new_id FROM temp_league_id_map WHERE old_id = pr.to_league_id),
       pr.positions_promoted, pr.positions_relegated, pr.season
FROM promotion_relegation_rules pr
JOIN temp_promotion_relegation_rule_id_map prm ON prm.old_id = pr.id;

    (6, 3), (7, 2), (8, 10), (9, 2), (10, 3)
) AS mappings(news_old_id, tag_old_id)
JOIN news n ON n.id = news_old_id

-- STEP 6: Recreate indexes and constraints

-- Teams indexes
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_teams_name ON teams (name);
CREATE INDEX ix_teams_id ON teams (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_teams_gender ON teams (gender);

-- Users indexes
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_users_email ON users (email);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_users_username ON users (username);
CREATE INDEX ix_users_id ON users (id);

-- Leagues indexes
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_leagues_name ON leagues (name);
CREATE INDEX ix_leagues_id ON leagues (id);

-- League standings indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_league_standings_id ON league_standings (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_league_standings_league_id ON league_standings (league_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_league_standings_team_id ON league_standings (team_id);

-- News indexes
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_news_slug ON news (slug);
CREATE INDEX ix_news_title ON news (title);
CREATE INDEX ix_news_id ON news (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_news_author_id ON news (author_id);

-- Matches indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_matches_id ON matches (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_matches_home_team ON matches (home_team);
CREATE INDEX ix_matches_away_team ON matches (away_team);
CREATE INDEX ix_matches_status ON matches (status);
CREATE INDEX ix_matches_referee_id ON matches (referee_id);

-- Players indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_players_id ON players (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_players_name ON players (name);
CREATE INDEX ix_players_team_id ON players (team_id);

-- Cup competitions indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_cup_competitions_id ON cup_competitions (id);

-- Cup seasons indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_cup_seasons_id ON cup_seasons (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_cup_seasons_cup_competition_id ON cup_seasons (cup_competition_id);

-- Cup matches indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_cup_matches_id ON cup_matches (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_cup_matches_cup_season_id ON cup_matches (cup_season_id);

-- National teams indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_national_teams_id ON national_teams (id);

-- Tournaments indexes
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_tournaments_name ON tournaments (name);
CREATE INDEX ix_tournaments_id ON tournaments (id);

-- Tournament participations indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_tournament_participations_id ON tournament_participations (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_tournament_participations_national_team_id ON tournament_participations (national_team_id);

-- Tags indexes
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_tags_name ON tags (name);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix_tags_slug ON tags (slug);
CREATE INDEX ix_tags_id ON tags (id);

-- Bulk import logs indexes
CREATE INDEX ix_bulk_import_logs_id ON bulk_import_logs (id);
CREATE INDEX ix_bulk_import_logs_imported_by ON bulk_import_logs (imported_by);

-- Foreign key constraints
ALTER TABLE users ADD CONSTRAINT fk_users_club_id FOREIGN KEY (club_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE leagues ADD CONSTRAINT fk_leagues_current_champion_id FOREIGN KEY (current_champion_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE league_standings ADD CONSTRAINT fk_league_standings_league_id FOREIGN KEY (league_id) REFERENCES leagues(id);
ALTER TABLE league_standings ADD CONSTRAINT fk_league_standings_team_id FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE news ADD CONSTRAINT fk_news_author_id FOREIGN KEY (author_id) REFERENCES users(id);
ALTER TABLE matches ADD CONSTRAINT fk_matches_referee_id FOREIGN KEY (referee_id) REFERENCES users(id);
ALTER TABLE players ADD CONSTRAINT fk_players_team_id FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_competitions ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_competitions_current_champion_id FOREIGN KEY (current_champion_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_seasons ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_seasons_cup_competition_id FOREIGN KEY (cup_competition_id) REFERENCES cup_competitions(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_seasons ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_seasons_winner_id FOREIGN KEY (winner_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_seasons ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_seasons_runner_up_id FOREIGN KEY (runner_up_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_matches ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_matches_cup_season_id FOREIGN KEY (cup_season_id) REFERENCES cup_seasons(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_matches ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_matches_home_team_id FOREIGN KEY (home_team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_matches ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_matches_away_team_id FOREIGN KEY (away_team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE cup_matches ADD CONSTRAINT fk_cup_matches_winner_id FOREIGN KEY (winner_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE national_teams ADD CONSTRAINT fk_national_teams_captain_id FOREIGN KEY (captain_id) REFERENCES players(id);
ALTER TABLE tournament_participations ADD CONSTRAINT fk_tournament_participations_national_team_id FOREIGN KEY (national_team_id) REFERENCES national_teams(id);
ALTER TABLE promotion_relegation_rules ADD CONSTRAINT fk_pr_rules_from_league_id FOREIGN KEY (from_league_id) REFERENCES leagues(id);
ALTER TABLE promotion_relegation_rules ADD CONSTRAINT fk_pr_rules_to_league_id FOREIGN KEY (to_league_id) REFERENCES leagues(id);
ALTER TABLE promotion_relegations ADD CONSTRAINT fk_pr_team_id FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE promotion_relegations ADD CONSTRAINT fk_pr_from_league_id FOREIGN KEY (from_league_id) REFERENCES leagues(id);
ALTER TABLE promotion_relegations ADD CONSTRAINT fk_pr_to_league_id FOREIGN KEY (to_league_id) REFERENCES leagues(id);
ALTER TABLE news_tags ADD CONSTRAINT fk_news_tags_news_id FOREIGN KEY (news_id) REFERENCES news(id);
ALTER TABLE news_tags ADD CONSTRAINT fk_news_tags_tag_id FOREIGN KEY (tag_id) REFERENCES tags(id);
ALTER TABLE bulk_import_logs ADD CONSTRAINT fk_bulk_import_logs_imported_by FOREIGN KEY (imported_by) REFERENCES users(id);
ALTER TABLE continental_qualifications ADD CONSTRAINT fk_continental_qualifications_team_id FOREIGN KEY (qualifying_team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);
ALTER TABLE match_events ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_events_match_id FOREIGN KEY (match_id) REFERENCES matches(id);
ALTER TABLE match_events ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_events_player_id FOREIGN KEY (player_id) REFERENCES players(id);
ALTER TABLE match_events ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_events_related_player_id FOREIGN KEY (related_player_id) REFERENCES players(id);
ALTER TABLE match_reports ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_reports_match_id FOREIGN KEY (match_id) REFERENCES matches(id);
ALTER TABLE match_reports ADD CONSTRAINT fk_match_reports_referee_id FOREIGN KEY (referee_id) REFERENCES users(id);
ALTER TABLE national_team_fixtures ADD CONSTRAINT fk_ntf_national_team_id FOREIGN KEY (national_team_id) REFERENCES national_teams(id);
ALTER TABLE league_tables ADD CONSTRAINT fk_league_tables_team_id FOREIGN KEY (team_id) REFERENCES teams(id);

# backend/rebuild_db.py
from app.database import engine
from app import models
models.Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=engine)
models.Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
print("Database rebuilt.")

FILE: backend\run.py

from app import create_app
from app.database import db
from app.models import User
from app.auth import get_password_hash
from sqlalchemy import text

app = create_app()

with app.app_context():
    # Create tables only if they don't exist – no data loss
    db.create_all()
    print("✅ Tables created (if missing).")

    # Ensure role column is VARCHAR (if still ENUM, alter it)
    try:
        db.session.execute(text("ALTER TABLE users ALTER COLUMN role TYPE VARCHAR USING role::text;"))
        db.session.commit()
        print("✅ Column role altered to VARCHAR")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ Column role already VARCHAR or error: {e}")

    # Create a default admin user if none exists
    if not User.query.filter_by(email='admin@faz.co.zm').first():
        admin = User(
            email='admin@faz.co.zm',
            username='admin@faz.co.zm',
            full_name='FAZ Administrator',
            role='super_admin',
            is_active=True
        )
        admin.hashed_password = get_password_hash('Admin@123')
        db.session.add(admin)
        db.session.commit()
        print("✅ Default admin created (admin@faz.co.zm / Admin@123)")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    app.run(host='0.0.0.0', port=8000, debug=True)


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\set_admin_role.py
====================================================================================================

from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import User, UserRole

db = SessionLocal()
admin = db.query(User).filter(User.username == "admin").first()
if admin:
    admin.role = UserRole.ADMIN
    db.commit()
    print("Admin role updated to ADMIN")
else:
    print("Admin not found, run init_db.py first")
db.close()

# backend/test_auth.py
from app.auth import verify_password
from app.database import SessionLocal
from app.models import User

db = SessionLocal()
user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == "admin").first()
if user:
    result = verify_password("admin123", user.hashed_password)
    print(f"Verification result for 'admin123': {result}")
else:
    print("User not found")
db.close()

# app/__init__.py
from flask import Flask
from flask_cors import CORS
from flask_jwt_extended import JWTManager
from flask_migrate import Migrate
from app.database import db   # <-- import the single db instance
import os
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()

def create_app():
    app = Flask(__name__)
    app.url_map.strict_slashes = False
    app.config['SECRET_KEY'] = os.getenv('SECRET_KEY', 'dev-secret')
    app.config['JWT_SECRET_KEY'] = os.getenv('SECRET_KEY', 'dev-jwt-secret')
    app.config['SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI'] = os.getenv('DATABASE_URL')
    app.config['SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS'] = False

    # Initialize db with the app
    db.init_app(app)
    migrate = Migrate(app, db)

    CORS(app)
    JWTManager(app)

    # Register blueprints
    from app.routes import (
        admin, bulk_import, competitions, cups, leagues,
        match_events, matches, media, national_teams, news,
        players, reports, shop, tables, teams, users
    )
    from app.ticketing import api as ticket_api
    from app.ticketing import auth as ticket_auth
    from app.ticketing import admin as ticket_admin
    from app.ticketing import club as ticket_club
    from app.ticketing import payment as ticket_payment

    from app.routes import competitions_management

    app.register_blueprint(admin.bp, url_prefix='/api/admin')
    app.register_blueprint(bulk_import.bp, url_prefix='/api/bulk-import')
    app.register_blueprint(competitions.bp, url_prefix='/api/competitions')
    app.register_blueprint(competitions_management.bp, url_prefix='/api/competitions-management')
    app.register_blueprint(cups.bp, url_prefix='/api/cups')
    app.register_blueprint(leagues.bp, url_prefix='/api/leagues')
    app.register_blueprint(match_events.bp, url_prefix='/api/match-events')
    app.register_blueprint(matches.bp, url_prefix='/api/matches')
    app.register_blueprint(media.bp, url_prefix='/api/media')
    app.register_blueprint(national_teams.bp, url_prefix='/api/national-teams')
    app.register_blueprint(news.bp, url_prefix='/api/news')
    app.register_blueprint(players.bp, url_prefix='/api/players')
    app.register_blueprint(reports.bp, url_prefix='/api/reports')
    app.register_blueprint(shop.bp, url_prefix='/api/shop')
    app.register_blueprint(tables.bp, url_prefix='/api/league-table')
    app.register_blueprint(teams.bp, url_prefix='/api/teams')
    app.register_blueprint(users.bp, url_prefix='/api/users')
    app.register_blueprint(ticket_api.bp, url_prefix='/api/ticketing')
    app.register_blueprint(ticket_auth.bp, url_prefix='/api/ticketing/auth')
    app.register_blueprint(ticket_admin.bp, url_prefix='/api/ticketing/admin')
    app.register_blueprint(ticket_club.bp, url_prefix='/api/ticketing/club')
    app.register_blueprint(ticket_payment.bp, url_prefix='/api/ticketing/payment')

    @app.route('/api/health')
    def health_check():
        return {"status": "healthy", "message": "FAZ API is running"}

    @app.route('/api')
    def root():
        return {"message": "Welcome to Football Association of Zambia API"}

    return app

# app/auth.py
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Optional
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from flask import request, jsonify, abort, Blueprint
from flask_jwt_extended import create_access_token, get_jwt_identity, jwt_required, verify_jwt_in_request
from functools import wraps
from app.database import db
from app.models import User, UserRole
import os

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")

SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("SECRET_KEY", "dev-secret")
ALGORITHM = os.getenv("ALGORITHM", "HS256")
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES", 30))

# ---------- Password helpers ----------
def verify_password(plain_password, hashed_password):
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)

def get_password_hash(password):
    return pwd_context.hash(password)

# ---------- JWT helpers ----------
def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None):
    to_encode = data.copy()
    if expires_delta:
        expire = datetime.utcnow() + expires_delta
    else:
        expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=15)
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)
    return encoded_jwt

# ---------- User authentication ----------
def authenticate_user(username: str, password: str):
    user = User.query.filter_by(username=username).first()
    if not user or not verify_password(password, user.hashed_password):
        return False
    return user

def get_current_user():
    try:
        verify_jwt_in_request()
        user_id = get_jwt_identity()
        user = User.query.get(user_id)
        if not user:
            abort(401, description="User not found")
        return user
    except Exception:
        abort(401, description="Invalid token")

def get_current_user_optional():
    try:
        verify_jwt_in_request()
        user_id = get_jwt_identity()
        return User.query.get(user_id)
    except:
        return None

def admin_required(f):
    @wraps(f)
    def decorated(*args, **kwargs):
        user = get_current_user()
        if user.role not in ['admin', 'super_admin']:
            abort(403, description="Admin only")
        return f(*args, **kwargs)
    return decorated

# ---------- Auth Blueprint ----------
auth_bp = Blueprint('auth', __name__, url_prefix='/auth')

@auth_bp.route('/register', methods=['POST'])
def register():
    data = request.get_json()
    if not data:
        return jsonify({'error': 'No data provided'}), 400

    email = data.get('email')
    password = data.get('password')
    first_name = data.get('first_name')
    last_name = data.get('last_name')
    phone = data.get('phone')
    role = data.get('role', 'viewer')

    if not email or not password:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Email and password required'}), 400

    if User.query.filter_by(email=email).first():
        return jsonify({'error': 'Email already registered'}), 409

    user = User(
        email=email,
        username=email,
        full_name=f"{first_name or ''} {last_name or ''}".strip(),
        role=role,
        is_active=True
    )
    user.hashed_password = get_password_hash(password)

    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    access_token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={'role': user.role},
        expires_delta=timedelta(hours=24)
    )
    return jsonify({
        'message': 'Registration successful',
        'user': user.to_dict(),
        'access_token': access_token
    }), 201

@auth_bp.route('/login', methods=['POST'])
def login():
    data = request.get_json()
    email = data.get('email')
    password = data.get('password')

    user = User.query.filter_by(email=email).first()
    if not user or not verify_password(password, user.hashed_password):
        return jsonify({'error': 'Invalid email or password'}), 401

    if not user.is_active:
        return jsonify({'error': 'Account disabled'}), 403

    access_token = create_access_token(
        identity=str(user.id),
        additional_claims={'role': user.role},
        expires_delta=timedelta(hours=24)
    )
    return jsonify({
        'user': user.to_dict(),
        'access_token': access_token
    }), 200

@auth_bp.route('/me', methods=['GET'])
@jwt_required()
def get_current_user_info():
    user_id = get_jwt_identity()
    user = User.query.get(user_id)
    if not user:
        return jsonify({'error': 'User not found'}), 404
    return jsonify(user.to_dict()), 200

@auth_bp.route('/ping', methods=['GET'])
def ping():
    return jsonify({"message": "Auth blueprint is alive!"}), 200
