from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, DateTime, Text, Boolean, ForeignKey, Table, JSON, Date, Enum as SQLEnum
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from .database import Base
import enum
import uuid

# Association table for news and tags
news_tags = Table(
    'news_tags',
    Base.metadata,
    Column('news_id', UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey('news.id')),
    Column('tag_id', UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey('tags.id'))
)

# ---------- Enums ----------
class UserRole(str, enum.Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MATCH_COMMISSIONER = "match_commissioner"
    MEDIA_OFFICER = "media_officer"
    CLUB_REP = "club_rep"
    REFEREE = "referee"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

class MatchEventType(str, enum.Enum):
    GOAL = "goal"
    ASSIST = "assist"
    YELLOW_CARD = "yellow_card"
    RED_CARD = "red_card"
    SUBSTITUTION = "substitution"
    PENALTY = "penalty"
    OWN_GOAL = "own_goal"

class Gender(str, enum.Enum):
    MALE = "male"
    FEMALE = "female"

class LeagueLevel(str, enum.Enum):
    SUPER_LEAGUE = "super_league"
    DIVISION_ONE = "division_one"
    DIVISION_TWO = "division_two"
    DIVISION_THREE = "division_three"
    PROVINCIAL = "provincial"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_SOUTH = "national_league_south"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_NORTH = "national_league_north"

class MatchType(str, enum.Enum):
    LEAGUE = "league"
    CUP = "cup"
    FRIENDLY = "friendly"
    TOURNAMENT = "tournament"
    QUALIFIER = "qualifier"

class TournamentCategory(str, enum.Enum):
    FRIENDLY = "friendly"
    COSAFA = "cosafa"
    AFCON = "afcon"
    WAFCON = "wafcon"
    FOUR_NATIONS = "four_nations"
    CHAN = "chan"
    FIFA_WORLD_CUP = "fifa_world_cup"
    OLYMPICS = "olympics"

# ---------- Team ----------
class Team(Base):
    __tablename__ = "teams"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    short_name = Column(String)
    logo = Column(String, nullable=True)
    founded = Column(Integer)
    stadium = Column(String)
    coach = Column(String)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    gender = Column(String, default="male")
    team_type = Column(String, default="club")
    current_league_level = Column(String, nullable=True)
    league_tier = Column(Integer, default=3)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

# ---------- User ----------
class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    email = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    username = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    hashed_password = Column(String)
    full_name = Column(String)
    role = Column(SQLEnum(UserRole), default=UserRole.VIEWER)
    club_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    club = relationship("Team")
    matches_as_referee = relationship("Match", foreign_keys="Match.referee_id", backref="referee")

# ---------- Player ----------

    goals = Column(Integer, default=0)
    assists = Column(Integer, default=0)
    appearances = Column(Integer, default=0)
    
    team = relationship("Team")

# ---------- Match ----------
class Match(Base):
    __tablename__ = "matches"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    home_team = Column(String, index=True)
    away_team = Column(String, index=True)
    home_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    away_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    match_date = Column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    venue = Column(String)
    competition = Column(String)
    status = Column(String, default="upcoming")
    is_home_match = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    referee_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    
    events = relationship("MatchEvent", back_populates="match", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    report = relationship("MatchReport", back_populates="match", uselist=False)

# ---------- MatchEvent ----------
class MatchEvent(Base):
    __tablename__ = "match_events"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("matches.id"))
    event_type = Column(SQLEnum(MatchEventType))
    player_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("players.id"))
    related_player_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("players.id"), nullable=True)
    minute = Column(Integer)
    minute_added = Column(Integer, default=0)
    description = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    match = relationship("Match", back_populates="events")
    player = relationship("Player", foreign_keys=[player_id])
    related_player = relationship("Player", foreign_keys=[related_player_id])

# ---------- MatchReport ----------

    __tablename__ = "match_reports"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("matches.id"), unique=True)
    referee_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"))
    content = Column(Text)
    yellow_cards = Column(JSON, default=[])
    red_cards = Column(JSON, default=[])
    incidents = Column(JSON, default=[])
    submitted_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    match = relationship("Match", back_populates="report")
    referee = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[referee_id])

    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    team_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"))
    played = Column(Integer, default=0)
    won = Column(Integer, default=0)
    drawn = Column(Integer, default=0)
    lost = Column(Integer, default=0)
    goals_for = Column(Integer, default=0)
    goals_against = Column(Integer, default=0)
    points = Column(Integer, default=0)
    season = Column(String)
    
    team = relationship("Team")


# ---------- Competition (Main competition structure) ----------
class Competition(Base):
    __tablename__ = "competitions"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    gender = Column(String)  # 'male', 'female', 'both'
    competition_type = Column(String)  # 'league', 'cup', 'tournament', 'continental'
    level = Column(String, nullable=True)  # 'tier1', 'tier2', 'tier3'
    icon = Column(String, nullable=True)  # emoji or icon name
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    season = Column(String)  # e.g., "2025-2026"
    current_champion_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=True)
    is_archived = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())
    
    current_champion = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[current_champion_id])
    seasons = relationship("CompetitionSeason", back_populates="competition", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class CompetitionSeason(Base):
    __tablename__ = "competition_seasons"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    competition_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("competitions.id"), nullable=False)
    season = Column(String, nullable=False)
    champion_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=True)
    runner_up_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=True)
    started_at = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    ended_at = Column(Date, nullable=True)
    is_archived = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    competition = relationship("Competition", back_populates="seasons")
    champion = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[champion_id])
    runner_up = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[runner_up_id])

class ProductCategory(Base):
    __tablename__ = "product_categories"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)  # replicas, legends, training, accessories
    display_name = Column(String)  # "Official Replicas", "Legends Collection", etc.
    icon = Column(String, nullable=True)  # emoji or icon class
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    display_order = Column(Integer, default=0)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

class Product(Base):
    __tablename__ = "products"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String, index=True)
    slug = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    category_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("product_categories.id"))
    description = Column(Text)
    price = Column(Integer)  # in Zambian Kwacha (ZMW) - stored as cents? or whole number
    compare_price = Column(Integer, nullable=True)  # original price for sale display
    image_url = Column(String, nullable=True)
    images = Column(JSON, default=[])  # array of additional image URLs
    sizes = Column(JSON, default=[])  # array of available sizes (e.g., ["S", "M", "L", "XL"])
    colors = Column(JSON, default=[])  # array of available colors
    stock_quantity = Column(Integer, default=0)
    is_featured = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())
    
    category = relationship("ProductCategory", backref="products")

class Cart(Base):
    __tablename__ = "carts"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)  # null for guest carts
    session_id = Column(String, index=True)  # for guest users
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())
    
    user = relationship("User")
    items = relationship("CartItem", back_populates="cart", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class CartItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cart_items"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    cart_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("carts.id"))
    product_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("products.id"))
    quantity = Column(Integer, default=1)
    size = Column(String, nullable=True)
    color = Column(String, nullable=True)
    price_at_add = Column(Integer)  # price when added to cart
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    cart = relationship("Cart", back_populates="items")
    product = relationship("Product")

class Order(Base):
    __tablename__ = "orders"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    order_number = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    customer_name = Column(String)
    customer_email = Column(String)
    customer_phone = Column(String)
    shipping_address = Column(Text)
    subtotal = Column(Integer)
    delivery_fee = Column(Integer, default=0)
    total_amount = Column(Integer)
    payment_method = Column(String, nullable=True)  # e.g., "mtn_momo", "airtel_momo", "card"
    payment_reference = Column(String, nullable=True)  # deposit_id from PawaPay
    payment_status = Column(String, default="pending")  # pending, paid, failed, refunded
    order_status = Column(String, default="pending")  # pending, processing, shipped, delivered, cancelled
    notes = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())
    
    user = relationship("User")
    items = relationship("OrderItem", back_populates="order", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class OrderItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "order_items"
    
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    order_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("orders.id"))
    product_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("products.id"))
    product_name = Column(String)
    product_image = Column(String, nullable=True)
    quantity = Column(Integer)
    size = Column(String, nullable=True)
    color = Column(String, nullable=True)
    unit_price = Column(Integer)
    subtotal = Column(Integer)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    order = relationship("Order", back_populates="items")
    product = relationship("Product")

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, DateTime, Text, Boolean, ForeignKey, Table, JSON, Date, Enum as SQLEnum
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from .database import Base
import enum

# Association table for news and tags
news_tags = Table(
    'news_tags',
    Base.metadata,
    Column('news_id', Integer, ForeignKey('news.id')),
    Column('tag_id', Integer, ForeignKey('tags.id'))
)

# ---------- Enums ----------
class UserRole(str, enum.Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MATCH_COMMISSIONER = "match_commissioner"
    MEDIA_OFFICER = "media_officer"
    CLUB_REP = "club_rep"
    REFEREE = "referee"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

class MatchEventType(str, enum.Enum):
    GOAL = "goal"
    ASSIST = "assist"
    YELLOW_CARD = "yellow_card"
    RED_CARD = "red_card"
    SUBSTITUTION = "substitution"
    PENALTY = "penalty"
    OWN_GOAL = "own_goal"

# ---------- Team (used by others) ----------
class Team(Base):
    __tablename__ = "teams"
    
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    short_name = Column(String)
    logo = Column(String, nullable=True)
    founded = Column(Integer)
    stadium = Column(String)
    coach = Column(String)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    # New fields:
    gender = Column(String, default="male")  # "male" or "female"
    team_type = Column(String, default="club")  # "club" or "national"
    current_league_level = Column(String, nullable=True)
    league_tier = Column(Integer, default=3)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
# ---------- Player ----------
class Player(Base):
    __tablename__ = "players"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String, index=True)
    position = Column(String)
    jersey_number = Column(Integer)
    date_of_birth = Column(Date)
    nationality = Column(String)
    photo = Column(String)
    team_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("teams.id"))
    goals = Column(Integer, default=0)
    assists = Column(Integer, default=0)
    appearances = Column(Integer, default=0)
    team = relationship("Team")

# ---------- Match (must be before User because User references it) ----------
class Match(Base):
    __tablename__ = "matches"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    home_team = Column(String, index=True)
    away_team = Column(String, index=True)
    home_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    away_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    match_date = Column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    venue = Column(String)
    competition = Column(String)
    status = Column(String, default="upcoming")  # upcoming, live, finished
    is_home_match = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    referee_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    
    events = relationship("MatchEvent", back_populates="match", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    report = relationship("MatchReport", back_populates="match", uselist=False)

# ---------- User ----------
class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    email = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    username = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    hashed_password = Column(String)
    full_name = Column(String)
    role = Column(SQLEnum(UserRole), default=UserRole.VIEWER)
    club_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    club = relationship("Team")
    matches_as_referee = relationship("Match", foreign_keys=[Match.referee_id], backref="referee")

# ---------- MatchEvent ----------
class MatchEvent(Base):
    __tablename__ = "match_events"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    match_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("matches.id"))
    event_type = Column(SQLEnum(MatchEventType))
    player_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("players.id"))
    related_player_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("players.id"), nullable=True)
    minute = Column(Integer)
    minute_added = Column(Integer, default=0)
    description = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    match = relationship("Match", back_populates="events")
    player = relationship("Player", foreign_keys=[player_id])
    related_player = relationship("Player", foreign_keys=[related_player_id])

# ---------- MatchReport ----------
class MatchReport(Base):
    __tablename__ = "match_reports"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    match_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("matches.id"), unique=True)
    referee_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"))
    content = Column(Text)
    yellow_cards = Column(JSON, default=[])
    red_cards = Column(JSON, default=[])
    incidents = Column(JSON, default=[])
    submitted_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    match = relationship("Match", back_populates="report")
    referee = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[referee_id])

# ---------- BulkImportLog ----------
class BulkImportLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "bulk_import_logs"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    table_name = Column(String)
    file_name = Column(String)
    rows_imported = Column(Integer)
    errors = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    imported_by = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"))
    imported_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

# ---------- News & Tags ----------
class News(Base):
    __tablename__ = "news"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    title = Column(String, index=True)
    slug = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    content = Column(Text)
    excerpt = Column(String(300))
    featured_image = Column(String)
    author_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"))
    views = Column(Integer, default=0)
    is_published = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    published_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())
    
    author = relationship("User")
    tags = relationship("Tag", secondary=news_tags, back_populates="news")

class Tag(Base):
    __tablename__ = "tags"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    slug = Column(String, unique=True)
    news = relationship("News", secondary=news_tags, back_populates="tags")

    goals_against = Column(Integer, default=0)
    points = Column(Integer, default=0)

    team = relationship("Team")

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    season = Column(String)
    competition_name = Column(String)  # e.g., "CAF Champions League", "CAF Confederation Cup", "COSAFA Women's Champions League"
    gender = Column(SQLEnum(Gender))
    qualifying_team_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("teams.id"))
    qualification_reason = Column(String)  # e.g., "Super League Winner", "Super League Runner-up"
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    
    qualifying_team = relationship("Team")

# ---------- Enums ----------
class UserRoleEnum(str, enum.Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MATCH_COMMISSIONER = "match_commissioner"
    MEDIA_OFFICER = "media_officer"
    CLUB_REP = "club_rep"
    REFEREE = "referee"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

class MatchEventType(str, enum.Enum):
    GOAL = "goal"
    ASSIST = "assist"
    YELLOW_CARD = "yellow_card"
    RED_CARD = "red_card"
    SUBSTITUTION = "substitution"
    PENALTY = "penalty"
    OWN_GOAL = "own_goal"

class Gender(str, enum.Enum):
    MALE = "male"
    FEMALE = "female"

class LeagueLevel(str, enum.Enum):
    SUPER_LEAGUE = "super_league"
    DIVISION_ONE = "division_one"
    DIVISION_TWO = "division_two"
    DIVISION_THREE = "division_three"
    PROVINCIAL = "provincial"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_SOUTH = "national_league_south"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_NORTH = "national_league_north"

====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\websocket.py
====================================================================================================

from fastapi import WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from typing import List, Dict
import json

class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: Dict[str, List[WebSocket]] = {}  # match_id -> list of websockets

    async def connect(self, websocket: WebSocket, match_id: str):
        await websocket.accept()
        if match_id not in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[match_id] = []
        self.active_connections[match_id].append(websocket)

    def disconnect(self, websocket: WebSocket, match_id: str):
        if match_id in self.active_connections:
            self.active_connections[match_id].remove(websocket)

    async def broadcast_match_update(self, match_id: str, data: dict):
        if match_id in self.active_connections:
            for connection in self.active_connections[match_id]:
                try:
                    await connection.send_json(data)
                except:
                    pass

manager = ConnectionManager()

====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\models\_base.py
====================================================================================================

from app.database import db
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, DateTime, Text, Boolean, ForeignKey, Table, JSON, Date, Enum as SQLEnum
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
import enum
import uuid

# ------------------ Custom base model with to_dict ------------------
class Base(db.Model):
    __abstract__ = True

    def to_dict(self):
        return {c.name: getattr(self, c.name) for c in self.__table__.columns}

# Mixin for models that don't inherit from Base (optional)
class ToDictMixin:
    def to_dict(self):
        return {c.name: getattr(self, c.name) for c in self.__table__.columns}

# ------------------ Enums (unchanged) ------------------
class UserRole(str, enum.Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    MATCH_COMMISSIONER = "match_commissioner"
    MEDIA_OFFICER = "media_officer"
    CLUB_REP = "club_rep"
    REFEREE = "referee"
    VIEWER = "viewer"

class MatchEventType(str, enum.Enum):
    GOAL = "goal"
    ASSIST = "assist"
    YELLOW_CARD = "yellow_card"
    RED_CARD = "red_card"
    SUBSTITUTION = "substitution"
    PENALTY = "penalty"
    OWN_GOAL = "own_goal"

class Gender(str, enum.Enum):
    MALE = "male"
    FEMALE = "female"

class LeagueLevel(str, enum.Enum):
    SUPER_LEAGUE = "super_league"
    DIVISION_ONE = "division_one"
    DIVISION_TWO = "division_two"
    DIVISION_THREE = "division_three"
    PROVINCIAL = "provincial"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_SOUTH = "national_league_south"
    NATIONAL_LEAGUE_NORTH = "national_league_north"

class MatchType(str, enum.Enum):
    LEAGUE = "league"
    CUP = "cup"
    FRIENDLY = "friendly"
    TOURNAMENT = "tournament"
    QUALIFIER = "qualifier"

class TournamentCategory(str, enum.Enum):
    FRIENDLY = "friendly"
    COSAFA = "cosafa"
    AFCON = "afcon"
    WAFCON = "wafcon"
    FOUR_NATIONS = "four_nations"
    CHAN = "chan"
    FIFA_WORLD_CUP = "fifa_world_cup"
    OLYMPICS = "olympics"

# app/models/associations.py
from sqlalchemy import Table, Column, ForeignKey
from app.database import db

news_tags = Table(
    'news_tags',
    db.metadata,          # <-- changed from Base.metadata
    Column('news_id', ForeignKey('news.id'), primary_key=True),
    Column('tag_id', ForeignKey('tags.id'), primary_key=True)
)


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\models\bulk_import_log.py
====================================================================================================

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, Text, ForeignKey, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base
import uuid

class BulkImportLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "bulk_import_logs"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    table_name = Column(String)
    file_name = Column(String)
    rows_imported = Column(Integer)
    errors = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    imported_by = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"))
    imported_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    importer = relationship("User")

FILE: backend\app\models\cart.py

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, ForeignKey, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base
import uuid

class Cart(Base):
    __tablename__ = "carts"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    session_id = Column(String, index=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    user = relationship("User")
    items = relationship("CartItem", back_populates="cart", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, ForeignKey, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base
import uuid

class CartItem(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cart_items"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    cart_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("carts.id"))
    product_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("products.id"))

    size = Column(String, nullable=True)

    price_at_add = Column(Integer)

    cart = relationship("Cart", back_populates="items")
    product = relationship("Product")

# app/models/club.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Text, Boolean, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class Club(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "clubs"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String(100), nullable=False, unique=True)
    short_name = Column(String(10))
    logo_url = Column(String(255))
    description = Column(Text)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    users = relationship("User", back_populates="club")
    ticket_matches = relationship("TicketMatch", back_populates="club")


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\models\competition.py
====================================================================================================

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Text, Boolean, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class Competition(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "competitions"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    name = Column(String(200), nullable=False)
    competition_type = Column(String(50), nullable=False)  # 'league', 'tournament', 'friendly_series'
    gender = Column(String(20), nullable=False)  # 'male', 'female', 'mixed'
    age_group = Column(String(50))  # 'senior', 'u23', 'u20', 'u17', 'u15'
    scope = Column(String(50))  # 'local', 'regional', 'continental', 'international'
    description = Column(Text)
    logo_url = Column(String(255))
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

FILE: backend\app\models\competition_match.py
====================================================================================================

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, DateTime, Boolean, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class CompetitionMatch(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "competition_matches"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    season_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("competition_seasons.id"), nullable=False)
    stage_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("stages.id"), nullable=False)
    group_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("groups.id"), nullable=True)
    home_team_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=False)
    away_team_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=False)
    match_date = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    venue = Column(String(200))
    home_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    away_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    status = Column(String(20), default='scheduled')
    is_ticketed = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    ticket_match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), onupdate=func.now())

    season = relationship("CompetitionSeason", back_populates="matches")
    stage = relationship("Stage", back_populates="matches")
    group = relationship("Group", back_populates="matches")
    home_team = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[home_team_id])
    away_team = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[away_team_id])

FILE: backend\app\models\competition_season.py
====================================================================================================

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Date, ForeignKey, Boolean, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class CompetitionSeason(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "competition_seasons"

    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    competition_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("competitions.id"), nullable=False)
    name = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    start_date = Column(Date)
    end_date = Column(Date)
    status = Column(String(20), default='upcoming')
    is_current = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    competition = relationship("Competition", backref="seasons")
    stages = relationship("Stage", back_populates="season", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    matches = relationship("CompetitionMatch", back_populates="season")
    standings = relationship("Standing", back_populates="season")

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, DateTime, Boolean, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base
import uuid

class CupMatch(Base):
    __tablename__ = "cup_matches"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    cup_season_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("cup_seasons.id"))
    round_name = Column(String)
    home_team_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"))
    away_team_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"))
    home_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    away_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    match_date = Column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    venue = Column(String)
    is_played = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    winner_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("teams.id"), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    cup_season = relationship("CupSeason")
    home_team = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[home_team_id])
    away_team = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[away_team_id])
    winner = relationship("Team", foreign_keys=[winner_id])

# app/models/hold.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, DateTime, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class SeatHold(Base):
    __tablename__ = "seat_holds"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    hold_token = Column(String(36), unique=True, nullable=False, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    ticket_match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("ticket_matches.id"), nullable=False)
    ticket_type_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("ticket_types.id"), nullable=False)
    quantity = Column(Integer, nullable=False, default=1)
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
    expires_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=False)

    ticket_match = relationship("TicketMatch", back_populates="holds")
    ticket_type = relationship("TicketType", back_populates="holds")

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(**kwargs)
        if not self.expires_at:
            self.expires_at = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=10)

    def is_expired(self):
        return datetime.utcnow() > self.expires_at

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, DateTime, Boolean, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base
import uuid

class Match(Base):
    __tablename__ = "matches"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    home_team = Column(String, index=True)
    away_team = Column(String, index=True)
    home_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    away_score = Column(Integer, default=0)
    match_date = Column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    venue = Column(String)
    competition = Column(String)
    status = Column(String, default="upcoming")
    is_home_match = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    referee_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)

    events = relationship("MatchEvent", back_populates="match", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    report = relationship("MatchReport", back_populates="match", uselist=False)
    referee = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[referee_id])

FILE: backend\app\models\match_event.py

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, ForeignKey, DateTime
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, MatchEventType
import uuid

class MatchEvent(Base):
    __tablename__ = "match_events"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("matches.id"))
    event_type = Column(String)
    player_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("players.id"))
    related_player_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("players.id"), nullable=True)
    minute = Column(Integer)
    minute_added = Column(Integer, default=0)
    description = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    match = relationship("Match", back_populates="events")
    player = relationship("Player", foreign_keys=[player_id])
    related_player = relationship("Player", foreign_keys=[related_player_id])

# app/models/order.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Numeric, DateTime, ForeignKey, Text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class Order(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "orders"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    order_number = Column(String(36), unique=True, nullable=False, default=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    user_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    customer_email = Column(String(120), nullable=False)
    customer_name = Column(String(120))
    customer_phone = Column(String(20))
    total_amount = Column(Numeric(10,2), nullable=False)
    payment_status = Column(String(20), default='pending')
    payment_method = Column(String(50))
    payment_reference = Column(String(100))
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="orders")
    items = relationship("OrderItem", back_populates="order", lazy="dynamic")

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'order_number': self.order_number,
            'customer_email': self.customer_email,
            'customer_name': self.customer_name,
            'total_amount': float(self.total_amount),
            'payment_status': self.payment_status,
            'created_at': self.created_at.isoformat() if self.created_at else None,
            'items': [item.to_dict() for item in self.items]
        }

# app/models/order_item.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, Numeric, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class OrderItem(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "order_items"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    order_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("orders.id"), nullable=False)
    ticket_type_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("ticket_types.id"), nullable=False)
    quantity = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    unit_price = Column(Numeric(10,2), nullable=False)

    order = relationship("Order", back_populates="items")
    ticket_type = relationship("TicketType", back_populates="order_items")
    tickets = relationship("Ticket", back_populates="order_item", lazy="dynamic")

    def to_dict(self):
        match_name = None
        match_id = None
        if self.ticket_type and self.ticket_type.ticket_match:
            match = self.ticket_type.ticket_match
            match_name = f"{match.home_team} vs {match.away_team}"
            match_id = match.id

        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'ticket_type': self.ticket_type.name if self.ticket_type else None,
            'ticket_type_id': self.ticket_type_id,
            'quantity': self.quantity,
            'unit_price': float(self.unit_price),
            'match_id': match_id,
            'match_name': match_name,
            'tickets': [t.to_dict() for t in self.tickets]
        }

    )

FILE: backend\app\models\ticket.py
====================================================================================================

# app/models/ticket.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Boolean, DateTime, ForeignKey, Text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid
import qrcode
import io
import base64

class Ticket(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "tickets"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    ticket_number = Column(String(36), unique=True, nullable=False, index=True)
    order_item_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("order_items.id"), nullable=False)
    ticket_match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("ticket_matches.id"), nullable=False)
    ticket_type_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("ticket_types.id"), nullable=False)
    qr_code_data = Column(Text)
    is_used = Column(Boolean, default=False)
    used_at = Column(DateTime)
    verified_by = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())

    ticket_match = relationship("TicketMatch", back_populates="tickets")
    ticket_type = relationship("TicketType", back_populates="tickets")
    order_item = relationship("OrderItem", back_populates="tickets")

    def generate_qr_code(self):
        qr = qrcode.QRCode(
            version=1,
            error_correction=qrcode.constants.ERROR_CORRECT_L,
            box_size=10,
            border=4,
        )
        data = f"FAZ-TICKET:{self.ticket_number}:{self.ticket_match_id}:{self.ticket_type_id}"
        qr.add_data(data)
        qr.make(fit=True)

        img = qr.make_image(fill_color="black", back_color="white")
        buffered = io.BytesIO()
        img.save(buffered, "PNG")
        img_str = base64.b64encode(buffered.getvalue()).decode()
        self.qr_code_data = img_str
        return img_str

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'ticket_number': self.ticket_number,
            'ticket_match_id': self.ticket_match_id,
            'ticket_type': self.ticket_type.name if self.ticket_type else None,
            'qr_code': self.qr_code_data,
            'is_used': self.is_used,
            'used_at': self.used_at.isoformat() if self.used_at else None,
            'match': self.ticket_match.to_dict() if self.ticket_match else None
        }


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\models\ticket_match.py
====================================================================================================

# app/models/ticket_match.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, DateTime, Text, Boolean, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
from app.database import db
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

class TicketMatch(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "ticket_matches"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    home_team = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    away_team = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    match_type = Column(String(20), nullable=False)   # 'national' or 'club'
    club_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("clubs.id"), nullable=True)
    venue = Column(String(200), nullable=False)
    date_time = Column(DateTime, nullable=False)
    description = Column(Text)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now())
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now())
    created_by = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("users.id"))

    club = relationship("Club", back_populates="ticket_matches")
    ticket_types = relationship("TicketType", back_populates="ticket_match", lazy="dynamic", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    tickets = relationship("Ticket", back_populates="ticket_match", lazy="dynamic")
    holds = relationship("SeatHold", back_populates="ticket_match")

    def to_dict(self):
        from app.models.ticket_type import TicketType
        from app.models.hold import SeatHold
        from sqlalchemy import func

        total_capacity = sum(tt.capacity for tt in self.ticket_types)
        total_sold = sum(tt.tickets_sold for tt in self.ticket_types)

        holds = db.session.query(
            SeatHold.ticket_type_id,
            func.sum(SeatHold.quantity).label('held')
        ).filter(
            SeatHold.ticket_match_id == self.id,
            SeatHold.expires_at > datetime.utcnow()
        ).group_by(SeatHold.ticket_type_id).all()
        held_map = {h.ticket_type_id: h.held for h in holds}

        for tt in self.ticket_types:
            tt.held = held_map.get(tt.id, 0)
            tt.dynamic_available = tt.capacity - tt.tickets_sold - tt.held

        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'home_team': self.home_team,
            'away_team': self.away_team,
            'match_type': self.match_type,
            'club_id': self.club_id,
            'venue': self.venue,
            'date_time': self.date_time.isoformat() if self.date_time else None,
            'description': self.description,
            'is_active': self.is_active,
            'ticket_types': [tt.to_dict() for tt in self.ticket_types],
            'total_capacity': total_capacity,
            'total_tickets_sold': total_sold,
            'total_available': total_capacity - total_sold - sum(held_map.values())
        }


====================================================================================================
FILE: backend\app\models\ticket_type.py
====================================================================================================

# app/models/ticket_type.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Integer, Numeric, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class TicketType(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "ticket_types"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    ticket_match_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("ticket_matches.id"), nullable=False)
    name = Column(String(50), nullable=False)
    price = Column(Numeric(10,2), nullable=False)
    capacity = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    tickets_sold = Column(Integer, default=0)

    ticket_match = relationship("TicketMatch", back_populates="ticket_types")
    tickets = relationship("Ticket", back_populates="ticket_type", lazy="dynamic")
    order_items = relationship("OrderItem", back_populates="ticket_type")
    holds = relationship("SeatHold", back_populates="ticket_type")

    def to_dict(self):
        return {
            'id': self.id,
            'name': self.name,
            'price': float(self.price),
            'capacity': self.capacity,
            'tickets_sold': self.tickets_sold,
            'available': self.capacity - self.tickets_sold - getattr(self, 'held', 0),
            'held': getattr(self, 'held', 0),
            'dynamic_available': self.capacity - self.tickets_sold - getattr(self, 'held', 0)
        }

# app/models/user.py
from sqlalchemy import Column, String, Boolean, DateTime, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
from sqlalchemy.sql import func
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from app.models._base import Base, ToDictMixin
import uuid

class User(Base, ToDictMixin):
    __tablename__ = "users"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    email = Column(String, unique=True, index=True)
    username = Column(String, unique=True, index=True, nullable=True)
    hashed_password = Column(String)
    full_name = Column(String)
    role = Column(String, default='viewer')  # store as plain string
    club_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), ForeignKey("clubs.id"), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())

    club = relationship("Club", back_populates="users")
    orders = relationship("Order", back_populates="user")

    @property
    def roles(self):
        return [self.role] if self.role else []

    def has_role(self, role_name):
        return self.role == role_name

import uuid

class Video(Base):
    __tablename__ = "videos"
    id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4)
    title = Column(String, index=True, nullable=False)
    youtube_url = Column(String, nullable=False)
    youtube_id = Column(String, nullable=False)
    thumbnail_url = Column(String, nullable=True)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    duration = Column(String, nullable=True)
    views = Column(Integer, default=0)
    published_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    created_at = Column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now())
    is_published = Column(Boolean, default=True)

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import User, UserRole, News, Match, Team, Player
from app.schemas import UserCreate, NewsCreate, MatchCreate, TeamCreate, PlayerCreate
from app.auth import admin_required, get_current_user, get_password_hash
from datetime import datetime

bp = Blueprint('admin', __name__)

# ---------- User Management (Admin only) ----------
@bp.route('/users', methods=['GET'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def get_all_users():
    users = User.query.all()
    return jsonify([u.to_dict() for u in users])  # assuming you have to_dict, or use schemas

@bp.route('/users', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def create_user():
    data = request.get_json()
    # validate with schema
    # For simplicity we'll just use data directly
    existing = User.query.filter((User.username == data['username']) | (User.email == data['email'])).first()
    if existing:
        abort(400, "Username or email exists")
    hashed = get_password_hash(data['password'])
    user = User(
        username=data['username'],
        email=data['email'],
        hashed_password=hashed,
        full_name=data['full_name'],
        role=data.get('role', UserRole.VIEWER.value),
        club_id=data.get('club_id'),
        is_active=True
    )
    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(user.id)}), 201

@bp.route('/users/<user_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def delete_user(user_id):
    user = User.query.get(user_id)
    if not user:
        abort(404)
    current = get_current_user()
    if user.id == current.id:
        abort(400, "Cannot delete yourself")
    db.session.delete(user)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "User deleted"})

@bp.route('/news', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
def create_news():
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in [UserRole.ADMIN.value, UserRole.MEDIA_OFFICER.value]:
        abort(403, "Not authorized")
    data = request.get_json()
    slug = data['title'].lower().replace(" ", "-")
    news = News(
        title=data['title'],
        content=data['content'],
        excerpt=data['excerpt'],
        featured_image=data.get('featured_image'),
        slug=slug,
        author_id=user.id,
        is_published=True
    )
    db.session.add(news)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(news.id)}), 201

@jwt_required()
def update_news(news_id):
    user = get_current_user()
    if user.role not in [UserRole.ADMIN.value, UserRole.MEDIA_OFFICER.value]:
        abort(403)
    news = News.query.get(news_id)
    if not news:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    news.title = data.get('title', news.title)
    news.content = data.get('content', news.content)
    news.excerpt = data.get('excerpt', news.excerpt)
    news.featured_image = data.get('featured_image', news.featured_image)
    news.updated_at = datetime.utcnow()
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"message": "Updated"})

import csv, io, json
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required
from app.database import db
from app.models import Team, Player, Match, MatchEvent, UserRole
from app.auth import get_current_user, admin_required
from datetime import datetime

bp = Blueprint('bulk_import', __name__)

@bp.route('/teams', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def import_teams():
    file = request.files.get('file')
    if not file:
        abort(400, "No file")
    content = file.read().decode('utf-8')
    try:
        reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(content))
        count = 0
        for row in reader:
            team = Team(
                name=row['name'],
                short_name=row.get('short_name', ''),
                founded=int(row['founded']),
                stadium=row['stadium'],
                coach=row['coach'],
                description=row.get('description', '')
            )
            db.session.add(team)
            count += 1
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({"success": True, "rows_imported": count})
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "rows_imported": 0, "errors": str(e)}), 400

@bp.route('/players', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def import_players():
    file = request.files.get('file')
    if not file:
        abort(400)
    content = file.read().decode('utf-8')
    try:
        reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(content))
        count = 0
        for row in reader:
            team = Team.query.filter_by(name=row['team_name']).first()
            if not team:
                continue
            player = Player(
                name=row['name'],
                position=row['position'],
                jersey_number=int(row['jersey_number']),
                date_of_birth=datetime.strptime(row['date_of_birth'], '%Y-%m-%d').date(),
                nationality=row['nationality'],
                team_id=team.id,
                goals=int(row.get('goals', 0)),
                assists=int(row.get('assists', 0)),
                appearances=int(row.get('appearances', 0))
            )
            db.session.add(player)
            count += 1
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({"success": True, "rows_imported": count})
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "rows_imported": 0, "errors": str(e)}), 400

@bp.route('/matches', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def import_matches():
    file = request.files.get('file')
    if not file:
        abort(400)
    content = file.read().decode('utf-8')
    try:
        data = json.loads(content)
        count = 0
        for item in data:
            home = Team.query.filter_by(name=item['home_team']).first()
            away = Team.query.filter_by(name=item['away_team']).first()
            if not home or not away:
                continue
            match = Match(
                home_team=home.name,
                away_team=away.name,
                home_score=item['home_score'],
                away_score=item['away_score'],
                match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(item['match_date']),
                venue=item['venue'],
                competition=item.get('competition', 'Super League'),
                status=item.get('status', 'finished')
            )
            db.session.add(match)
            db.session.flush()
            for evt in item.get('events', []):
                player = Player.query.filter_by(name=evt['player_name']).first()
                if not player:
                    continue
                assist_player = None
                if evt.get('assist_player'):
                    assist_player = Player.query.filter_by(name=evt['assist_player']).first()
                event = MatchEvent(
                    match_id=match.id,
                    event_type=evt['event_type'],
                    player_id=player.id,
                    related_player_id=assist_player.id if assist_player else None,
                    minute=evt['minute'],
                    minute_added=evt.get('minute_added', 0)
                )
                db.session.add(event)
            count += 1
        db.session.commit()
        return jsonify({"success": True, "rows_imported": count})
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({"success": False, "rows_imported": 0, "errors": str(e)}), 400

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import Competition, CompetitionSeason, League, LeagueStanding
from app.schemas import CompetitionCreate, CompetitionSeasonCreate
from app.auth import admin_required, get_current_user
import uuid

bp = Blueprint('competitions', __name__)

@bp.route('/', methods=['GET'])
def get_competitions():
    gender = request.args.get('gender')
    competition_type = request.args.get('competition_type')
    query = Competition.query.filter_by(is_archived=False)
    if gender:
        query = query.filter_by(gender=gender)
    if competition_type:
        query = query.filter_by(competition_type=competition_type)
    competitions = query.all()
    return jsonify([c.to_dict() for c in competitions])

@bp.route('/seasons', methods=['GET'])
def get_seasons():
    seasons = db.session.query(Competition.season).distinct().order_by(Competition.season.desc()).all()
    seasons_list = [s[0] for s in seasons if s[0]]
    # also from league_standings
    league_seasons = db.session.query(LeagueStanding.season).distinct().order_by(LeagueStanding.season.desc()).all()
    for s in league_seasons:
        if s[0] and s[0] not in seasons_list:
            seasons_list.append(s[0])
    seasons_list.sort(reverse=True)
    return jsonify(seasons_list)

@bp.route('/<competition_id>', methods=['GET'])
def get_competition_by_id(competition_id):
    try:
        comp_id = uuid.UUID(competition_id)
    except:
        abort(400, "Invalid competition ID format")
    competition = Competition.query.get(comp_id)
    if not competition:
        abort(404)
    season = request.args.get('season')
    target_season = season or competition.season
    # get standings
    league = League.query.filter_by(name=competition.name, season=target_season).first()
    standings = []
    if league:
        standings = LeagueStanding.query.filter_by(league_id=league.id, season=target_season)\
            .order_by(LeagueStanding.points.desc()).all()
    seasons_history = CompetitionSeason.query.filter_by(competition_id=comp_id).order_by(CompetitionSeason.season.desc()).all()
    return jsonify({
        "competition": competition.to_dict(),
        "standings": [s.to_dict() for s in standings],
        "current_season": target_season,
        "history": [s.to_dict() for s in seasons_history]
    })

@bp.route('/<competition_id>/standings', methods=['GET'])
def get_competition_standings(competition_id):
    try:
        comp_id = uuid.UUID(competition_id)
    except:
        abort(400)
    competition = Competition.query.get(comp_id)
    if not competition:
        abort(404)
    season = request.args.get('season')
    if not season:
        abort(400, "season required")
    league = League.query.filter_by(name=competition.name, season=season).first()
    if not league:
        # try fuzzy match
        league = League.query.filter(League.name.ilike(f"%{competition.name.split()[0]}%"), League.season==season).first()
    if league:
        standings = LeagueStanding.query.filter_by(league_id=league.id, season=season)\
            .order_by(LeagueStanding.points.desc()).all()
        return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in standings])
    return jsonify([])

@bp.route('/<competition_id>/seasons', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def add_competition_season(competition_id):
    try:
        comp_id = uuid.UUID(competition_id)
    except:
        abort(400)
    competition = Competition.query.get(comp_id)
    if not competition:
        abort(404)
    data = request.get_json()
    existing = CompetitionSeason.query.filter_by(competition_id=comp_id, season=data['season']).first()
    if existing:
        abort(400, "Season already exists")
    season = CompetitionSeason(
        competition_id=comp_id,
        season=data['season'],
        champion_id=data.get('champion_id'),
        runner_up_id=data.get('runner_up_id'),
        started_at=data.get('started_at'),
        ended_at=data.get('ended_at')
    )
    db.session.add(season)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({"id": str(season.id)}), 201

from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, abort
from flask_jwt_extended import jwt_required, get_jwt_identity
from app.database import db
from app.models import (
    Competition, CompetitionSeason, Stage, Group,
    CompetitionMatch, Standing, QualificationRule,
    Team, User
)
from app.auth import get_current_user, admin_required
from sqlalchemy.orm import joinedload
from datetime import datetime
import uuid

bp = Blueprint('competitions_management', __name__, url_prefix='/competitions-management')

# ---------- Helper: Recalculate standings for a season or group ----------
def recalc_standings(season_id=None, group_id=None):
    """Recalculate standings for a given season or group."""
    matches = CompetitionMatch.query
    if season_id:
        matches = matches.filter_by(season_id=season_id)
    if group_id:
        matches = matches.filter_by(group_id=group_id)
    matches = matches.filter_by(status='finished').all()

    # Get teams involved
    if season_id:
        standings_q = Standing.query.filter_by(season_id=season_id)
    elif group_id:
        standings_q = Standing.query.filter_by(group_id=group_id)
    else:
        return

    standings = {s.team_id: s for s in standings_q.all()}

    # Reset stats
    for s in standings.values():
        s.played = 0
        s.won = 0
        s.drawn = 0
        s.lost = 0
        s.goals_for = 0
        s.goals_against = 0
        s.points = 0

    for match in matches:
        home_team_id = match.home_team_id
        away_team_id = match.away_team_id
        hs = match.home_score
        as_ = match.away_score

        if home_team_id not in standings:
            s = Standing(
                team_id=home_team_id,
                season_id=season_id,
                group_id=group_id
            )
            db.session.add(s)
            standings[home_team_id] = s
        if away_team_id not in standings:
            s = Standing(
                team_id=away_team_id,
                season_id=season_id,
                group_id=group_id
            )
            db.session.add(s)
            standings[away_team_id] = s

        home_stand = standings[home_team_id]
        away_stand = standings[away_team_id]

        home_stand.played += 1
        away_stand.played += 1
        home_stand.goals_for += hs
        home_stand.goals_against += as_
        away_stand.goals_for += as_
        away_stand.goals_against += hs

        if hs > as_:
            home_stand.won += 1
            home_stand.points += 3
            away_stand.lost += 1
        elif hs < as_:
            away_stand.won += 1
            away_stand.points += 3
            home_stand.lost += 1
        else:
            home_stand.drawn += 1
            home_stand.points += 1
            away_stand.drawn += 1
            away_stand.points += 1

    db.session.commit()

# ==================== COMPETITIONS ====================
@bp.route('/competitions', methods=['POST'])

@bp.route('/competitions/<competition_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def update_competition(competition_id):
    comp = Competition.query.get_or_404(competition_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    for field in ['name', 'competition_type', 'gender', 'age_group', 'scope', 'description', 'logo_url', 'is_active']:
        if field in data:
            setattr(comp, field, data[field])
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(comp.to_dict()), 200

    comp.is_active = False
    db.session.commit()

@bp.route('/competitions', methods=['GET'])

    query = Competition.query

        query = query.filter_by(is_active=True)

@bp.route('/competitions/<competition_id>', methods=['GET'])

@bp.route('/competitions/<competition_id>/seasons', methods=['POST'])

@admin_required
def create_season(competition_id):
    comp = Competition.query.get_or_404(competition_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    season = CompetitionSeason(
        competition_id=comp.id,
        name=data['name'],
        start_date=datetime.strptime(data['start_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date() if data.get('start_date') else None,
        end_date=datetime.strptime(data['end_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date() if data.get('end_date') else None,
        status=data.get('status', 'upcoming'),
        is_current=data.get('is_current', False)
    )
    db.session.add(season)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(season.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/seasons/<season_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def update_season(season_id):
    season = CompetitionSeason.query.get_or_404(season_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    for field in ['name', 'status', 'is_current']:
        if field in data:
            setattr(season, field, data[field])
    if 'start_date' in data:
        season.start_date = datetime.strptime(data['start_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
    if 'end_date' in data:
        season.end_date = datetime.strptime(data['end_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(season.to_dict()), 200

# ==================== MATCHES ====================
@bp.route('/stages/<stage_id>/matches', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def create_match(stage_id):
    stage = Stage.query.get_or_404(stage_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    home_team = Team.query.get(data['home_team_id'])
    away_team = Team.query.get(data['away_team_id'])
    if not home_team or not away_team:
        abort(400, "Team not found")

    match = CompetitionMatch(
        season_id=stage.season_id,
        stage_id=stage.id,
        group_id=data.get('group_id'),
        home_team_id=data['home_team_id'],
        away_team_id=data['away_team_id'],
        match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['match_date']),
        venue=data.get('venue'),
        is_ticketed=data.get('is_ticketed', False)
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['PUT'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def update_match(match_id):
    match = CompetitionMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    data = request.get_json()
    for field in ['match_date', 'venue', 'home_score', 'away_score', 'status', 'is_ticketed']:
        if field in data:
            if field == 'match_date':
                match.match_date = datetime.fromisoformat(data[field])
            else:
                setattr(match, field, data[field])

    if data.get('status') == 'finished' and match.status != 'finished':
        db.session.commit()
        if match.group_id:
            recalc_standings(group_id=match.group_id)
        else:
            recalc_standings(season_id=match.season_id)
    else:
        db.session.commit()

    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 200

@bp.route('/matches/<match_id>', methods=['DELETE'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def delete_match(match_id):
    match = CompetitionMatch.query.get_or_404(match_id)
    db.session.delete(match)
    db.session.commit()
    return jsonify({'message': 'Match deleted'}), 200

    stage = Stage.query.get_or_404(stage_id)
    matches = stage.matches
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

# ==================== STANDINGS ====================
@bp.route('/groups/<group_id>/standings', methods=['GET'])
def get_group_standings(group_id):
    group = Group.query.get_or_404(group_id)
    standings = group.standings.order_by(Standing.points.desc(), (Standing.goals_for - Standing.goals_against).desc(), Standing.goals_for.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in standings]), 200

@bp.route('/seasons/<season_id>/standings', methods=['GET'])
def get_season_standings(season_id):
    season = CompetitionSeason.query.get_or_404(season_id)
    # Check if any stage has groups
    groups = Group.query.join(Stage).filter(Stage.season_id == season_id).all()
    if groups:
        result = {}
        for g in groups:
            standings = Standing.query.filter_by(group_id=g.id)\
                .order_by(Standing.points.desc(), (Standing.goals_for - Standing.goals_against).desc(), Standing.goals_for.desc()).all()
            result[g.name] = [s.to_dict() for s in standings]
        return jsonify(result), 200
    else:
        # No groups, return single table
        standings = Standing.query.filter_by(season_id=season_id)\
            .order_by(Standing.points.desc(), (Standing.goals_for - Standing.goals_against).desc(), Standing.goals_for.desc()).all()
        return jsonify([s.to_dict() for s in standings]), 200

# ==================== FRIENDLIES ====================
@bp.route('/friendlies', methods=['POST'])
@jwt_required()
@admin_required
def create_friendly():
    data = request.get_json()
    home_team = Team.query.get(data['home_team_id'])
    away_team = Team.query.get(data['away_team_id'])
    if not home_team or not away_team:
        abort(400, "Team not found")

    competition = None
    if data.get('series_name'):
        series_name = data['series_name']
        competition = Competition.query.filter_by(name=series_name, competition_type='friendly_series').first()
        if not competition:
            competition = Competition(
                name=series_name,
                competition_type='friendly_series',
                gender=data.get('gender', 'mixed'),
                age_group=data.get('age_group'),
                scope='international',
                is_active=True
            )
            db.session.add(competition)
            db.session.flush()

    season = None
    if competition:
        season_name = data.get('season_name', f"Friendly Series {datetime.now().year}")
        season = CompetitionSeason.query.filter_by(competition_id=competition.id, name=season_name).first()
        if not season:
            season = CompetitionSeason(
                competition_id=competition.id,
                name=season_name,
                start_date=datetime.now().date(),
                status='active',
                is_current=True
            )
            db.session.add(season)
            db.session.flush()

    stage = None
    if season:
        stage = Stage.query.filter_by(season_id=season.id, name='Friendlies').first()
        if not stage:
            stage = Stage(
                season_id=season.id,
                name='Friendlies',
                stage_order=0,
                is_knockout=False,
                is_group_stage=False
            )
            db.session.add(stage)
            db.session.flush()

    match = CompetitionMatch(
        season_id=season.id if season else None,
        stage_id=stage.id if stage else None,
        home_team_id=data['home_team_id'],
        away_team_id=data['away_team_id'],
        match_date=datetime.fromisoformat(data['match_date']),
        venue=data.get('venue'),
        is_ticketed=data.get('is_ticketed', False),
        status='scheduled'
    )
    db.session.add(match)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify(match.to_dict()), 201

@bp.route('/friendlies', methods=['GET'])
def get_friendlies():
    matches = CompetitionMatch.query.join(Stage).join(CompetitionSeason).join(Competition).filter(
        Competition.competition_type == 'friendly_series'
    ).order_by(CompetitionMatch.match_date.desc()).all()
    return jsonify([m.to_dict() for m in matches]), 200

FILE: backend\app\routes\competitions_old.py

# backend/app/routes/competitions.py
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, joinedload
from typing import List, Optional
from uuid import UUID
from .. import database, models, schemas, auth

router = APIRouter()

@router.get("/")
async def get_competitions(
    gender: Optional[str] = None,
    season: Optional[str] = None,
    competition_type: Optional[str] = None,
    include_archived: bool = False,
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Get all competitions with optional filters"""
    query = db.query(models.Competition).options(
        joinedload(models.Competition.current_champion),
        joinedload(models.Competition.seasons)
    )
    
    if gender:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.gender == gender)
    if competition_type:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.competition_type == competition_type)
    if season:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.season == season)
    if not include_archived:
        query = query.filter(models.Competition.is_archived == False)
    
    return query.all()

@router.get("/seasons")
async def get_available_seasons(db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)):
    """Get all unique seasons from competitions"""
    seasons = db.query(models.Competition.season).distinct().order_by(models.Competition.season.desc()).all()
    return [s[0] for s in seasons]

@router.get("/{competition_id}")
async def get_competition_detail(
    competition_id: str,
    season: Optional[str] = None,
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Get detailed competition info including standings for a specific season"""
    try:
        comp_uuid = UUID(competition_id)
    except ValueError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid competition ID format")
    
    competition = db.query(models.Competition).filter(
        models.Competition.id == comp_uuid
    ).first()
    
    if not competition:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Competition not found")
    
    # Get standings for the specified season
    standings = []
    if season:
        # Find the league associated with this competition
        league = db.query(models.League).filter(
            models.League.name == competition.name,
            models.League.season == season
        ).first()
        
        if league:
            standings = db.query(models.LeagueStanding).options(
                joinedload(models.LeagueStanding.team)
            ).filter(
                models.LeagueStanding.league_id == league.id,
                models.LeagueStanding.season == season
            ).order_by(
                models.LeagueStanding.points.desc(),
                (models.LeagueStanding.goals_for - models.LeagueStanding.goals_against).desc(),
                models.LeagueStanding.goals_for.desc()
            ).all()
    
    # Get historical champions
    seasons_history = db.query(models.CompetitionSeason).filter(
        models.CompetitionSeason.competition_id == comp_uuid
    ).order_by(models.CompetitionSeason.season.desc()).all()
    
    return {
        "competition": competition,
        "standings": standings,
        "season": season,
        "history": seasons_history
    }

@router.get("/{competition_id}/history")
async def get_competition_history(
    competition_id: str,
    limit: int = 10,
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Get historical champions and runners-up for a competition"""
    try:
        comp_uuid = UUID(competition_id)
    except ValueError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid competition ID format")
    
    history = db.query(models.CompetitionSeason).options(
        joinedload(models.CompetitionSeason.champion),
        joinedload(models.CompetitionSeason.runner_up)
    ).filter(
        models.CompetitionSeason.competition_id == comp_uuid
    ).order_by(
        models.CompetitionSeason.season.desc()
    ).limit(limit).all()
    
    return history

@router.post("/", response_model=schemas.CompetitionOut)
async def create_competition(
    competition: schemas.CompetitionCreate,
    current_user: models.User = Depends(auth.get_current_user),
    db: Session = Depends(database.get_db)
):
    """Create a new competition (admin only)"""
    if current_user.role != models.UserRole.ADMIN:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Admin only")
    
    db_competition = models.Competition(**competition.dict())
    db.add(db_competition)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(db_competition)
    return db_competition
