export const mediaAPI = {
  // Videos
  getVideos: (params) => api.get('/media/videos', { params }),
  getVideo: (id) => api.get(`/media/videos/${id}`),
  searchVideos: (query) => api.get('/media/videos/search', { params: { q: query } }),
  createVideo: (data) => api.post('/media/videos', data),
  deleteVideo: (id) => api.delete(`/media/videos/${id}`),
  
  // Galleries
  getGalleries: (params) => api.get('/media/galleries', { params }),
  getGallery: (id) => api.get(`/media/galleries/${id}`),
  searchGalleries: (query) => api.get('/media/galleries/search', { params: { q: query } }),
  createGallery: (data) => api.post('/media/galleries', data),
  addGalleryImage: (galleryId, data) => api.post(`/media/galleries/${galleryId}/images`, data),
  deleteGallery: (id) => api.delete(`/media/galleries/${id}`),
  deleteGalleryImage: (galleryId, imageId) => api.delete(`/media/galleries/${galleryId}/images/${imageId}`),
};

// Update newsAPI with filter endpoints
export const newsAPI = {
  getAll: (params) => api.get('/news/', { params }),
  getFeatured: () => api.get('/news/featured'),
  getById: (id) => api.get(`/news/${id}`),
  search: (query) => api.get('/news/search', { params: { q: query } }),
  filterByDate: (month, year) => api.get('/news/filter', { params: { month, year } }),
  getYears: () => api.get('/news/years'),
  create: (data) => api.post('/admin/news', data),
  update: (id, data) => api.put(`/admin/news/${id}`, data),
  delete: (id) => api.delete(`/admin/news/${id}`),
};

// ---------- Matches API ----------
export const matchesAPI = {
  getAll: (params) => api.get('/matches/', { params }),
  getLatest: () => api.get('/matches/latest'),
  getUpcoming: () => api.get('/matches/upcoming'),
  getById: (id) => api.get(`/matches/${id}`),
  updateScore: (id, homeScore, awayScore, status) =>
    api.patch(`/matches/${id}/score`, null, { params: { home_score: homeScore, away_score: awayScore, status } }),
  updateStatus: (id, status) =>
    api.patch(`/matches/${id}/score`, null, { params: { home_score: 0, away_score: 0, status } }),
  create: (data) => api.post('/admin/matches', data),
  update: (id, data) => api.put(`/admin/matches/${id}`, data),
  delete: (id) => api.delete(`/admin/matches/${id}`),
  getByReferee: (refereeId) => api.get(`/matches/referee/${refereeId}`),
};

export const teamsAPI = {
  getAll: () => api.get('/teams/'),
  getById: (id) => api.get(`/teams/${id}`),
  create: (data) => api.post('/admin/teams', data),
  update: (id, data) => api.put(`/admin/teams/${id}`, data),
  delete: (id) => api.delete(`/admin/teams/${id}`),
};

// ---------- Competitions API ----------
export const competitionsAPI = {
  getAll: (params) => api.get('/competitions/', { params }),
  getById: (id, params) => api.get(`/competitions/${id}`, { params }),
  getSeasons: () => api.get('/competitions/seasons'),
  getStandings: (competitionId, season) => api.get(`/competitions/${competitionId}/standings`, { params: { season } }),
};


// ---------- Competition Management API (admin) ----------
export const competitionManagementAPI = {
  // Competitions
  getCompetitions: (params) => api.get('/competitions-management/competitions', { params }),
  getCompetition: (id) => api.get(`/competitions-management/competitions/${id}`),
  createCompetition: (data) => api.post('/competitions-management/competitions', data),
  updateCompetition: (id, data) => api.put(`/competitions-management/competitions/${id}`, data),
  deleteCompetition: (id) => api.delete(`/competitions-management/competitions/${id}`),

  // Seasons
  getSeasons: (competitionId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/competitions/${competitionId}/seasons`),
  createSeason: (competitionId, data) => api.post(`/competitions-management/competitions/${competitionId}/seasons`, data),
  updateSeason: (seasonId, data) => api.put(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}`, data),
  deleteSeason: (seasonId) => api.delete(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}`),

  // Stages
  getStages: (seasonId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/stages`),
  createStage: (seasonId, data) => api.post(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/stages`, data),
  updateStage: (stageId, data) => api.put(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}`, data),
  deleteStage: (stageId) => api.delete(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}`),

  // Groups
  getGroups: (stageId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}/groups`),
  createGroup: (stageId, data) => api.post(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}/groups`, data),
  updateGroup: (groupId, data) => api.put(`/competitions-management/groups/${groupId}`, data),
  deleteGroup: (groupId) => api.delete(`/competitions-management/groups/${groupId}`),

  // Matches (competition matches)
  getMatches: (seasonId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/matches`),
  createMatch: (stageId, data) => api.post(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}/matches`, data),
  updateMatch: (matchId, data) => api.put(`/competitions-management/matches/${matchId}`, data),
  deleteMatch: (matchId) => api.delete(`/competitions-management/matches/${matchId}`),

  // Standings
  getGroupStandings: (groupId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/groups/${groupId}/standings`),
  getSeasonStandings: (seasonId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/seasons/${seasonId}/standings`),

  // Qualification Rules
  createQualificationRule: (data) => api.post('/competitions-management/qualification-rules', data),
  updateQualificationRule: (ruleId, data) => api.put(`/competitions-management/qualification-rules/${ruleId}`, data),
  deleteQualificationRule: (ruleId) => api.delete(`/competitions-management/qualification-rules/${ruleId}`),
  getQualificationRules: (stageId) => api.get(`/competitions-management/stages/${stageId}/qualification-rules`),

  // Friendlies
  createFriendly: (data) => api.post('/competitions-management/friendlies', data),
};

// ---------- Shop API ----------
export const shopAPI = {
  // Categories
  getCategories: () => api.get('/shop/categories'),
  getProducts: (params) => api.get('/shop/products', { params }),
  getProduct: (id) => api.get(`/shop/products/${id}`),
  
  // Cart
  getCart: () => api.get('/shop/cart'),
  addToCart: (data) => api.post('/shop/cart/add', data),
  updateCartItem: (itemId, quantity) => api.put(`/shop/cart/update/${itemId}`, null, { params: { quantity } }),
  removeCartItem: (itemId) => api.delete(`/shop/cart/remove/${itemId}`),

  getOrder: (id) => api.get(`/shop/orders/${id}`),
  
  // Checkout & Payment
  checkout: (data) => api.post('/shop/checkout', data),
  initiatePayment: (data) => api.post('/shop/payment/initiate', data),
  checkPaymentStatus: (depositId) => api.get(`/shop/payment/status/${depositId}`),
  getPaymentProviders: () => api.get('/shop/payment/providers'),
};

FILE: frontend\src\components\CalendarExport.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FaCalendarPlus } from 'react-icons/fa';
import GlassCard from './GlassCard';

const CalendarExport = ({ teams }) => {
  const [showOptions, setShowOptions] = useState(false);
  const [selectedTeam, setSelectedTeam] = useState('');
  const [competition, setCompetition] = useState('');

  const exportUrl = `/api/matches/export/calendar?${selectedTeam ? `team_id=${selectedTeam}&` : ''}${competition ? `competition=${encodeURIComponent(competition)}` : ''}`;

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <button
        onClick={() => setShowOptions(!showOptions)}
        className="btn-secondary flex items-center gap-2"
      >
        <FaCalendarPlus /> Export Calendar
      </button>
      {showOptions && (
        <GlassCard className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-64 p-4 z-10">
          <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2">Filter matches</h4>
          <select
            className="glass w-full p-2 rounded mb-2"
            value={selectedTeam}
            onChange={(e) => setSelectedTeam(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="">All Teams</option>
            {teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
          </select>
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Competition (e.g., Super League)"
            className="glass w-full p-2 rounded mb-3"
            value={competition}
            onChange={(e) => setCompetition(e.target.value)}
          />
          <a
            href={exportUrl}
            download
            className="btn-primary block text-center text-sm"
            onClick={() => setShowOptions(false)}
          >
            Download .ics
          </a>
        </GlassCard>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default CalendarExport;

FILE: frontend\src\components\GlassCard.jsx

import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';

const GlassCard = ({ children, className, ...props }) => {
  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
      whileInView={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      viewport={{ once: true }}
      className={`glass p-6 rounded-2xl ${className}`}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </motion.div>
  );
};

export default GlassCard;

import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaTrophy, FaCalendarAlt, FaUsers } from 'react-icons/fa';

const Hero = () => {
  return (
    <div className="relative min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
      {/* Background Video/Image */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green/30 via-faz-orange/20 to-faz-red/30 mix-blend-overlay" />
        <img
          src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1522778119026-d647f0596c67?ixlib=rb-4.0.3"
          alt="Football Stadium"
          className="w-full h-full object-cover"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Animated particles */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0">
        {[...Array(20)].map((_, i) => (
          <motion.div
            key={i}
            className="absolute w-2 h-2 bg-white/30 rounded-full"
            initial={{
              x: Math.random() * window.innerWidth,
              y: Math.random() * window.innerHeight,
            }}
            animate={{
              y: [null, -30, 30, -30],
              opacity: [0.3, 1, 0.3],
            }}
            transition={{
              duration: Math.random() * 5 + 3,
              repeat: Infinity,
              repeatType: 'reverse',
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-10 text-center px-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8 }}
        >
          <motion.div
            animate={{ rotate: 360 }}
            transition={{ duration: 20, repeat: Infinity, ease: 'linear' }}
            className="w-24 h-24 mx-auto mb-8 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green to-faz-orange rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"

FILE: frontend\src\components\HeroCarousel.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import profileImage from '../assets/under 20 afcon champions.jpg';
import shepolopolo from '../assets/shepolopolo.jpg';
import chipolopolo from '../assets/chipolopolo.jpg';

const slides = [
  {
    image: profileImage,
    title: 'Chipolopolo Rising',
    subtitle: 'Pride of Zambia',
    cta: 'Support the Team',
    link: '/teams',
  },
  {
    image: shepolopolo,
    title: 'FAZ Super League',
    subtitle: 'The best of Zambian football',
    cta: 'View Fixtures',
    link: '/matches',
  },
  {
    image: chipolopolo,
    title: 'Grassroots Development',
    subtitle: 'Building tomorrow’s stars today',
    cta: 'Get Involved',
    link: '/about',
  },
];

const HeroCarousel = () => {
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setCurrentIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % slides.length);
    }, 5000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative h-screen w-full overflow-hidden">
      <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
        <motion.div
          key={currentIndex}
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 1 }}
          className="absolute inset-0"
          style={{
            backgroundImage: `url(${slides[currentIndex].image})`,
            backgroundSize: 'cover',
            backgroundPosition: 'center',
          }}
        >
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-black/50 via-black/30 to-black/70" />
          <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-center px-6">
            <motion.h1
              initial={{ y: 50, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.2 }}
              className="text-5xl md:text-7xl font-bold text-white drop-shadow-lg"
            >
              {slides[currentIndex].title}
            </motion.h1>
            <motion.p
              initial={{ y: 30, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ y: 0, opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.4 }}
              className="text-xl md:text-2xl text-white/90 mt-4"
            >
              {slides[currentIndex].subtitle}
            </motion.p>
            <motion.a
              href={slides[currentIndex].link}
              initial={{ scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
              transition={{ delay: 0.6 }}
              className="btn-primary mt-8 inline-block"
            >
              {slides[currentIndex].cta}
            </motion.a>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </AnimatePresence>

      {/* Dots indicator */}
      <div className="absolute bottom-8 left-0 right-0 flex justify-center gap-3 z-20">
        {slides.map((_, i) => (
          <button
            key={i}
            onClick={() => setCurrentIndex(i)}
            className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
              i === currentIndex ? 'bg-faz-orange w-6' : 'bg-white/50'
            }`}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default HeroCarousel;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\components\LiveScoreWidget.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import GlassCard from './GlassCard';
import { FaBroadcastTower, FaFutbol, FaFlagCheckered } from 'react-icons/fa';

const LiveScoreWidget = ({ matchId }) => {
  const [liveEvents, setLiveEvents] = useState([]);
  const [homeScore, setHomeScore] = useState(0);
  const [awayScore, setAwayScore] = useState(0);
  const [matchStatus, setMatchStatus] = useState('live');
  const [ws, setWs] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const socket = new WebSocket(`ws://localhost:8000/ws/live/${matchId}`);
    socket.onmessage = (event) => {
      const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
      if (data.type === 'event') {
        setLiveEvents(prev => [...prev, data.event]);
        if (data.event.event_type === 'goal') {
          // Update score – you need to know which team the player belongs to
          // For simplicity, we assume the backend sends team info. Here we'll just increment later via API fetch.
        }
      } else if (data.type === 'score_update') {
        setHomeScore(data.home_score);
        setAwayScore(data.away_score);
      } else if (data.type === 'match_status') {
        setMatchStatus(data.status);
      }
    };
    setWs(socket);
    return () => socket.close();
  }, [matchId]);

  return (
    <GlassCard className="p-4 mb-4">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
        <h3 className="font-bold flex items-center gap-2"><FaBroadcastTower className="text-faz-red animate-pulse" /> Live Updates</h3>
        <span className="text-sm glass px-2 py-1 rounded">{matchStatus.toUpperCase()}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex justify-between text-center mb-4">
        <div><p className="text-3xl font-bold">{homeScore}</p></div>
        <div><p className="text-xl">-</p></div>
        <div><p className="text-3xl font-bold">{awayScore}</p></div>
      </div>
      <div className="max-h-40 overflow-y-auto space-y-1 text-sm">
        {liveEvents.map((ev, idx) => (
          <div key={idx} className="glass p-1 px-2">
            {ev.minute}' – {ev.event_type === 'goal' ? '⚽ Goal' : ev.event_type} : {ev.player_name}
            {ev.assist_player && ` (assist: ${ev.assist_player})`}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </GlassCard>
  );
};

export default LiveScoreWidget;

import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaCalendar, FaMapMarker, FaTrophy } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

const MatchCard = ({ match, index }) => {
  const getStatusColor = () => {
    switch(match.status) {
      case 'live': return 'text-red-500 animate-pulse';
      case 'finished': return 'text-faz-green';
      default: return 'text-faz-orange';
    }
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -30 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay: index * 0.1 }}
      className="glass p-6 hover:bg-white/15 transition-all duration-300"
    >
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <FaTrophy className="text-faz-orange" />
          <span>{match.competition}</span>
        </div>
        <div className={`text-sm font-semibold ${getStatusColor()}`}>
          {match.status.toUpperCase()}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
        <div className="flex-1 text-right">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">{match.home_team}</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="text-3xl font-bold gradient-text">
          {match.status === 'upcoming' ? 'VS' : `${match.home_score} - ${match.away_score}`}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">{match.away_team}</h3>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm text-gray-300 pt-4 border-t border-white/10">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <FaCalendar className="text-faz-green" />
          <span>{format(new Date(match.match_date), 'MMM dd, yyyy')}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <FaMapMarker className="text-faz-orange" />
          <span>{match.venue}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
};

export default MatchCard;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\components\MatchResultForm.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import GlassCard from './GlassCard';
import { matchesAPI, matchEventsAPI, playersAPI } from '../api';
import { FaPlus, FaTrash } from 'react-icons/fa';

const MatchResultForm = ({ matchId, onComplete }) => {
  const [match, setMatch] = useState(null);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState([]);
  const [players, setPlayers] = useState([]);
  const [newEvent, setNewEvent] = useState({ event_type: 'goal', player_id: '', minute: '', assist_player_id: '' });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetch = async () => {
      try {
        const matchRes = await matchesAPI.getById(matchId);
        setMatch(matchRes.data);
        
        const eventsRes = await matchEventsAPI.getByMatch(matchId);
        setEvents(eventsRes.data);
        
        // Fetch all players (no filtering)
        const playersRes = await playersAPI.getAll({});
        setPlayers(playersRes.data);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        setError(err.message);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetch();
  }, [matchId]);

  const addEvent = async () => {
    if (!newEvent.player_id || !newEvent.minute) {
      alert('Please select player and minute');
      return;
    }
    try {
      const data = {
        match_id: matchId,
        event_type: newEvent.event_type,
        player_id: parseInt(newEvent.player_id),
        minute: parseInt(newEvent.minute),
        related_player_id: newEvent.assist_player_id ? parseInt(newEvent.assist_player_id) : null,
      };
      const res = await matchEventsAPI.create(data);
      setEvents([...events, res.data]);
      setNewEvent({ event_type: 'goal', player_id: '', minute: '', assist_player_id: '' });
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Failed to add event: ' + err.message);
    }
  };

  const deleteEvent = async (eventId) => {
    try {
      await matchEventsAPI.delete(eventId);
      setEvents(events.filter(e => e.id !== eventId));
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Failed to delete event: ' + err.message);
    }
  };

  const finishMatch = async () => {
    const homeScore = prompt(`Enter final score for ${match.home_team}:`, match.home_score || 0);
    const awayScore = prompt(`Enter final score for ${match.away_team}:`, match.away_score || 0);
    if (homeScore !== null && awayScore !== null) {
      try {
        await matchesAPI.updateScore(matchId, parseInt(homeScore), parseInt(awayScore), 'finished');
        onComplete();
      } catch (err) {
        alert('Failed to finish match: ' + err.message);
      }
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <GlassCard className="p-4 text-center">Loading match data...</GlassCard>;
  if (error) return <GlassCard className="p-4 text-center text-red-500">Error: {error}</GlassCard>;

  return (
    <GlassCard className="p-6 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{match?.home_team} vs {match?.away_team}</h2>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-6 text-center">
        <div><p className="text-3xl font-bold gradient-text">{match?.home_score || 0}</p></div>
        <div><p className="text-3xl font-bold gradient-text">{match?.away_score || 0}</p></div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3 mb-6">
        <select className="glass w-full p-2 rounded" value={newEvent.event_type} onChange={e => setNewEvent({...newEvent, event_type: e.target.value})}>
          <option value="goal">⚽ Goal</option>
          <option value="yellow_card">🟨 Yellow Card</option>
          <option value="red_card">🟥 Red Card</option>
        </select>

        <select className="glass w-full p-2 rounded" value={newEvent.player_id} onChange={e => setNewEvent({...newEvent, player_id: e.target.value})}>
          <option value="">Select player</option>
          {players.map(p => (
            <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
          ))}
        </select>

        {newEvent.event_type === 'goal' && (
          <select className="glass w-full p-2 rounded" value={newEvent.assist_player_id} onChange={e => setNewEvent({...newEvent, assist_player_id: e.target.value})}>
            <option value="">Assist by (optional)</option>
            {players.map(p => (
              <option key={`assist-${p.id}`} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        )}

        <input type="number" placeholder="Minute" className="glass w-full p-2 rounded" value={newEvent.minute} onChange={e => setNewEvent({...newEvent, minute: e.target.value})} />

        <button onClick={addEvent} className="btn-primary w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          <FaPlus /> Add Event
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 mb-6">
        {events.sort((a,b) => a.minute - b.minute).map(ev => {
          const player = players.find(p => p.id === ev.player_id);
          const assistPlayer = ev.related_player_id ? players.find(p => p.id === ev.related_player_id) : null;
          return (
            <div key={ev.id} className="glass p-2 text-sm flex justify-between items-center">
              <span>
                {ev.minute}' – {ev.event_type === 'goal' ? '⚽' : ev.event_type === 'yellow_card' ? '🟨' : '🟥'} {player?.name}
                {assistPlayer && ` (assist: ${assistPlayer.name})`}
              </span>
              <button onClick={() => deleteEvent(ev.id)} className="text-faz-red hover:text-faz-red/70">
                <FaTrash />
              </button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <button onClick={finishMatch} className="btn-secondary w-full">Finish Match</button>
    </GlassCard>
  );
};

export default MatchResultForm;

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

const Navbar = () => {

    { icon: FaYoutube, href: 'https://www.youtube.com/@FAZTVOfficial', label: 'YouTube' },
  ];

  // Helper to render dropdown content recursively (for desktop)
  const renderDropdownContent = (items, level = 1) => {
    return (
      <div className={`absolute ${level === 1 ? 'top-full left-0 mt-1 w-64' : 'left-full top-0 ml-1 w-64'} glass bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md rounded-xl shadow-2xl py-2 z-50`}>
        {items.map((item, idx) => (
          <div key={idx} className="relative group/sub">

              <>

                  <span className="text-sm font-medium">{item.name}</span>
                  <FaChevronRight size={12} className="text-gray-400" />
                </div>
                <div className="absolute left-full top-0 hidden group-hover/sub:block">
                  {renderDropdownContent(item.children, level + 1)}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <Link
                to={item.path}
                className="block px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors"
                onClick={() => closeDropdowns()}
              >
                {item.name}
              </Link>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    );
  };

  // Mobile: recursive accordion menu
  const MobileMenuItem = ({ item, level = 0 }) => {
    const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);
    const hasChildren = item.children && item.children.length > 0;
    return (
      <div className="border-b border-white/10 last:border-0">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center py-2 px-2">
          {item.path && !hasChildren ? (
            <Link
              to={item.path}
              className="flex-1 text-left py-1 text-white hover:text-faz-orange"
              onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
            >
              {item.name}
            </Link>
          ) : (
            <span className="flex-1 text-left py-1 font-medium">{item.name}</span>
          )}
          {hasChildren && (
            <button onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)} className="p-2">
              {expanded ? <FaChevronDown size={12} /> : <FaChevronRight size={12} />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {hasChildren && expanded && (
          <div className="ml-4 space-y-1">
            {item.children.map((child, idx) => (
              <MobileMenuItem key={idx} item={child} level={level + 1} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Top Bar */}
      <div className="fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-50 hidden md:block bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm border-b border-white/10">
        <div className="container mx-auto px-6 py-2">

              </div>
              {user ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-white/80">Welcome, {user.full_name || user.username}</span>
                  <button onClick={logout} className="glass px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-xs hover:bg-faz-red/20">Logout</button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <Link to="/login" className="hover:text-faz-orange flex items-center gap-1"><FaUserCircle size={12} /> Login</Link>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Navigation – background becomes solid when any dropdown is open */}
      <nav className={`fixed w-full z-40 transition-all duration-300 ${
        scrolled || isAnyDropdownOpen ? 'bg-transparent backdrop-blur-md' : 'bg-transparent'
      } py-2`} style={{ top: '36px' }}>
        <div className="container mx-auto px-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
            {/* Logo */}
            <Link to="/" className="flex items-center space-x-3 group">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green to-faz-orange rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                <img src={logoImage} alt="FAZ Logo" className="h-10 w-auto object-contain rounded-md" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-lg font-bold gradient-text">Football Association</h1>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-300">of Zambia</p>
              </div>
            </Link>

            {/* Desktop Mega Menu */}
            <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center space-x-1">
              {menuItems.map((item, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={idx}
                  className="relative"
                  onMouseEnter={() => handleMouseEnter(idx)}
                  onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
                >
                  {item.path ? (
                    <Link
                      to={item.path}
                      className={`px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-300 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 ${
                        location.pathname === item.path ? 'text-faz-orange' : 'text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {item.name}
                    </Link>
                  ) : (
                    <button className="px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:text-faz-orange transition-all duration-300 flex items-center gap-1">
                      {item.name} <FaChevronDown size={10} />
                    </button>
                  )}
                  {item.children && activeDropdown === idx && (
                    <div className="absolute top-full left-0 mt-1">
                      {renderDropdownContent(item.children)}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}

              {/* Cart Icon with Counter */}
              <Link to="/cart" className="relative ml-2">
                <div className="glass px-3 py-2 rounded-full hover:bg-white/20 transition flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FaShoppingCart />
                  {cartCount > 0 && (
                    <span className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 bg-faz-red text-white text-xs rounded-full w-5 h-5 flex items-center justify-center">
                      {cartCount > 99 ? '99+' : cartCount}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </Link>

              {/* Dashboard / Login shortcut (desktop) */}
              {user && (
                <Link to={getDashboardLink()} className="glass px-3 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold ml-2 hover:bg-white/20">
                  <FaUserCircle className="inline mr-1" /> Dashboard
                </Link>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Mobile menu toggle and Cart Icon */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 lg:hidden">
              <Link to="/cart" className="relative">
                <div className="glass p-2 rounded-full">
                  <FaShoppingCart />
                  {cartCount > 0 && (
                    <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-faz-red text-white text-xs rounded-full w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">
                      {cartCount > 99 ? '99+' : cartCount}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </Link>
              <button onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)} className="text-2xl focus:outline-none">
                {isOpen ? <FaTimes /> : <FaBars />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Mobile Menu (accordion) */}
          <AnimatePresence>
            {isOpen && (
              <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                className="lg:hidden mt-4 glass rounded-2xl p-4 max-h-[80vh] overflow-y-auto"
              >
                {menuItems.map((item, idx) => (
                  <MobileMenuItem key={idx} item={item} />
                ))}
                <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-white/10">
                  {user ? (
                    <>
                      <Link to={getDashboardLink()} className="block py-2 text-center glass rounded-lg mb-2" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}>Dashboard</Link>
                      <button onClick={() => { logout(); setIsOpen(false); }} className="block w-full py-2 text-center glass rounded-lg hover:bg-faz-red/20">Logout</button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <Link to="/login" className="block py-2 text-center glass rounded-lg" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}>Login</Link>
                  )}
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      </nav>
    </>
  );
};

export default Navbar;

FILE: frontend\src\components\NewsCard.jsx

import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaCalendar, FaEye, FaArrowRight } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const NewsCard = ({ news, index }) => {
  // Always show only excerpt, never expand inline
  const truncateText = (text, maxLength = 120) => {
    if (!text) return '';
    if (text.length <= maxLength) return text;
    return text.substring(0, maxLength) + '...';
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 30 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay: index * 0.1 }}
      whileHover={{ y: -10 }}
      className="glass-card overflow-hidden cursor-pointer group h-full flex flex-col"
    >
      <div className="relative h-48 overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
        <img
          src={news.featured_image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/400x300?text=FAZ+News'}
          alt={news.title}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform duration-500"
        />
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/70 to-transparent" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 glass px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs">
          {format(new Date(news.published_at), 'MMM dd, yyyy')}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="p-6 flex flex-col flex-grow">
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-3 group-hover:text-faz-orange transition-colors line-clamp-2">
          {news.title}
        </h3>
        
        <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm mb-4 line-clamp-3">
          {truncateText(news.excerpt || news.content, 120)}
        </p>
        
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm text-gray-400 mt-auto pt-3 border-t border-white/10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <FaCalendar className="text-faz-green" />
            <span>{format(new Date(news.published_at), 'MMM dd, yyyy')}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <FaEye className="text-faz-orange" />
            <span>{news.views} views</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <Link
          to={`/news/${news.id}`}
          className="mt-3 text-faz-green hover:text-faz-orange transition-colors text-sm flex items-center gap-1 group-hover:gap-2"
        >
          Read full article <FaArrowRight size={12} />
        </Link>
      </div>
    </motion.div>
  );
};

export default NewsCard;

FILE: frontend\src\components\ProtectedRoute.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';

const ProtectedRoute = ({ children, allowedRoles = [] }) => {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  if (loading) return <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">Loading...</div>;
  if (!user) return <Navigate to="/admin" />;
  if (allowedRoles.length && !allowedRoles.includes(user.role)) return <Navigate to="/" />;
  return children;
};

export default ProtectedRoute;

import React from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaTrophy, FaArrowUp, FaArrowDown } from 'react-icons/fa';

const TableRow = ({ entry, position, previousPosition }) => {
  const getPositionChange = () => {
    if (!previousPosition) return null;
    if (position < previousPosition) return <FaArrowUp className="text-faz-green" />;
    if (position > previousPosition) return <FaArrowDown className="text-faz-red" />;
    return null;
  };

  const getPositionIcon = () => {
    if (position === 1) return <FaTrophy className="text-yellow-500" />;
    if (position === 2) return <FaTrophy className="text-gray-400" />;
    if (position === 3) return <FaTrophy className="text-amber-600" />;
    return null;
  };

  return (
    <motion.tr
      initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
      transition={{ delay: position * 0.03 }}
      className="border-b border-white/10 hover:bg-white/5 transition-colors group"
    >
      <td className="py-4 px-4 font-bold">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {getPositionIcon()}
          <span>{position}</span>
          <span className="text-xs ml-1">{getPositionChange()}</span>
        </div>
      </td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 font-semibold group-hover:text-faz-orange transition-colors">
        {entry.team?.name || entry.team_name}
      </td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center">{entry.played}</td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center text-faz-green">{entry.won}</td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center text-gray-400">{entry.drawn}</td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center text-faz-red">{entry.lost}</td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_for}</td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_against}</td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center font-semibold">
        {entry.goals_for - entry.goals_against}
      </td>
      <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center font-bold text-faz-orange text-xl">
        {entry.points}
      </td>
    </motion.tr>
  );
};

export default TableRow;

FILE: frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx
====================================================================================================

// frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx
import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import { authAPI } from '../api';
import { jwtDecode } from 'jwt-decode';

const AuthContext = createContext();

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext);

export const AuthProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    const userStr = localStorage.getItem('user');
    if (token) {
      try {
        const decoded = jwtDecode(token);
        if (userStr) {
          const userData = JSON.parse(userStr);
          // Merge: use userData fields, but ensure role is set from decoded token if missing
          setUser({ ...userData, role: userData.role || decoded.role });
        } else {
          // Fallback: only username and role from token
          setUser({ username: decoded.sub, role: decoded.role });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        localStorage.removeItem('token');
        localStorage.removeItem('user');
        setUser(null);
      }
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }, []);

  const login = async (email, password) => {
    try {
      const response = await authAPI.login(email, password);
      const { access_token, user: userData } = response.data;
      localStorage.setItem('token', access_token);
      localStorage.setItem('user', JSON.stringify(userData));
      const decoded = jwtDecode(access_token);
      setUser({ ...userData, role: userData.role || decoded.role });
      return { success: true, user: userData };
    } catch (error) {
      throw error;
    }
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem('token');
    localStorage.removeItem('user');
    setUser(null);
    window.location.href = '/';
  };

  const hasRole = (role) => user?.role === role;
  const hasAnyRole = (roles) => roles.includes(user?.role);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, login, logout, loading, isAuthenticated: !!user, hasRole, hasAnyRole }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\context\CartContext.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { createContext, useState, useContext, useEffect } from 'react';
import { shopAPI } from '../api';

const CartContext = createContext();

export const useCart = () => useContext(CartContext);

export const CartProvider = ({ children }) => {
  const [cartCount, setCartCount] = useState(0);
  const [cartItems, setCartItems] = useState([]);
  const [cartSubtotal, setCartSubtotal] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const fetchCart = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await shopAPI.getCart();
      const items = res.data.items || [];
      const subtotal = res.data.subtotal || 0;
      const total = res.data.total || 0;
      setCartItems(items);
      setCartSubtotal(subtotal);
      const totalCount = items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
      setCartCount(totalCount);
      
      // Dispatch event for navbar to listen (for components not using context)
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('cartUpdated', { detail: { count: totalCount, items, subtotal, total } }));
      return { items, subtotal, total };
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching cart:', error);
      return { items: [], subtotal: 0, total: 0 };
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const addToCart = async (productId, quantity = 1, buyNow = false) => {
    try {
      await shopAPI.addToCart({ product_id: productId, quantity });
      // Wait a moment for the server to process
      setTimeout(() => {
        fetchCart();
      }, 100);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error adding to cart:', error);
      return false;
    }
  };

  const updateQuantity = async (itemId, quantity) => {
    try {
      await shopAPI.updateCartItem(itemId, quantity);
      await fetchCart();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error updating quantity:', error);
    }
  };

  const removeItem = async (itemId) => {
    try {
      await shopAPI.removeCartItem(itemId);
      await fetchCart();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error removing item:', error);
    }
  };

  const clearCart = async () => {
    for (const item of cartItems) {
      await shopAPI.removeCartItem(item.id);
    }
    await fetchCart();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCart();
    
    // Listen for storage events (for cross-tab updates)
    const handleStorageChange = (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'cart_updated') {
        fetchCart();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('storage', handleStorageChange);
    
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('storage', handleStorageChange);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <CartContext.Provider value={{
      cartCount,
      cartItems,
      cartSubtotal,
      loading,
      addToCart,
      updateQuantity,
      removeItem,
      clearCart,
      refreshCart: fetchCart
    }}>
      {children}
    </CartContext.Provider>
  );
};

FILE: frontend\src\pages\Admin.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import { authAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaUserPlus, FaSignInAlt, FaLock } from 'react-icons/fa';

const Admin = () => {
  const [isLogin, setIsLogin] = useState(true);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    username: '',
    email: '',
    password: '',
  });
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  const handleChange = (e) => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, [e.target.name]: e.target.value });
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setMessage('');
    
    try {
      if (isLogin) {
        const response = await authAPI.login(formData.username, formData.password);
        localStorage.setItem('token', response.data.access_token);
        setMessage('Login successful! Redirecting...');
        setTimeout(() => {
          window.location.href = '/admin/dashboard';
        }, 1500);
      } else {
        await authAPI.register(formData);
        setMessage('Registration successful! You can now login.');
        setTimeout(() => setIsLogin(true), 2000);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(error.response?.data?.detail || 'An error occurred');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="container mx-auto px-6 py-12 max-w-md">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
        >
          <GlassCard>
            <div className="text-center mb-8">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green to-faz-orange rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                {isLogin ? <FaSignInAlt className="text-white text-3xl" /> : <FaUserPlus className="text-white text-3xl" />}
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold gradient-text">
                {isLogin ? 'Admin Login' : 'Create Account'}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-gray-400 mt-2">
                {isLogin ? 'Access the FAZ administration panel' : 'Register as a new administrator'}
              </p>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-2">Username</label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <FaLock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    name="username"
                    value={formData.username}
                    onChange={handleChange}
                    required
                    className="w-full glass pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
                    placeholder="Enter username"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {!isLogin && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-2">Email</label>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    name="email"
                    value={formData.email}
                    onChange={handleChange}
                    required
                    className="w-full glass px-4 py-3 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
                    placeholder="Enter email"
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-2">Password</label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  name="password"
                  value={formData.password}
                  onChange={handleChange}
                  required
                  className="w-full glass px-4 py-3 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
                  placeholder="Enter password"
                />
              </div>

              {message && (
                <div className={`p-3 rounded-lg text-center ${message.includes('successful') ? 'bg-faz-green/20 text-faz-green' : 'bg-faz-red/20 text-faz-red'}`}>
                  {message}
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="submit"
                className="w-full btn-primary py-3"
              >
                {isLogin ? 'Login' : 'Register'}
              </button>
            </form>

            <div className="mt-6 text-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsLogin(!isLogin)}
                className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green transition-colors"
              >
                {isLogin ? "Don't have an account? Register" : "Already have an account? Login"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default Admin;
