FILE: frontend\src\pages\AdminDashboard.jsx
====================================================================================================

// frontend/src/pages/AdminDashboard.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import { 
  newsAPI, matchesAPI, teamsAPI, playersAPI, 
  usersAPI, bulkImportAPI, competitionManagementAPI
} from '../api';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { 
  FaNewspaper, FaFutbol, FaUsers, FaChartLine, 
  FaPlus, FaEdit, FaTrash, FaSignOutAlt, FaUpload, 
  FaDatabase, FaUserPlus, FaTrophy, FaHandshake,
  FaCalendarAlt, FaArrowRight
} from 'react-icons/fa';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const AdminDashboard = () => {
  const { logout } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('overview');
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ news: 0, matches: 0, teams: 0, players: 0 });
  const [news, setNews] = useState([]);
  const [matches, setMatches] = useState([]);
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState([]);
  const [players, setPlayers] = useState([]);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingItem, setEditingItem] = useState(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({});

  // Competition management state
  const [competitions, setCompetitions] = useState([]);
  const [friendlies, setFriendlies] = useState([]);
  const [compLoading, setCompLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAllData();
  }, []);

  const fetchAllData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [newsRes, matchesRes, teamsRes, playersRes] = await Promise.all([
        newsAPI.getAll(),
        matchesAPI.getAll({}),
        teamsAPI.getAll(),
        playersAPI.getAll({}),
      ]);
      setNews(newsRes.data);
      setMatches(matchesRes.data);
      setTeams(teamsRes.data);
      setPlayers(playersRes.data);
      setStats({
        news: newsRes.data.length,
        matches: matchesRes.data.length,
        teams: teamsRes.data.length,
        players: playersRes.data.length,
      });

      try {
        const usersRes = await usersAPI.getAll();
        setUsers(usersRes.data);
      } catch (userErr) {
        console.warn('Could not fetch users:', userErr);
        setUsers([]);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error fetching data:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Fetch competition data when tab is 'competitions' or 'friendlies'
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'competitions') {
      fetchCompetitions();
    } else if (activeTab === 'friendlies') {
      fetchFriendlies();
    }
  }, [activeTab]);

  const fetchCompetitions = async () => {
    setCompLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await competitionManagementAPI.getCompetitions({ active_only: false });
      setCompetitions(res.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching competitions:', error);
    } finally {
      setCompLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchFriendlies = async () => {
    setCompLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await competitionManagementAPI.getFriendlies?.() || { data: [] };
      setFriendlies(res.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching friendlies:', error);
    } finally {
      setCompLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (type, id) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this item?')) return;
    try {
      if (type === 'news') await newsAPI.delete(id);
      else if (type === 'match') await matchesAPI.delete(id);
      else if (type === 'team') await teamsAPI.delete(id);
      else if (type === 'player') await playersAPI.delete(id);
      else if (type === 'user') await usersAPI.delete(id);
      fetchAllData();
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    try {
      if (activeTab === 'news') {
        if (editingItem) await newsAPI.update(editingItem.id, formData);
        else await newsAPI.create(formData);
      } 
      else if (activeTab === 'matches') {
        if (editingItem) await matchesAPI.update(editingItem.id, formData);
        else await matchesAPI.create(formData);
      } 
      else if (activeTab === 'teams') {
        if (editingItem) await teamsAPI.update(editingItem.id, formData);
        else await teamsAPI.create(formData);
      } 
      else if (activeTab === 'players') {
        if (editingItem) await playersAPI.update(editingItem.id, formData);
        else await playersAPI.create(formData);
      }
      else if (activeTab === 'users') {
        if (!editingItem) {
          await usersAPI.create(formData);
        } else {
          alert('Editing users not yet implemented');
        }
      }
      setShowForm(false);
      setEditingItem(null);
      setFormData({});
      fetchAllData();
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Save failed: ' + err.message);
    }
  };

  const handleEdit = (item) => {
    setEditingItem(item);
    setFormData(item);
    setShowForm(true);
  };

  const handleTeamImport = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    const res = await bulkImportAPI.importTeams(formData);
    alert(`Imported ${res.data.rows_imported} teams`);
    fetchAllData();
  };

  const handlePlayerImport = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    const res = await bulkImportAPI.importPlayers(formData);
    alert(`Imported ${res.data.rows_imported} players`);
    fetchAllData();
  };

  const handleMatchImport = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', file);
    const res = await bulkImportAPI.importMatches(formData);
    alert(`Imported ${res.data.rows_imported} matches`);
    fetchAllData();
  };

  const statCards = [
    { icon: FaNewspaper, label: 'News', value: stats.news, color: 'from-faz-green' },
    { icon: FaFutbol, label: 'Matches', value: stats.matches, color: 'from-faz-orange' },
    { icon: FaUsers, label: 'Teams', value: stats.teams, color: 'from-faz-red' },
    { icon: FaChartLine, label: 'Players', value: stats.players, color: 'from-faz-green' },
  ];

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
      <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin" />
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen">
      <div className="container mx-auto px-6 py-12">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text">Admin Dashboard</h1>
          <button onClick={logout} className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-red-500/20">
            <FaSignOutAlt /> Logout
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-8">
          {['overview', 'news', 'matches', 'teams', 'players', 'users', 'competitions', 'friendlies', 'bulk-import'].map(tab => (
            <button 
              key={tab} 
              onClick={() => { setActiveTab(tab); setShowForm(false); setEditingItem(null); }}
              className={`px-6 py-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                activeTab === tab ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white' : 'glass hover:bg-white/20'
              }`}
            >
              {tab === 'bulk-import' ? 'Bulk Import' : tab.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tab.slice(1)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Overview */}
        {activeTab === 'overview' && (
          <>
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6 mb-8">
              {statCards.map((stat, i) => (
                <GlassCard key={i} className="text-center">
                  <stat.icon className={`text-5xl mx-auto mb-4 bg-gradient-to-r ${stat.color} to-transparent bg-clip-text text-transparent`} />
                  <h3 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text mb-2">{stat.value}</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-300">{stat.label}</p>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Quick Actions */}
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
              <Link to="/admin/competitions">
                <GlassCard className="text-center hover:scale-105 transition hover:bg-white/10 cursor-pointer">
                  <FaTrophy className="text-5xl text-faz-orange mx-auto mb-3" />
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Manage Competitions</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Leagues, tournaments, and cups</p>
                </GlassCard>
              </Link>
              <Link to="/admin/friendlies">
                <GlassCard className="text-center hover:scale-105 transition hover:bg-white/10 cursor-pointer">
                  <FaHandshake className="text-5xl text-faz-green mx-auto mb-3" />
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Manage Friendlies</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">International & club friendlies</p>
                </GlassCard>
              </Link>
              <Link to="/admin/promotion-relegation">
                <GlassCard className="text-center hover:scale-105 transition hover:bg-white/10 cursor-pointer">
                  <FaArrowRight className="text-5xl text-faz-red mx-auto mb-3" />
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">Promotion/Relegation</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">End‑of‑season movements</p>
                </GlassCard>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </>
        )}

        {/* News Tab - unchanged */}
        {activeTab === 'news' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-end mb-6">
              <button onClick={() => { setEditingItem(null); setFormData({}); setShowForm(true); }} className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaPlus /> Add News
              </button>
            </div>
            {showForm && <NewsForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} editing={editingItem} onCancel={() => setShowForm(false)} />}
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {news.map(item => (
                <GlassCard key={item.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div><h3 className="font-bold">{item.title}</h3><p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{item.excerpt}</p></div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(item)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-green"><FaEdit /></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('news', item.id)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-red"><FaTrash /></button>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Matches Tab - unchanged */}
        {activeTab === 'matches' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-end mb-6">
              <button onClick={() => { setEditingItem(null); setFormData({}); setShowForm(true); }} className="btn-primary"><FaPlus /> Add Match</button>
            </div>
            {showForm && <MatchForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} editing={editingItem} onCancel={() => setShowForm(false)} />}
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {matches.map(m => (
                <GlassCard key={m.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div><p className="font-semibold">{m.home_team} vs {m.away_team}</p><p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{m.venue} - {new Date(m.match_date).toLocaleDateString()}</p></div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(m)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-green"><FaEdit /></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('match', m.id)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-red"><FaTrash /></button>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Teams Tab - unchanged */}
        {activeTab === 'teams' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-end mb-6">
              <button onClick={() => { setEditingItem(null); setFormData({}); setShowForm(true); }} className="btn-primary"><FaPlus /> Add Team</button>
            </div>
            {showForm && <TeamForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} editing={editingItem} onCancel={() => setShowForm(false)} />}
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              {teams.map(t => (
                <GlassCard key={t.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div><h3 className="font-bold">{t.name}</h3><p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Coach: {t.coach}</p></div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-green"><FaEdit /></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('team', t.id)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-red"><FaTrash /></button>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Players Tab - unchanged */}
        {activeTab === 'players' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-end mb-6">
              <button onClick={() => { setEditingItem(null); setFormData({}); setShowForm(true); }} className="btn-primary"><FaPlus /> Add Player</button>
            </div>
            {showForm && <PlayerForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} editing={editingItem} teams={teams} onCancel={() => setShowForm(false)} />}
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              {players.map(p => (
                <GlassCard key={p.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div><h3 className="font-bold">{p.name}</h3><p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{p.position} #{p.jersey_number}</p></div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(p)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-green"><FaEdit /></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('player', p.id)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-red"><FaTrash /></button>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Users Tab - unchanged */}
        {activeTab === 'users' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-end mb-6">
              <button onClick={() => { setEditingItem(null); setFormData({}); setShowForm(true); }} className="btn-primary"><FaUserPlus /> Add User</button>
            </div>
            {showForm && <UserForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} formData={formData} setFormData={setFormData} editing={editingItem} teams={teams} onCancel={() => setShowForm(false)} />}
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {users.map(u => (
                <GlassCard key={u.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                  <div><p className="font-semibold">{u.full_name} (@{u.username})</p><p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{u.role} • {u.email}</p></div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('user', u.id)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-red"><FaTrash /></button>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Competitions Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'competitions' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold gradient-text">Competitions</h2>
              <Link to="/admin/competitions" className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaTrophy /> Full Management
              </Link>
            </div>
            {compLoading ? (
              <div className="text-center py-8"><div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" /></div>
            ) : competitions.length === 0 ? (
              <GlassCard className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">No competitions found.</GlassCard>
            ) : (
              <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                {competitions.slice(0, 6).map(comp => (
                  <Link key={comp.id} to={`/admin/competitions/${comp.id}`}>
                    <GlassCard className="hover:scale-105 transition p-4">
                      <h3 className="font-bold">{comp.name}</h3>
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{comp.competition_type} • {comp.gender}</p>
                      <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full ${comp.is_active ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400' : 'bg-gray-500/20 text-gray-400'}`}>
                        {comp.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                      </span>
                    </GlassCard>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Friendlies Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'friendlies' && (
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold gradient-text">Friendlies</h2>
              <Link to="/admin/friendlies" className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaHandshake /> Full Management
              </Link>
            </div>
            {compLoading ? (
              <div className="text-center py-8"><div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" /></div>
            ) : friendlies.length === 0 ? (
              <GlassCard className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">No friendlies scheduled.</GlassCard>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {friendlies.slice(0, 10).map(m => (
                  <GlassCard key={m.id} className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-semibold">{m.home_team?.name || 'TBD'} vs {m.away_team?.name || 'TBD'}</p>
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{new Date(m.match_date).toLocaleString()} • {m.venue || 'TBD'}</p>
                    </div>
                    <span className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full ${m.status === 'finished' ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-400' : 'bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-400'}`}>
                      {m.status || 'Scheduled'}
                    </span>
                  </GlassCard>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Bulk Import Tab - unchanged */}
        {activeTab === 'bulk-import' && (
          <GlassCard>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><FaDatabase /> Bulk Import Historical Data</h2>
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div><h4 className="font-semibold">Import Teams (CSV)</h4><input type="file" accept=".csv" onChange={handleTeamImport} className="glass p-2 rounded w-full" /></div>
              <div><h4 className="font-semibold">Import Players (CSV)</h4><input type="file" accept=".csv" onChange={handlePlayerImport} className="glass p-2 rounded w-full" /></div>
              <div><h4 className="font-semibold">Import Matches (JSON)</h4><input type="file" accept=".json" onChange={handleMatchImport} className="glass p-2 rounded w-full" /></div>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ---------- Form Components (unchanged) ----------
const NewsForm = ({ onSubmit, formData, setFormData, editing, onCancel }) => (
  <GlassCard className="mb-6">
    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit News' : 'Create News'}</h3>
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="text" placeholder="Title" value={formData.title || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <textarea placeholder="Excerpt" rows="2" value={formData.excerpt || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, excerpt: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <textarea placeholder="Content" rows="5" value={formData.content || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, content: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="url" placeholder="Featured Image URL" value={formData.featured_image || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, featured_image: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" />
      <div className="flex gap-3"><button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save</button><button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn-secondary">Cancel</button></div>
    </form>
  </GlassCard>
);

const MatchForm = ({ onSubmit, formData, setFormData, editing, onCancel }) => (
  <GlassCard className="mb-6">
    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Match' : 'Add Match'}</h3>
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="text" placeholder="Home Team" value={formData.home_team || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, home_team: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Away Team" value={formData.away_team || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, away_team: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="datetime-local" value={formData.match_date ? new Date(formData.match_date).toISOString().slice(0,16) : ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, match_date: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Venue" value={formData.venue || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, venue: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Competition" value={formData.competition || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, competition: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" />
      <select value={formData.status || 'upcoming'} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, status: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded">
        <option value="upcoming">Upcoming</option><option value="live">Live</option><option value="finished">Finished</option>
      </select>
      <div className="flex gap-3"><button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save</button><button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn-secondary">Cancel</button></div>
    </form>
  </GlassCard>
);

const TeamForm = ({ onSubmit, formData, setFormData, editing, onCancel }) => (
  <GlassCard className="mb-6">
    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Team' : 'Add Team'}</h3>
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="text" placeholder="Name" value={formData.name || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, name: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Short Name" value={formData.short_name || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, short_name: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="number" placeholder="Founded" value={formData.founded || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, founded: parseInt(e.target.value)})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Stadium" value={formData.stadium || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, stadium: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Coach" value={formData.coach || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, coach: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <textarea placeholder="Description" value={formData.description || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, description: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" />
      <div className="flex gap-3"><button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save</button><button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn-secondary">Cancel</button></div>
    </form>
  </GlassCard>
);

const PlayerForm = ({ onSubmit, formData, setFormData, editing, teams, onCancel }) => (
  <GlassCard className="mb-6">
    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Player' : 'Add Player'}</h3>
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="text" placeholder="Name" value={formData.name || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, name: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <select value={formData.position || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, position: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required>
        <option value="">Select Position</option><option>Goalkeeper</option><option>Defender</option><option>Midfielder</option><option>Forward</option>
      </select>
      <input type="number" placeholder="Jersey Number" value={formData.jersey_number || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, jersey_number: parseInt(e.target.value)})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="date" placeholder="Date of Birth" value={formData.date_of_birth || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, date_of_birth: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Nationality" value={formData.nationality || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, nationality: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <select value={formData.team_id || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, team_id: parseInt(e.target.value)})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required>
        <option value="">Select Team</option>{teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
      </select>
      <input type="url" placeholder="Photo URL" value={formData.photo || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, photo: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" />
      <div className="flex gap-3"><button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save</button><button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn-secondary">Cancel</button></div>
    </form>
  </GlassCard>
);

const UserForm = ({ onSubmit, formData, setFormData, editing, teams, onCancel }) => (
  <GlassCard className="mb-6">
    <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit User' : 'Add User'}</h3>
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <input type="text" placeholder="Username" value={formData.username || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, username: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="email" placeholder="Email" value={formData.email || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      <input type="text" placeholder="Full Name" value={formData.full_name || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, full_name: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />
      {!editing && <input type="password" placeholder="Password" value={formData.password || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, password: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" required />}
      <select value={formData.role || 'viewer'} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, role: e.target.value})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded">
        <option value="viewer">Viewer</option><option value="match_commissioner">Match Commissioner</option>
        <option value="media_officer">Media Officer</option><option value="club_rep">Club Rep</option>
        <option value="referee">Referee</option><option value="admin">Admin</option>
      </select>
      <select value={formData.club_id || ''} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, club_id: e.target.value ? parseInt(e.target.value) : null})} className="glass w-full p-3 rounded">
        <option value="">No club</option>{teams.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
      </select>
      <div className="flex gap-3"><button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save</button><button type="button" onClick={onCancel} className="btn-secondary">Cancel</button></div>
    </form>
  </GlassCard>
);

export default AdminDashboard;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\Cart.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { shopAPI } from '../api';
import { useCart } from '../context/CartContext';
import { FaTrash, FaArrowLeft, FaCreditCard, FaSpinner, FaShoppingCart } from 'react-icons/fa';

// Import provider logos
import airtelLogo from './assets/momo/logos/momo_airtel.png';
import mtnLogo from './assets/momo/logos/momo_mtn.png';
import zamtelLogo from './assets/momo/logos/momo_zamtel.jpg';

const providerLogos = {
  'MTN_MOMO_ZMB': mtnLogo,
  'AIRTEL_OAPI_ZMB': airtelLogo,
  'ZAMTEL_ZMB': zamtelLogo,
};

const providerNames = {
  'MTN_MOMO_ZMB': 'MTN Mobile Money',
  'AIRTEL_OAPI_ZMB': 'Airtel Money',
  'ZAMTEL_ZMB': 'Zamtel Kwacha',
};

const sanitizeErrorMessage = (message) => {
  if (!message) return '';
  let sanitized = message;
  for (const [code, name] of Object.entries(providerNames)) {
    sanitized = sanitized.replace(new RegExp(code, 'g'), name);
    const shortCode = code.replace('_ZMB', '').replace('_OAPI_ZMB', '');
    if (shortCode !== code) {
      sanitized = sanitized.replace(new RegExp(shortCode, 'g'), name);
    }
  }
  return sanitized;
};

const Cart = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { cartItems, cartSubtotal, loading, updateQuantity, removeItem, refreshCart } = useCart();
  const [checkoutData, setCheckoutData] = useState({
    customer_name: '',
    customer_email: '',
    customer_phone: '',
    shipping_address: ''
  });
  const [showCheckout, setShowCheckout] = useState(false);
  const [providers, setProviders] = useState([]);
  const [selectedProvider, setSelectedProvider] = useState('');
  const [phoneNumber, setPhoneNumber] = useState('');
  const [processingPayment, setProcessingPayment] = useState(false);
  const [pollingActive, setPollingActive] = useState(false);
  const [paymentStatus, setPaymentStatus] = useState(null);
  const [depositId, setDepositId] = useState(null);
  const [orderId, setOrderId] = useState(null);
  const [cart, setCart] = useState({ items: [], subtotal: 0, total: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCartData();
    fetchProviders();
    const user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('user') || '{}');
    if (user.name) {
      setCheckoutData(prev => ({ ...prev, customer_name: user.name, customer_email: user.email }));
    }
  }, []);

  const fetchCartData = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await shopAPI.getCart();
      setCart(res.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching cart:', error);
    }
  };

  const fetchProviders = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await shopAPI.getPaymentProviders();
      setProviders(res.data || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching providers:', error);
    }
  };

  // Cleanup polling on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (window.pollingInterval) clearInterval(window.pollingInterval);
    };
  }, []);

  const handlePhoneChange = (e) => {
    let input = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
    if (input.length > 10) input = input.slice(0, 10);
    if (input.length > 0 && input[0] !== '0') return;
    setPhoneNumber(input);
  };

  const startPolling = (depId, ordId) => {
    setDepositId(depId);
    setOrderId(ordId);
    setPaymentStatus('WAITING');
    setPollingActive(true);
    
    window.pollingInterval = setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await shopAPI.checkPaymentStatus(depId);
        const data = res.data;
        
        if (data.status === 'COMPLETED') {
          clearInterval(window.pollingInterval);
          setPaymentStatus('COMPLETED');
          setPollingActive(false);
          alert('Payment successful! Your order has been confirmed.');
          navigate(`/shop/confirmation/${ordId}`);
        } else if (data.status === 'FAILED') {
          clearInterval(window.pollingInterval);
          setPaymentStatus('FAILED');
          setPollingActive(false);
          const message = data.failureReason?.failureMessage || 'Payment failed. Please try again.';
          alert(sanitizeErrorMessage(message));
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Polling error:', err);
      }
    }, 5000);
    
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (window.pollingInterval) {
        clearInterval(window.pollingInterval);
        if (paymentStatus === 'WAITING') {
          setPaymentStatus('TIMEOUT');
          setPollingActive(false);
        }
      }
    }, 120000);
  };

  const handleCheckout = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setProcessingPayment(true);
    
    const isValidPhone = /^0\d{9}$/.test(phoneNumber);
    if (!selectedProvider) {
      alert('Please select a mobile money provider');
      setProcessingPayment(false);
      return;
    }
    if (!isValidPhone) {
      alert('Please enter a valid 10-digit phone number starting with 0 (e.g., 0977123456)');
      setProcessingPayment(false);
      return;
    }
    
    // Send raw phone number (with leading 0) - let backend format it
    const rawPhoneNumber = phoneNumber;
    
    try {
      const orderRes = await shopAPI.checkout(checkoutData);
      const newOrderId = orderRes.data.order_id;
      
      const paymentRes = await shopAPI.initiatePayment({
        order_id: newOrderId,
        provider: selectedProvider,
        phone_number: rawPhoneNumber
      });
      
      if (paymentRes.data.success) {
        alert('Payment request sent. Please check your phone to approve.');
        startPolling(paymentRes.data.depositId, newOrderId);
      } else {
        const reason = paymentRes.data.failureReason;
        if (reason) {
          const code = reason.failureCode;
          const msg = reason.failureMessage;
          if (code === 'INVALID_PHONE_NUMBER') {
            alert('Invalid phone number. Please check and try again.');
          } else if (code === 'AMOUNT_OUT_OF_BOUNDS') {
            alert('Amount is outside allowed limits.');
          } else if (code === 'PROVIDER_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE') {
            alert('Provider is temporarily unavailable. Try again later.');
          } else {
            alert(sanitizeErrorMessage(msg || 'Payment failed. Please try again.'));
          }
        } else {
          alert('Payment failed. Please try again.');
        }
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Checkout error:', error);
      alert('Checkout failed. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setProcessingPayment(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (cart.items?.length === 0 && !showCheckout) {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
          <GlassCard className="text-center py-12">
            <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🛒</div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Your cart is empty</h2>
            <p className="text-gray-400 mb-6">Looks like you haven't added any items to your cart yet.</p>
            <Link to="/shop" className="btn-primary inline-block">Continue Shopping</Link>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
        <Footer />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (paymentStatus === 'WAITING') {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
          <GlassCard className="max-w-md mx-auto text-center py-12">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-orange border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-4" />
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold gradient-text mb-4">Waiting for Payment</h2>
            <p className="text-gray-300 mb-2">
              Please check your mobile phone and enter your Mobile Money PIN to approve the payment.
            </p>
            <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Waiting for confirmation...</p>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
        <Footer />
      </>
    );
  }

  if (showCheckout) {
    return (
      <>
        <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <button onClick={() => setShowCheckout(false)} className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green inline-flex items-center gap-2">
              <FaArrowLeft /> Back to Cart
            </button>
            <Link to="/cart" className="relative">
              <div className="glass p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-white/10 transition">
                <FaShoppingCart />
                {cart.items?.length > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-faz-red text-white text-xs rounded-full w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center">
                    {cart.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0)}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </Link>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text mb-6">Checkout</h1>
          
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
            <GlassCard>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Order Summary</h2>
              <div className="space-y-3 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto mb-4">
                {cart.items?.map(item => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-white/10 pb-2">
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-semibold">{item.product_name}</p>
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Qty: {item.quantity} × ZMW {item.unit_price}</p>
                    </div>
                    <p className="font-bold">ZMW {item.subtotal}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div className="border-t border-white/20 pt-3">
                <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-lg">
                  <span>Total:</span>
                  <span className="gradient-text">ZMW {cart.total}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
            
            <GlassCard>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">Payment Details</h2>
              <form onSubmit={handleCheckout} className="space-y-4">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Full Name"
                  value={checkoutData.customer_name}
                  onChange={(e) => setCheckoutData({...checkoutData, customer_name: e.target.value})}
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded"
                  required
                />
                <input
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="Email Address"
                  value={checkoutData.customer_email}
                  onChange={(e) => setCheckoutData({...checkoutData, customer_email: e.target.value})}
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded"
                  required
                />
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  placeholder="Phone Number (e.g., 0977123456)"
                  value={checkoutData.customer_phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setCheckoutData({...checkoutData, customer_phone: e.target.value})}
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded"
                  required
                />
                <textarea
                  placeholder="Shipping Address"
                  rows="3"
                  value={checkoutData.shipping_address}
                  onChange={(e) => setCheckoutData({...checkoutData, shipping_address: e.target.value})}
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded"
                  required
                />
                
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-2">Select Mobile Money Provider</label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    {providers.map(prov => (
                      <button
                        key={prov.provider}
                        type="button"
                        disabled={!prov.deposit_operational}
                        onClick={() => setSelectedProvider(prov.provider)}
                        className={`p-3 border-2 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center gap-2 transition ${
                          !prov.deposit_operational
                            ? 'border-gray-600 bg-gray-800/50 cursor-not-allowed opacity-50'
                            : selectedProvider === prov.provider
                              ? 'border-faz-orange bg-faz-orange/10'
                              : 'border-gray-600 hover:border-gray-500'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <img
                          src={providerLogos[prov.provider]}
                          alt={providerNames[prov.provider]}
                          className="h-8 w-auto object-contain"
                        />
                        <span className="text-xs font-medium">
                          {providerNames[prov.provider]}
                        </span>
                        {!prov.deposit_operational && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-faz-red">Unavailable</span>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-2">Mobile Money Phone Number</label>
                  <input
                    type="tel"
                    placeholder="0xxxxxxxxx (10 digits)"
                    value={phoneNumber}
                    onChange={handlePhoneChange}
                    maxLength={10}
                    className="glass w-full p-3 rounded"
                    required
                  />
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                    Enter your 10-digit mobile number starting with 0 (e.g., 0977123456)
                  </p>
                </div>
                
                <button 
                  type="submit" 
                  disabled={processingPayment || !selectedProvider || !/^0\d{9}$/.test(phoneNumber)} 
                  className="btn-primary w-full py-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {processingPayment ? (
                    <FaSpinner className="animate-spin" />
                  ) : (
                    <><FaCreditCard /> Pay ZMW {cart.total}</>
                  )}
                </button>
              </form>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-4 text-center">
                You will receive a payment prompt on your mobile phone. Please approve to complete payment.
              </p>
            </GlassCard>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Footer />
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text">Shopping Cart</h1>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <Link to="/cart" className="relative">
              <div className="glass p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-white/10 transition">
                <FaShoppingCart />
                {cart.items?.length > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-faz-red text-white text-xs rounded-full w-5 h-5 flex items-center justify-center">
                    {cart.items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0)}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
            {cart.items?.map(item => (
              <GlassCard key={item.id} className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 w-full md:w-auto">
                  <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gray-700 rounded-lg overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                    <img src={item.product_image || 'https://via.placeholder.com/80x80?text=Product'} alt={item.product_name} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold">{item.product_name}</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                      {item.size && `Size: ${item.size} | `}
                      {item.color && `Color: ${item.color}`}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-faz-orange font-bold">ZMW {item.unit_price}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, item.quantity - 1)}
                      className="glass w-8 h-8 rounded-full hover:bg-white/20"
                    >-</button>
                    <span className="w-8 text-center">{item.quantity}</span>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => updateQuantity(item.id, item.quantity + 1)}
                      className="glass w-8 h-8 rounded-full hover:bg-white/20"
                    >+</button>
                  </div>
                  <button onClick={() => removeItem(item.id)} className="text-faz-red hover:text-faz-red/70">
                    <FaTrash />
                  </button>
                  <p className="font-bold min-w-[80px] text-right">ZMW {item.subtotal}</p>
                </div>
              </GlassCard>
            ))}
          </div>
          
          <div>
            <GlassCard>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Order Summary</h2>
              <div className="space-y-2 border-b border-white/10 pb-3">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span>Subtotal</span>
                  <span>ZMW {cart.subtotal}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-green-400">
                  <span>Delivery</span>
                  <span>Free</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-lg pt-3">
                <span>Total</span>
                <span className="gradient-text">ZMW {cart.total}</span>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setShowCheckout(true)} className="btn-primary w-full mt-6 py-3">
                Proceed to Checkout
              </button>
              <Link to="/shop" className="block text-center text-faz-orange mt-4 hover:underline">
                Continue Shopping
              </Link>
            </GlassCard>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default Cart;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\ClubRepDashboard.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { teamsAPI, playersAPI, matchesAPI, usersAPI } from '../api';
import { 
  FaUsers, FaFutbol, FaChartLine, FaPlus, FaEdit, FaTrash, 
  FaCheckCircle, FaExclamationTriangle, FaUserPlus 
} from 'react-icons/fa';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';

const ClubRepDashboard = () => {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const [team, setTeam] = useState(null);
  const [players, setPlayers] = useState([]);
  const [upcomingMatches, setUpcomingMatches] = useState([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ topScorer: null, mostAssists: null });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState({ type: '', text: '' });
  const [showPlayerForm, setShowPlayerForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingPlayer, setEditingPlayer] = useState(null);
  const [playerForm, setPlayerForm] = useState({
    name: '', position: '', jersey_number: '', date_of_birth: '', nationality: '', photo: ''
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchClubData();
  }, []);

  const fetchClubData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      // Get current user with club_id
      const userRes = await usersAPI.getMe();
      const clubId = userRes.data.club_id;
      if (!clubId) {
        setMessage({ type: 'error', text: 'No club assigned to your account. Please contact admin.' });
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      // Fetch team details
      const teamRes = await teamsAPI.getById(clubId);
      setTeam(teamRes.data);
      // Fetch players of this club
      const playersRes = await playersAPI.getAll({ team_id: clubId });
      setPlayers(playersRes.data);
      // Fetch upcoming matches where team is involved (by name)
      const allMatches = await matchesAPI.getAll({ status: 'upcoming' });
      const teamMatches = allMatches.data.filter(
        m => m.home_team === teamRes.data.name || m.away_team === teamRes.data.name
      );
      setUpcomingMatches(teamMatches);
      // Calculate top scorer and assister
      const topScorer = playersRes.data.reduce((max, p) => p.goals > (max?.goals || -1) ? p : max, null);
      const mostAssists = playersRes.data.reduce((max, p) => p.assists > (max?.assists || -1) ? p : max, null);
      setStats({ topScorer, mostAssists });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: 'Failed to load club data. Please refresh.' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePlayerSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const payload = {
      ...playerForm,
      jersey_number: parseInt(playerForm.jersey_number),
      team_id: team.id,
    };
    try {
      if (editingPlayer) {
        await playersAPI.update(editingPlayer.id, payload);
        setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Player updated successfully!' });
      } else {
        await playersAPI.create(payload);
        setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Player added successfully!' });
      }
      setShowPlayerForm(false);
      setEditingPlayer(null);
      setPlayerForm({ name: '', position: '', jersey_number: '', date_of_birth: '', nationality: '', photo: '' });
      fetchClubData();
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: err.response?.data?.detail || 'Save failed.' });
    }
  };

  const handleDeletePlayer = async (playerId) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Delete this player permanently?')) return;
    try {
      await playersAPI.delete(playerId);
      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'Player deleted.' });
      fetchClubData();
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: 'Delete failed.' });
    }
  };

  const editPlayer = (player) => {
    setEditingPlayer(player);
    setPlayerForm({
      name: player.name,
      position: player.position,
      jersey_number: player.jersey_number,
      date_of_birth: player.date_of_birth,
      nationality: player.nationality,
      photo: player.photo || '',
    });
    setShowPlayerForm(true);
  };

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="pt-24 text-center">
      <div className="inline-block w-12 h-12 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      <p className="mt-4 text-gray-400">Loading club dashboard...</p>
    </div>
  );

  if (!team && !loading) return (
    <div className="pt-24 text-center text-faz-red">No club assigned. Contact your FAZ administrator.</div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
      <AnimatePresence>
        {message.text && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}
            className={`fixed top-24 right-6 z-50 glass p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 ${
              message.type === 'success' ? 'border-faz-green' : 'border-faz-red'
            }`}
          >
            {message.type === 'success' ? <FaCheckCircle className="text-faz-green" /> : <FaExclamationTriangle className="text-faz-red" />}
            <span>{message.text}</span>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-4 mb-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green to-faz-orange rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-3xl font-bold text-white shadow-lg">
            {team?.short_name?.[0] || '?'}
          </div>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text">{team?.name} – Club Dashboard</h1>
            <p className="text-gray-400">Welcome back, {user?.full_name || user?.username}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => { setEditingPlayer(null); setPlayerForm({ name: '', position: '', jersey_number: '', date_of_birth: '', nationality: '', photo: '' }); setShowPlayerForm(true); }} 
          className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
          <FaUserPlus /> Add Player
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Stats Cards */}
      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-10">
        <GlassCard className="text-center">
          <FaUsers className="text-3xl text-faz-green mx-auto mb-2" />
          <p className="text-gray-400">Players</p>
          <p className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text">{players.length}</p>
        </GlassCard>
        <GlassCard className="text-center">
          <FaFutbol className="text-3xl text-faz-orange mx-auto mb-2" />
          <p className="text-gray-400">Upcoming Matches</p>
          <p className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text">{upcomingMatches.length}</p>
        </GlassCard>
        <GlassCard className="text-center">
          <FaChartLine className="text-3xl text-faz-red mx-auto mb-2" />
          <p className="text-gray-400">Top Scorer</p>
          <p className="text-xl font-bold">{stats.topScorer?.name || 'N/A'} <span className="text-faz-green">({stats.topScorer?.goals || 0})</span></p>
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Most assists: {stats.mostAssists?.name || 'N/A'} ({stats.mostAssists?.assists || 0})</p>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>

      {/* Squad Management */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Squad</h2>
        <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">{players.length} registered players</span>
      </div>
      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {players.length === 0 ? (
          <GlassCard className="col-span-full text-center py-8 text-gray-400">
            No players added yet. Click "Add Player" to register your squad.
          </GlassCard>
        ) : (
          players.map(player => (
            <GlassCard key={player.id} className="hover:scale-[1.02] transition-transform">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-lg">{player.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{player.position} • #{player.jersey_number}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{player.nationality}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => editPlayer(player)} className="p-1 hover:text-faz-green transition">
                    <FaEdit />
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleDeletePlayer(player.id)} className="p-1 hover:text-faz-red transition">
                    <FaTrash />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between mt-3 text-sm border-t border-white/10 pt-2">
                <span className="text-faz-green">{player.goals} goals</span>
                <span className="text-faz-orange">{player.assists} assists</span>
                <span className="text-gray-400">{player.appearances} apps</span>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Upcoming Matches */}
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold mt-10 mb-4">Upcoming Fixtures</h2>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {upcomingMatches.length === 0 ? (
          <GlassCard className="text-center py-6 text-gray-400">No upcoming matches scheduled.</GlassCard>
        ) : (
          upcomingMatches.map(match => (
            <GlassCard key={match.id} className="flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
              <div>
                <p className="font-semibold">{match.home_team} vs {match.away_team}</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{new Date(match.match_date).toLocaleString()}</p>
                <p className="text-xs">{match.venue}</p>
              </div>
              <span className={`text-sm px-3 py-1 rounded-full ${
                match.status === 'live' ? 'bg-faz-red/20 text-faz-red animate-pulse' : 'bg-faz-green/20 text-faz-green'
              }`}>
                {match.status.toUpperCase()}
              </span>
            </GlassCard>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Player Form Modal */}
      {showPlayerForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <GlassCard className="w-full max-w-2xl p-6 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">{editingPlayer ? 'Edit Player' : 'Add New Player'}</h2>
            <form onSubmit={handlePlayerSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <input type="text" placeholder="Full Name" className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" value={playerForm.name} onChange={e => setPlayerForm({...playerForm, name: e.target.value})} required />
              <select className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" value={playerForm.position} onChange={e => setPlayerForm({...playerForm, position: e.target.value})} required>
                <option value="">Select Position</option>
                <option>Goalkeeper</option><option>Defender</option><option>Midfielder</option><option>Forward</option>
              </select>
              <input type="number" placeholder="Jersey Number" className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" value={playerForm.jersey_number} onChange={e => setPlayerForm({...playerForm, jersey_number: e.target.value})} required />
              <input type="date" placeholder="Date of Birth" className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" value={playerForm.date_of_birth} onChange={e => setPlayerForm({...playerForm, date_of_birth: e.target.value})} required />
              <input type="text" placeholder="Nationality" className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" value={playerForm.nationality} onChange={e => setPlayerForm({...playerForm, nationality: e.target.value})} required />
              <input type="url" placeholder="Photo URL (optional)" className="glass w-full p-3 rounded" value={playerForm.photo} onChange={e => setPlayerForm({...playerForm, photo: e.target.value})} />
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPlayerForm(false)} className="btn-secondary">Cancel</button>
                <button type="submit" className="btn-primary">Save Player</button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ClubRepDashboard;

FILE: frontend\src\pages\CompetitionDetail.jsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, useLocation, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { competitionsAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaTrophy, FaCalendarAlt, FaChartLine, FaArrowLeft, FaHistory, FaFilter } from 'react-icons/fa';

const CompetitionDetail = () => {
  const { competitionId } = useParams();
  const [competition, setCompetition] = useState(null);
  const [standings, setStandings] = useState([]);
  const [availableSeasons, setAvailableSeasons] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('standings');
  const [selectedHistorySeason, setSelectedHistorySeason] = useState('');
  const [champions, setChampions] = useState([]);
  const [currentSeason, setCurrentSeason] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchCompetitionDetail();
    fetchAvailableSeasons();
  }, [competitionId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'standings' && competition) {
      fetchCurrentStandings();
    }
  }, [competition, activeTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'history' && selectedHistorySeason) {
      fetchHistoricalStandings();
    }
  }, [selectedHistorySeason, activeTab]);

  const fetchAvailableSeasons = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await competitionsAPI.getSeasons();
      setAvailableSeasons(res.data);
      if (res.data.length > 0) {
        setSelectedHistorySeason(res.data[0]);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching seasons:', error);
    }
  };

  const fetchCompetitionDetail = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await competitionsAPI.getById(competitionId);
      setCompetition(res.data.competition);
      setChampions(res.data.seasons || []);
      setCurrentSeason(res.data.current_season || res.data.competition?.season || '');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching competition:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchCurrentStandings = async () => {
    if (!competition) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await competitionsAPI.getStandings(competitionId, currentSeason);
      setStandings(res.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching current standings:', error);
      setStandings([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchHistoricalStandings = async () => {
    if (!selectedHistorySeason) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await competitionsAPI.getStandings(competitionId, selectedHistorySeason);
      // For history tab, we store standings separately or just use the same state
      setStandings(res.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching historical standings:', error);
      setStandings([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading && !competition) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!competition) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <GlassCard className="max-w-md mx-auto">
          <p className="text-gray-400">Competition not found.</p>
          <Link to="/competitions" className="btn-primary mt-4 inline-block">Back to Competitions</Link>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <Link to="/competitions" className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2">
          <FaArrowLeft /> Back to Competitions
        </Link>

        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className="mb-8"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap">
            <div className="text-6xl">
              {competition.competition_type === 'league' ? '🏆' : 
               competition.competition_type === 'cup' ? '🏅' : '⚽'}
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold gradient-text">{competition.name}</h1>
              <p className="text-gray-300">
                {competition.gender === 'male' ? 'Men\'s' : competition.gender === 'female' ? 'Women\'s' : 'Mixed'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex gap-4 border-b border-white/10 mb-6">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('standings')}
            className={`pb-2 px-4 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all duration-300 ${
              activeTab === 'standings'
                ? 'text-faz-orange border-b-2 border-faz-orange'
                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <FaChartLine /> Standings
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('history')}
            className={`pb-2 px-4 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all duration-300 ${
              activeTab === 'history'
                ? 'text-faz-orange border-b-2 border-faz-orange'
                : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <FaHistory /> History
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Standings Tab - Current Season ONLY (no filter) */}
        {activeTab === 'standings' && (
          <div>
            <div className="mb-6">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">
                Current Season Standings {currentSeason && <span className="text-faz-orange text-sm ml-2">({currentSeason})</span>}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-1">Showing standings for the current season</p>
            </div>

            <GlassCard className="overflow-x-auto">
              {loading ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
                </div>
              ) : standings.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">
                  No standings data available for the current season.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <table className="w-full">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-white/20">
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Pos</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Team</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">P</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">W</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">D</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">L</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">GF</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">GA</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">GD</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold">Pts</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {standings.map((entry, idx) => (
                      <tr key={entry.id} className="border-b border-white/10 hover:bg-white/5">
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-bold">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            {idx === 0 && <FaTrophy className="text-yellow-500" />}
                            <span>{idx + 1}</span>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">
                          <Link to={`/teams/${entry.team_id}`} className="hover:text-faz-orange">
                            {entry.team?.name || 'Unknown Team'}
                          </Link>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.played}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.won}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.drawn}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.lost}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_for}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_against}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_for - entry.goals_against}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold text-faz-orange text-xl">
                          {entry.points}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              )}
            </GlassCard>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* History Tab - Past Champions and Historical Standings */}
        {activeTab === 'history' && (
          <div>
            {/* Season Filter - Only visible in History tab */}
            <div className="mb-6 flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-4">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Historical Standings</h2>
              {availableSeasons.length > 0 && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <FaFilter className="text-faz-orange" />
                  <select
                    value={selectedHistorySeason}
                    onChange={(e) => setSelectedHistorySeason(e.target.value)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-white bg-gray-900 border border-gray-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
                  >
                    {availableSeasons.map((season) => (
                      <option key={season} value={season}>{season} Season</option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Historical Standings Table */}
            <GlassCard className="overflow-x-auto mb-8">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 px-4 pt-4">Standings for {selectedHistorySeason}</h3>
              {loading ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8">
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
                </div>
              ) : standings.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">
                  No standings data available for {selectedHistorySeason} season.
                </div>
              ) : (
                <table className="w-full">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-white/20">
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Pos</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Team</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">P</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">W</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">D</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">L</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">GF</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">GA</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">GD</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold">Pts</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {standings.map((entry, idx) => (
                      <tr key={entry.id} className="border-b border-white/10 hover:bg-white/5">
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-bold">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            {idx === 0 && <FaTrophy className="text-yellow-500" />}
                            <span>{idx + 1}</span>
                          </div>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">
                          <Link to={`/teams/${entry.team_id}`} className="hover:text-faz-orange">
                            {entry.team?.name || 'Unknown Team'}
                          </Link>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.played}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.won}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.drawn}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.lost}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_for}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_against}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center">{entry.goals_for - entry.goals_against}</td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold text-faz-orange text-xl">
                          {entry.points}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              )}
            </GlassCard>

            {/* Past Champions Table */}
            <GlassCard>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Past Champions</h3>
              {champions.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="w-full">
                    <thead>
                      <tr className="border-b border-white/20">
                        <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Season</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Champion</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-4 text-left">Runner-up</th>
                       </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {champions.map((season) => (
                        <tr key={season.id} className="border-b border-white/10 hover:bg-white/5">
                          <td className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">{season.season}</td>
                          <td className="py-3 px-4 text-faz-green">
                            {season.champion?.name || 'Unknown'}
                            {season.champion && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 ml-2">🏆</span>}
                           </td>
                          <td className="py-3 px-4 text-gray-300">
                            {season.runner_up?.name || 'Unknown'}
                           </td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">
                  <FaTrophy className="text-5xl mx-auto mb-3 text-gray-500" />
                  <p>Historical data coming soon.</p>
                </div>
              )}
            </GlassCard>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default CompetitionDetail;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\Competitions.jsx
