====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\MediaGalleries.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { mediaAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaCamera, FaCalendarAlt, FaSearch, FaImage } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

const MediaGalleries = () => {
  const [galleries, setGalleries] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(1);
  const [filterYear, setFilterYear] = useState('');
  const [years, setYears] = useState([]);
  const itemsPerPage = 9;

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchGalleries();
    extractYears();
  }, []);

  const extractYears = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await mediaAPI.getGalleries({ limit: 1000 });
      const allGalleries = res.data;
      const uniqueYears = [...new Set(allGalleries.map(gallery => 
        gallery.event_date ? new Date(gallery.event_date).getFullYear() : new Date(gallery.created_at).getFullYear()
      ))];
      setYears(uniqueYears.sort((a, b) => b - a));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error extracting years:', error);
    }
  };

  const fetchGalleries = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      let allGalleries = [];
      
      if (searchTerm) {
        const res = await mediaAPI.searchGalleries(searchTerm);
        allGalleries = res.data;
      } else {
        const res = await mediaAPI.getGalleries({ limit: 1000 });
        allGalleries = res.data;
      }
      
      // Apply year filter
      let filteredGalleries = allGalleries;
      if (filterYear) {
        filteredGalleries = allGalleries.filter(gallery => {
          const date = gallery.event_date ? new Date(gallery.event_date) : new Date(gallery.created_at);
          return date.getFullYear() === parseInt(filterYear);
        });
      }
      
      // Pagination
      const total = filteredGalleries.length;
      setTotalPages(Math.ceil(total / itemsPerPage));
      const start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
      const paginatedGalleries = filteredGalleries.slice(start, start + itemsPerPage);
      setGalleries(paginatedGalleries);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching galleries:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentPage(1);
    fetchGalleries();
  }, [searchTerm, filterYear]);

  const handleSearch = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setCurrentPage(1);
    fetchGalleries();
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearchTerm('');
    setFilterYear('');
    setCurrentPage(1);
  };

  if (loading && galleries.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <Link to="/media" className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2">
          ← Back to Media Centre
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text mb-6">Photo Galleries</h1>
        
        {/* Search and Filters */}
        <div className="mb-8">
          <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 justify-center mb-4">
            <div className="relative">
              <FaSearch className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search galleries..."
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                className="glass pl-10 pr-4 py-2 rounded-full w-64 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
              />
            </div>
            <select
              value={filterYear}
              onChange={(e) => setFilterYear(e.target.value)}
              className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-full focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
            >
              <option value="">All Years</option>
              {years.map(year => (
                <option key={year} value={year}>{year}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <button type="submit" className="btn-primary px-6 py-2 rounded-full">
              Apply Filters
            </button>
            {(searchTerm || filterYear) && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={clearFilters}
                className="glass px-6 py-2 rounded-full hover:bg-white/20"
              >
                Clear Filters
              </button>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>
        
        {galleries.length === 0 ? (
          <GlassCard className="text-center py-12">
            <p className="text-gray-400">No photo galleries found matching your criteria.</p>
          </GlassCard>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
              {galleries.map((gallery, idx) => (
                <motion.div
                  key={gallery.id}
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: idx * 0.05 }}
                >
                  <Link to={`/media/galleries/${gallery.id}`}>
                    <GlassCard className="overflow-hidden p-0 hover:scale-105 transition duration-300 group h-full flex flex-col">
                      <div className="relative h-48">
                        <img 
                          src={gallery.cover_image || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1522778119026-d647f0596c67?w=400'} 
                          alt={gallery.title} 
                          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-110 transition duration-500"
                        />
                        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/70 to-transparent" />
                        <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3">
                          <FaCamera className="text-white text-xl" />
                        </div>
                        {gallery.images && (
                          <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 glass px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs flex items-center gap-1">
                            <FaImage size={10} /> {gallery.images.length}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="p-4 flex flex-col flex-grow">
                        <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg mb-1 line-clamp-1">{gallery.title}</h3>
                        <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm line-clamp-2 mb-2">{gallery.description}</p>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-gray-500 mt-auto">
                          <FaCalendarAlt size={10} /> 
                          {gallery.event_date 
                            ? format(new Date(gallery.event_date), 'MMMM dd, yyyy')
                            : format(new Date(gallery.created_at), 'MMMM dd, yyyy')}
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-3 text-faz-orange text-sm flex items-center gap-1 group-hover:gap-2 transition-all">
                          View {gallery.images?.length || 0} photos →
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </GlassCard>
                  </Link>
                </motion.div>
              ))}
            </div>
            
            {/* Pagination */}
            {totalPages > 1 && (
              <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mt-12">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, 1))}
                  disabled={currentPage === 1}
                  className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>
                <span className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">
                  Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, totalPages))}
                  disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                  className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default MediaGalleries;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\MediaOfficerDashboard.jsx
====================================================================================================

// frontend/src/pages/MediaOfficerDashboard.jsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import { newsAPI, matchesAPI, teamsAPI } from '../api';
import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import { 
  FaNewspaper, FaCamera, FaCalendarAlt, FaPlus, FaEdit, FaTrash, 
  FaImage, FaTimes, FaUpload, FaCheckCircle, FaExclamationTriangle 
} from 'react-icons/fa';

const MediaOfficerDashboard = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [news, setNews] = useState([]);
  const [upcomingMatches, setUpcomingMatches] = useState([]);
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState([]); // for future reference
  const [showNewsForm, setShowNewsForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingNews, setEditingNews] = useState(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    title: '', 
    excerpt: '', 
    content: '', 
    featured_image: '' 
  });
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState({ type: '', text: '' });
  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData();
  }, []);

  const fetchData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [newsRes, matchesRes, teamsRes] = await Promise.all([
        newsAPI.getAll(),
        matchesAPI.getUpcoming(),
        teamsAPI.getAll(),
      ]);
      setNews(newsRes.data);
      setUpcomingMatches(matchesRes.data);
      setTeams(teamsRes.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: 'Failed to load data. Please refresh.' });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    setMessage({ type: '', text: '' });
    try {
      if (editingNews) {
        await newsAPI.update(editingNews.id, formData);
        setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'News article updated successfully!' });
      } else {
        await newsAPI.create(formData);
        setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'News article published successfully!' });
      }
      setShowNewsForm(false);
      setEditingNews(null);
      setFormData({ title: '', excerpt: '', content: '', featured_image: '' });
      setImagePreview('');
      fetchData(); // refresh list
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: error.response?.data?.detail || 'Failed to save news article.' });
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (!window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this news article? This action cannot be undone.')) return;
    try {
      await newsAPI.delete(id);
      setMessage({ type: 'success', text: 'News article deleted successfully.' });
      fetchData();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setMessage({ type: 'error', text: 'Failed to delete news article.' });
    }
  };

  const editNews = (item) => {
    setEditingNews(item);
    setFormData({
      title: item.title,
      excerpt: item.excerpt,
      content: item.content,
      featured_image: item.featured_image || '',
    });
    setImagePreview(item.featured_image || '');
    setShowNewsForm(true);
  };

  const handleImageUrlChange = (url) => {
    setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image: url });
    setImagePreview(url);
  };

  if (loading) return (
    <div className="pt-24 text-center">
      <div className="inline-block w-12 h-12 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin"></div>
      <p className="mt-4 text-gray-400">Loading dashboard...</p>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
      {/* Success/Error message */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {message.text && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -20 }}
            className={`fixed top-24 right-6 z-50 glass p-4 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 ${
              message.type === 'success' ? 'border-faz-green' : 'border-faz-red'
            }`}
          >
            {message.type === 'success' ? <FaCheckCircle className="text-faz-green" /> : <FaExclamationTriangle className="text-faz-red" />}
            <span>{message.text}</span>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8 flex-wrap gap-4">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text">Media Officer Dashboard</h1>
        <button onClick={() => setShowNewsForm(true)} className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
          <FaPlus /> Write News
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        {/* News Management Section */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold flex items-center gap-2"><FaNewspaper /> Manage News</h2>
            <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">{news.length} articles</span>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {news.length === 0 ? (
              <GlassCard className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">
                No news articles yet. Click "Write News" to create your first article.
              </GlassCard>
            ) : (
              news.map(item => (
                <GlassCard key={item.id} className="hover:scale-[1.01] transition-transform duration-200">
                  <div className="flex gap-4">
                    {item.featured_image && (
                      <img src={item.featured_image} alt="" className="w-24 h-24 object-cover rounded-lg" />
                    )}
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{item.title}</h3>
                      <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mb-2">
                        {new Date(item.published_at).toLocaleDateString()} • {item.views} views
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-gray-300 line-clamp-2">{item.excerpt}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <button onClick={() => editNews(item)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-green transition-colors">
                        <FaEdit />
                      </button>
                      <button onClick={() => handleDelete(item.id)} className="p-2 hover:text-faz-red transition-colors">
                        <FaTrash />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Upcoming matches & Media Tips */}
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><FaCalendarAlt /> Upcoming Fixtures</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3 mb-6">
            {upcomingMatches.length === 0 ? (
              <GlassCard className="text-center py-4 text-gray-400">No upcoming matches.</GlassCard>
            ) : (
              upcomingMatches.slice(0, 5).map(match => (
                <GlassCard key={match.id} className="hover:bg-white/10">
                  <p className="font-semibold">{match.home_team} vs {match.away_team}</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{new Date(match.match_date).toLocaleString()}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs mt-1">{match.venue}</p>
                </GlassCard>
              ))
            )}
          </div>

          <GlassCard>
            <h3 className="font-semibold mb-2 flex items-center gap-2"><FaCamera /> Media Tips</h3>
            <ul className="text-sm text-gray-300 space-y-1 list-disc list-inside">
              <li>Capture high‑resolution action shots</li>
              <li>Post match summaries within 2 hours</li>
              <li>Use #FAZ hashtag on social media</li>
              <li>Coordinate with match commissioner for official stats</li>
            </ul>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* News Form Modal */}
      {showNewsForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <GlassCard className="w-full max-w-3xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto p-6">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold gradient-text">{editingNews ? 'Edit News Article' : 'Create News Article'}</h2>
              <button onClick={() => { setShowNewsForm(false); setEditingNews(null); setImagePreview(''); }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white">
                <FaTimes size={24} />
              </button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">Title *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange transition"
                  value={formData.title}
                  onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})}
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">Excerpt (short summary) *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded-lg"
                  value={formData.excerpt}
                  onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, excerpt: e.target.value})}
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">Full Content *</label>
                <textarea
                  rows="8"
                  className="glass w-full p-3 rounded-lg font-mono"
                  value={formData.content}
                  onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, content: e.target.value})}
                  required
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium mb-1">Featured Image URL</label>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input
                    type="url"
                    placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
                    className="glass flex-1 p-3 rounded-lg"
                    value={formData.featured_image}
                    onChange={e => handleImageUrlChange(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      const url = prompt('Paste image URL:');
                      if (url) handleImageUrlChange(url);
                    }}
                    className="glass p-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/20"
                  >
                    <FaUpload />
                  </button>
                </div>
                {imagePreview && (
                  <div className="mt-2 relative group">
                    <img src={imagePreview} alt="Preview" className="h-32 w-auto rounded-lg object-cover" />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleImageUrlChange('')}
                      className="absolute top-1 right-1 bg-black/60 p-1 rounded-full text-white opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"
                    >
                      <FaTimes size={12} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => { setShowNewsForm(false); setEditingNews(null); }} className="btn-secondary">
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button type="submit" disabled={submitting} className="btn-primary flex items-center gap-2">
                  {submitting ? <div className="w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin" /> : null}
                  {editingNews ? 'Update Article' : 'Publish Article'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default MediaOfficerDashboard;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\MediaVideos.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { mediaAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaYoutube, FaEye, FaCalendarAlt, FaSearch, FaFilter } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

const MediaVideos = () => {
  const [videos, setVideos] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(1);
  const [filterYear, setFilterYear] = useState('');
  const [years, setYears] = useState([]);
  const itemsPerPage = 9;

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchVideos();
    extractYears();
  }, []);

  const extractYears = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await mediaAPI.getVideos({ limit: 1000 });
      const allVideos = res.data;
      const uniqueYears = [...new Set(allVideos.map(video => 
        new Date(video.published_at).getFullYear()
      ))];
      setYears(uniqueYears.sort((a, b) => b - a));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error extracting years:', error);
    }
  };

  const fetchVideos = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      let allVideos = [];
      
      if (searchTerm) {
        const res = await mediaAPI.searchVideos(searchTerm);
        allVideos = res.data;
      } else {
        const res = await mediaAPI.getVideos({ limit: 1000 });
        allVideos = res.data;
      }
      
      // Apply year filter
      let filteredVideos = allVideos;
      if (filterYear) {
        filteredVideos = allVideos.filter(video => 
          new Date(video.published_at).getFullYear() === parseInt(filterYear)
        );
      }
      
      // Pagination
      const total = filteredVideos.length;
      setTotalPages(Math.ceil(total / itemsPerPage));
      const start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
      const paginatedVideos = filteredVideos.slice(start, start + itemsPerPage);
      setVideos(paginatedVideos);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching videos:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentPage(1);
    fetchVideos();
  }, [searchTerm, filterYear]);

  const handleSearch = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setCurrentPage(1);
    fetchVideos();
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearchTerm('');
    setFilterYear('');
    setCurrentPage(1);
  };

  if (loading && videos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <Link to="/media" className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2">
          ← Back to Media Centre
        </Link>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text mb-6">Videos</h1>
        
        {/* Search and Filters */}
        <div className="mb-8">
          <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 justify-center mb-4">
            <div className="relative">
              <FaSearch className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search videos..."
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                className="glass pl-10 pr-4 py-2 rounded-full w-64 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
              />
            </div>
            <select
              value={filterYear}
              onChange={(e) => setFilterYear(e.target.value)}
              className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-full focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
            >
              <option value="">All Years</option>
              {years.map(year => (
                <option key={year} value={year}>{year}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <button type="submit" className="btn-primary px-6 py-2 rounded-full">
              Apply Filters
            </button>
            {(searchTerm || filterYear) && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={clearFilters}
                className="glass px-6 py-2 rounded-full hover:bg-white/20"
              >
                Clear Filters
              </button>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>
        
        {videos.length === 0 ? (
          <GlassCard className="text-center py-12">
            <p className="text-gray-400">No videos found matching your criteria.</p>
          </GlassCard>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
              {videos.map((video, idx) => (
                <motion.div
                  key={video.id}
                  initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                  transition={{ delay: idx * 0.05 }}
                >
                  <GlassCard className="overflow-hidden p-0 hover:scale-105 transition duration-300 h-full flex flex-col">
                    <div className="relative aspect-video">
                      <img 
                        src={video.thumbnail_url || `https://img.youtube.com/vi/${video.youtube_id}/hqdefault.jpg`} 
                        alt={video.title} 
                        className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                      />
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 opacity-0 hover:opacity-100 transition">
                        <FaYoutube className="text-6xl text-red-500" />
                      </div>
                      {video.duration && (
                        <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 bg-black/70 text-white text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded">
                          {video.duration}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="p-4 flex flex-col flex-grow">
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg mb-1 line-clamp-2">{video.title}</h3>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-sm text-gray-400 mb-2">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><FaEye size={12} /> {video.views} views</span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><FaCalendarAlt size={12} /> {format(new Date(video.published_at), 'MMM dd, yyyy')}</span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm line-clamp-2 mb-3">{video.description}</p>
                      <a 
                        href={video.youtube_url} 
                        target="_blank" 
                        rel="noopener noreferrer" 
                        className="text-faz-orange text-sm mt-auto inline-block hover:underline"
                      >
                        Watch on YouTube →
                      </a>
                    </div>
                  </GlassCard>
                </motion.div>
              ))}
            </div>
            
            {/* Pagination */}
            {totalPages > 1 && (
              <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mt-12">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, 1))}
                  disabled={currentPage === 1}
                  className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>
                <span className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">
                  Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, totalPages))}
                  disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                  className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default MediaVideos;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\NationalTeamDetail.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { nationalTeamsAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaCalendarAlt, FaTrophy, FaUsers, FaChartLine, FaFutbol, FaFlag } from 'react-icons/fa';

const NationalTeamDetail = () => {
  const { teamId } = useParams();
  const [team, setTeam] = useState(null);
  const [fixtures, setFixtures] = useState([]);
  const [tournaments, setTournaments] = useState([]);
  const [upcomingFixtures, setUpcomingFixtures] = useState([]);
  const [pastFixtures, setPastFixtures] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('fixtures');

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTeamData();
  }, [teamId]);

  const fetchTeamData = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [teamRes, fixturesRes, tournamentsRes] = await Promise.all([
        nationalTeamsAPI.getById(teamId),
        nationalTeamsAPI.getFixtures(teamId),
        nationalTeamsAPI.getTournaments(teamId),
      ]);
      setTeam(teamRes.data);
      setFixtures(fixturesRes.data);
      setTournaments(tournamentsRes.data);
      
      // Split fixtures into upcoming and past
      const now = new Date();
      const upcoming = fixturesRes.data.filter(f => new Date(f.match_date) > now && !f.is_played);
      const past = fixturesRes.data.filter(f => new Date(f.match_date) <= now || f.is_played);
      setUpcomingFixtures(upcoming);
      setPastFixtures(past);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching team data:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const getTournamentIcon = (category) => {
    switch(category) {
      case 'fifa_world_cup': return '🏆';
      case 'afcon': return '🌍';
      case 'wafcon': return '🌍';
      case 'cosafa': return '🇿🇦';
      case 'olympics': return '🏅';
      case 'chan': return '⭐';
      default: return '⚽';
    }
  };

  const getResultBadge = (fixture) => {
    if (!fixture.is_played) {
      return <span className="glass px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm">Upcoming</span>;
    }
    if (fixture.result === 'win') {
      return <span className="bg-faz-green/20 text-faz-green px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm">Win</span>;
    }
    if (fixture.result === 'loss') {
      return <span className="bg-faz-red/20 text-faz-red px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm">Loss</span>;
    }
    return <span className="bg-yellow-500/20 text-yellow-500 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm">Draw</span>;
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className="mb-8"
        >
          <Link to="/national-teams" className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green mb-4 inline-block">
            ← Back to National Teams
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green to-faz-orange rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
              <FaFlag className="text-white text-3xl" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold gradient-text">{team?.name}</h1>
              <p className="text-gray-300">
                {team?.gender === 'male' ? 'Men\'s' : 'Women\'s'} • {team?.age_group?.toUpperCase()}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Team Info Cards */}
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-6 mb-8">
          <GlassCard>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <FaUsers className="text-2xl text-faz-orange" />
              <div>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Head Coach</p>
                <p className="font-semibold">{team?.coach || 'TBA'}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
          <GlassCard>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <FaTrophy className="text-2xl text-yellow-500" />
              <div>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Major Titles</p>
                <p className="font-semibold">{tournaments.filter(t => t.round_reached === 'Winner').length}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
          <GlassCard>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <FaChartLine className="text-2xl text-faz-green" />
              <div>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Total Matches</p>
                <p className="font-semibold">{fixtures.length}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
          <GlassCard>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <FaFutbol className="text-2xl text-faz-orange" />
              <div>
                <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">Win Rate</p>
                <p className="font-semibold">
                  {fixtures.length > 0 
                    ? Math.round((fixtures.filter(f => f.result === 'win').length / fixtures.filter(f => f.is_played).length) * 100) 
                    : 0}%
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </GlassCard>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex gap-4 border-b border-white/10 mb-6 overflow-x-auto">
          {['fixtures', 'tournaments', 'squad'].map((tab) => (
            <button
              key={tab}
              onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab)}
              className={`pb-2 px-4 capitalize transition-all duration-300 ${
                activeTab === tab
                  ? 'text-faz-orange border-b-2 border-faz-orange'
                  : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {tab === 'fixtures' ? 'Fixtures & Results' : tab === 'tournaments' ? 'Tournament History' : 'Squad'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Fixtures Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'fixtures' && (
          <div className="space-y-8">
            {/* Upcoming Fixtures */}
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaCalendarAlt className="text-faz-orange" /> Upcoming Fixtures
              </h2>
              {upcomingFixtures.length === 0 ? (
                <GlassCard className="text-center py-6 text-gray-400">
                  No upcoming fixtures scheduled.
                </GlassCard>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {upcomingFixtures.map((fixture) => (
                    <GlassCard key={fixture.id}>
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-4">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{new Date(fixture.match_date).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                          <p className="font-semibold text-lg">
                            {fixture.home_away === 'home' ? '🏠 vs' : '✈️ at'} {fixture.opponent}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{fixture.venue}</p>
                          <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
                            <span className="glass px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs">{fixture.competition}</span>
                            {fixture.is_qualifier && (
                              <span className="glass px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs text-faz-orange">Qualifier</span>
                            )}
                            <span className="glass px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs">{fixture.match_type}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-center">
                          {getResultBadge(fixture)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </GlassCard>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Past Fixtures */}
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaTrophy className="text-faz-green" /> Recent Results
              </h2>
              {pastFixtures.length === 0 ? (
                <GlassCard className="text-center py-6 text-gray-400">
                  No past matches recorded.
                </GlassCard>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {pastFixtures.slice(0, 10).map((fixture) => (
                    <GlassCard key={fixture.id}>
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-4">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{new Date(fixture.match_date).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                          <p className="font-semibold">
                            {fixture.home_away === 'home' ? '🏠 vs' : '✈️ at'} {fixture.opponent}
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">{fixture.venue}</p>
                          <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
                            <span className="glass px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs">{fixture.competition}</span>
                            {fixture.is_qualifier && (
                              <span className="glass px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs text-faz-orange">Qualifier</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="text-center">
                          <p className="text-2xl font-bold">
                            {fixture.goals_for} - {fixture.goals_against}
                          </p>
                          {getResultBadge(fixture)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </GlassCard>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tournaments Tab */}
        {activeTab === 'tournaments' && (
          <div>
            {/* Major Tournaments */}
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold mb-4">Major Tournament History</h2>
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
              {tournaments.filter(t => t.tournament_name !== 'International Friendly').map((tournament) => (
                <GlassCard key={tournament.id}>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <div className="text-3xl">{getTournamentIcon(tournament.category)}</div>
                    {tournament.round_reached === 'Winner' && (
                      <FaTrophy className="text-yellow-500 text-xl" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">{tournament.tournament_name}</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400">{tournament.year}</p>
                  <div className="mt-3">
                    <span className={`glass px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm ${
                      tournament.round_reached === 'Winner' ? 'text-yellow-500' :
                      tournament.round_reached === 'Did Not Qualify' ? 'text-faz-red' :
                      'text-faz-green'
                    }`}>
                      {tournament.round_reached || 'Participated'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Tournament Info by Category */}
            <GlassCard>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4">Tournament Information</h3>
              <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-sm">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-semibold text-faz-orange mb-2">Men's Tournaments</h4>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-gray-300">
                    <li>• <strong>AFCON</strong> – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>FIFA World Cup</strong> – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>CHAN</strong> (U-23) – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>COSAFA Cup</strong> – Regional tournament (no qualifiers)</li>
                    <li>• <strong>Four Nations</strong> – Invitational friendly tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>Olympics</strong> (Senior) – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                  </ul>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-semibold text-faz-orange mb-2">Women's Tournaments</h4>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-gray-300">
                    <li>• <strong>WAFCON</strong> – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>FIFA Women's World Cup</strong> – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>COSAFA Women's Cup</strong> – Regional tournament</li>
                    <li>• <strong>Olympics</strong> – Qualifiers → Main Tournament</li>
                  </ul>
                </div>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Squad Tab - Placeholder */}
        {activeTab === 'squad' && (
          <GlassCard className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400">
            <FaUsers className="text-5xl mx-auto mb-4 text-gray-500" />
            <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold mb-2">Squad Information Coming Soon</h3>
            <p>Player profiles and squad list will be available in the next update.</p>
          </GlassCard>
        )}
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default NationalTeamDetail;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\NationalTeams.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { nationalTeamsAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaFlag, FaFutbol, FaCalendarAlt, FaTrophy } from 'react-icons/fa';

const NationalTeams = () => {
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedGender, setSelectedGender] = useState('all');

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNationalTeams();
  }, [selectedGender]);

  const fetchNationalTeams = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const params = selectedGender !== 'all' ? { gender: selectedGender } : {};
      const res = await nationalTeamsAPI.getAll(params);
      setTeams(res.data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching national teams:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const ageGroups = {
    senior: 'Senior Team',
    u23: 'U-23 (Olympic)',
    u20: 'U-20',
    u17: 'U-17',
    u15: 'U-15',
  };

  const genderFilters = [
    { value: 'all', label: 'All Teams' },
    { value: 'male', label: 'Men\'s Teams' },
    { value: 'female', label: 'Women\'s Teams' },
  ];

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className="text-center mb-8"
        >
          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold gradient-text mb-4">National Teams</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-300">Follow Zambia's national teams across all age groups</p>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Gender Filters */}
        <div className="flex justify-center gap-4 mb-8">
          {genderFilters.map((filter) => (
            <button
              key={filter.value}
              onClick={() => setSelectedGender(filter.value)}
              className={`px-6 py-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                selectedGender === filter.value
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-faz-green to-faz-orange text-white'
                  : 'glass hover:bg-white/20'
              }`}
            >
              {filter.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="text-center py-12">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {teams.map((team) => (
              <Link key={team.id} to={`/national-teams/${team.id}`}>
                <GlassCard className="hover:scale-105 transition-all duration-300 group">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-br from-faz-green to-faz-orange rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
                      <FaFlag className="text-white" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold group-hover:text-faz-orange transition-colors">
                        {team.name}
                      </h2>
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 capitalize">
                        {team.gender} • {ageGroups[team.age_group] || team.age_group}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {team.coach && (
                    <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm">Coach: {team.coach}</p>
                  )}
                  <div className="mt-3 text-faz-orange text-sm flex items-center gap-1 group-hover:gap-2 transition-all">
                    View Fixtures & Results → 
                  </div>
                </GlassCard>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default NationalTeams;


====================================================================================================
FILE: frontend\src\pages\News.jsx
====================================================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import NewsCard from '../components/NewsCard';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import { newsAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaSearch, FaFilter, FaCalendarAlt } from 'react-icons/fa';

const News = () => {
  const [news, setNews] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(1);
  const [filterMonth, setFilterMonth] = useState('');
  const [filterYear, setFilterYear] = useState('');
  const [months, setMonths] = useState([]);
  const [years, setYears] = useState([]);
  const itemsPerPage = 9;

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchNews();
    // Extract available months and years from news data
    const extractDates = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await newsAPI.getAll({ limit: 1000 });
        const allNews = response.data;
        const uniqueMonths = [...new Set(allNews.map(item => 
          new Date(item.published_at).toLocaleString('default', { month: 'long' })
        ))];
        const uniqueYears = [...new Set(allNews.map(item => 
          new Date(item.published_at).getFullYear()
        ))];
        setMonths(uniqueMonths);
        setYears(uniqueYears.sort((a, b) => b - a));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error extracting dates:', error);
      }
    };
    extractDates();
  }, []);

  const fetchNews = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      let response;
      if (searchTerm) {
        response = await newsAPI.search(searchTerm);
      } else if (filterMonth || filterYear) {
        response = await newsAPI.filterByDate(filterMonth, filterYear);
      } else {
        response = await newsAPI.getAll();
      }
      
      let filteredNews = response.data;
      
      // Apply date filtering if not already done by API
      if (!(filterMonth || filterYear) && (filterMonth || filterYear)) {
        filteredNews = filteredNews.filter(item => {
          const date = new Date(item.published_at);
          const monthMatch = filterMonth ? date.toLocaleString('default', { month: 'long' }) === filterMonth : true;
          const yearMatch = filterYear ? date.getFullYear() === parseInt(filterYear) : true;
          return monthMatch && yearMatch;
        });
      }
      
      // Pagination
      const total = filteredNews.length;
      setTotalPages(Math.ceil(total / itemsPerPage));
      const start = (currentPage - 1) * itemsPerPage;
      const paginatedNews = filteredNews.slice(start, start + itemsPerPage);
      setNews(paginatedNews);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching news:', error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentPage(1);
    fetchNews();
  }, [searchTerm, filterMonth, filterYear]);

  const handleSearch = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setCurrentPage(1);
    fetchNews();
  };

  const clearFilters = () => {
    setSearchTerm('');
    setFilterMonth('');
    setFilterYear('');
    setCurrentPage(1);
  };

  if (loading && news.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-4" />
          <p className="text-gray-300">Loading news...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen">
      <div className="container mx-auto px-6 py-12">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className="text-center mb-12"
        >
          <h1 className="text-5xl md:text-6xl font-bold gradient-text mb-4">
            Latest News
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xl text-gray-300">
            All the latest updates from the Football Association of Zambia
          </p>
        </motion.div>

        {/* Search and Filters */}
        <div className="mb-8">
          <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 justify-center mb-4">
            <div className="relative">
              <FaSearch className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" />
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search news..."
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                className="glass pl-10 pr-4 py-2 rounded-full w-64 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
              />
            </div>
            <select
              value={filterMonth}
              onChange={(e) => setFilterMonth(e.target.value)}
              className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-full focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
            >
              <option value="">All Months</option>
              {months.map(month => (
                <option key={month} value={month}>{month}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <select
              value={filterYear}
              onChange={(e) => setFilterYear(e.target.value)}
              className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-full focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-faz-orange"
            >
              <option value="">All Years</option>
              {years.map(year => (
                <option key={year} value={year}>{year}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <button type="submit" className="btn-primary px-6 py-2 rounded-full">
              Apply Filters
            </button>
            {(searchTerm || filterMonth || filterYear) && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={clearFilters}
                className="glass px-6 py-2 rounded-full hover:bg-white/20"
              >
                Clear Filters
              </button>
            )}
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* News Grid */}
        {news.length === 0 ? (
          <GlassCard className="text-center py-12">
            <p className="text-gray-400">No news articles found matching your criteria.</p>
          </GlassCard>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
              {news.map((item, idx) => (
                <NewsCard key={item.id} news={item} index={idx} />
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Pagination */}
            {totalPages > 1 && (
              <div className="flex justify-center gap-2 mt-12">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, 1))}
                  disabled={currentPage === 1}
                  className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>
                <span className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">
                  Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, totalPages))}
                  disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                  className="glass px-4 py-2 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-white/20 transition"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default News;

FILE: frontend\src\pages\NewsDetail.jsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';
import { newsAPI } from '../api';
import { motion } from 'framer-motion';
import { FaCalendar, FaEye, FaArrowLeft, FaShare } from 'react-icons/fa';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

const NewsDetail = () => {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const [news, setNews] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchNews = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await newsAPI.getById(id);
        setNews(response.data);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Error fetching news:', error);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };
    fetchNews();
  }, [id]);

  const handleShare = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
    alert('Link copied to clipboard!');
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 border-4 border-faz-green border-t-faz-orange rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto mb-4" />
          <p className="text-gray-300">Loading article...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!news) {
    return (
      <div className="pt-24 text-center">
        <GlassCard className="max-w-md mx-auto">
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-faz-red">Article Not Found</h2>
          <Link to="/news" className="btn-primary mt-4 inline-block">Back to News</Link>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen">
        <div className="container mx-auto px-6 py-12 max-w-4xl">
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          >
            <Link to="/news" className="text-faz-orange hover:text-faz-green mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2">
              <FaArrowLeft /> Back to News
            </Link>
            
            <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-bold gradient-text mb-4">{news.title}</h1>
            
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-gray-400 text-sm mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-white/10">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaCalendar className="text-faz-green" />
                <span>{format(new Date(news.published_at), 'MMMM dd, yyyy')}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <FaEye className="text-faz-orange" />
                <span>{news.views} views</span>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleShare}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-faz-green transition ml-auto"
              >
                <FaShare /> Share
              </button>
            </div>
            
            {news.featured_image && (
              <div className="mb-8 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
                <img
                  src={news.featured_image}
                  alt={news.title}
                  className="w-full h-auto object-cover max-h-96"
                />
              </div>
            )}
            
            <GlassCard className="p-6 md:p-8">
              <div className="prose prose-invert prose-lg max-w-none">
                <p className="text-xl text-gray-300 font-medium mb-4">{news.excerpt}</p>
                <div className="text-gray-300 space-y-4 leading-relaxed">
                  {news.content.split('\n').map((paragraph, idx) => (
                    <p key={idx}>{paragraph}</p>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </GlassCard>
          </motion.div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default NewsDetail;

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import GlassCard from '../components/GlassCard';
import Footer from '../components/Footer';

const PageDetail = () => {
  const location = useLocation();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  useEffect(() => {
    const path = location.pathname;
    // Format title from path
    let raw = path.split('/').pop().replace(/-/g, ' ');
    raw = raw.split('?')[0];
    const formatted = raw.split(' ').map(word => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1)).join(' ');
    setTitle(formatted || 'Page');
  }, [location]);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="pt-24 min-h-screen container mx-auto px-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold gradient-text mb-6">{title}</h1>
        <GlassCard>
          <p className="text-gray-300">Content for <strong>{title}</strong> will be added soon. This is a placeholder page.</p>
          <p className="text-gray-400 mt-4">Please check back later for updates.</p>
        </GlassCard>
      </div>
      <Footer />
    </>
  );
};

export default PageDetail;

FILE: frontend\src\pages\Players.jsx
